property of the supplement theorem(s) cited by the manuscript's proof, instantiated on the typed carrier where it exists. (i) typed future equivalence is an equivalence and a right congruence on positive-probability branches; on the finite-horizon branch (def:supp-event-grammar, FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar) Z^min is finite at every cut type; Z^min is the terminal object of the category of reachable predictive presentations of every typed operational law (unique coarsest reachable deterministic realization) and every presentation maps onto it. (ii) T_a[h] = [h a] on positive-probability branches (canonical successor maps; the persistent packet is a function of the process), plus the untyped derived-carrier theorem (path-weight factorization, unique surjective intertwining quotient of every realization). (iii) Hankel forward map existence/uniqueness on the column span and Hankel-core minimality. (iv) frame-independent Choi reconstruction, positive causal comb iff causal trace recursion, prefixes determined by the terminal comb, canonical prefix memory of dimension rank J unique up to a unique inner-product-preserving memory unitary. (v) contextual null set a two-sided *-ideal, contextual envelope universal property, normalized regular trace tracial/faithful/normalized/intrinsic, word-Gram positivity, least flat depth, exhaustion and panel reconstruction of the represented *-algebra. (vi) every presentation factors uniquely through Z^min (toMinimal_spec) and every future-operational compiler factors uniquely through the minimal predictive quotient (upstream_compiler_transfer): the formal content of 'canonical without an independently supplied ledger, hidden state, ...'. (vii) the readable relational completion is a surjective process quotient intertwining every branch word, and every word-record presentation has the minimal faithful all-future quotient (MinimalRecordExact). Disclosed: clauses (ii)-(v),(vii) are stated, as in the cited supplement theorems already accepted in this ledger, on their own finite carriers (untyped predictive systems, Hankel tables, Choi frames/comb families, finite matrix algebras, word-record machines) and universally quantified, so they apply to the carriers derived from the given process, but no single typed carrier ties them together; the unbounded-horizon hypothesis (common finite-dimensional sequential realization) is the finiteness of those matrix carriers, a hypothesis of the statement; the monoidal factorization under independent composition is prop:supp-grand-monoidal (separate, proved). All cited supplement records are proved in the ledger. Standard axioms. |

| Proved on the manuscript's compact convex chart by ProximalChart.chart_action_stationarity (Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean), with chart_action_stationarity_argmin (canonical y*_theta = argminProx theta; existence exists_isMinOn_proxΦ and uniqueness eq_of_isMinOn_proxΦ proved) and chart_action_stationarity_support (interiority for every theta in supp nu, the manuscript's literal hypothesis). Data ProximalChart = eq:main-fisher-metric + eq:main-action-hessian on Theta only: Theta compact convex in a finite-dimensional real inner product space (R^d), gradients of Psi and A on an open U containing Theta, Hessians (derivatives of the gradients) at points of Theta with m I <= G = HPsi <= M I and -lam G <= D^2 A <= L G as quadratic forms, lam, L >= 0, eta > 0, eta lam < 1 (weaker than C^2 on a neighbourhood; strict convexity of Psi follows). Kernel: the lazy kernel (1-th) I + th K^acc, 0 < th <= 1 state independent, has stationary probability law nu supported (a.e.) in Theta, K^acc Markov; nu K^acc = nu is derived (comp_eq_of_lazyKernel_comp_eq). Conclusions: int frak S dnu <= C_{Psi,A,eta} Dbar_Psi with frak S(theta) = <grad A(theta), G(theta)^{-1} grad A(theta)> (stationarityForm, inverse operator; G^{-1} <= m^{-1} I in inverse_form_bounds) and the explicit constant C = stationarityConstant = 4 Lambda_Phi^2/(m mu_Phi) + 4M^2(1+eta l)^2/(m^2 eta) of eq:supp-stationarity-constant; and Dbar_Psi = 0 forces grad A = 0 nu-almost surely (last conjunct). The proof uses interior first-order optimality grad Phi_theta(y*) = 0 (gradΦ_eq_zero), no whole-space descent lemma. Disclosed technical assumptions: the gap integrand is (nu (x) K^acc)-integrable (Dbar finite; if Dbar = infinity the inequality is trivial) and frak S is a.e.-strongly measurable (automatic when G is continuous, e.g. under the manuscript's C^2 hypothesis). Non-vacuity: exampleChart (Theta = [-1,1], Psi = A = x^2/2, eta = 1/2) with interior proximal minimizer 2 theta/3. The trailing AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact pointers (integral_stationarity_form_le etc.) are the superseded whole-space version and are not needed. |
| `thm:main-gravitational-classification` | theorem | Lorentz-natural classification of the generic gravitational score | [`RenewalGeometry.gravitational_score_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.lorentz_natural_pairing_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.lorentz_natural_operator_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairing_coefficients_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.inducedBivectorAction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsProperLorentz`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.boost4_isProperLorentz`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BivectorRotationCommutant.rotation_commutant_scalar_blocks`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/BivectorRotationCommutantExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.holst_palatini_coefficients_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniInsertion.lean) | Proved; re-verified against the new hypotheses, which now cite the polynomial local class def:main-gravitational-class (curvature part of bidegree (2,1) in (e,R), curvature-free part of degree four in e, spacetime-constant real coefficients, internal contractions only η, ε and the scalar multiplicity commutant) instead of the older 'real and Lorentz natural on the represented variables'. The finite-dimensional Lean model is exactly that class at a point: the (2,1) part is a real bilinear pairing A of the coframe bivector e∧e with the curvature R (both in Λ²ℝ^{1,3}, carrier BivectorIndex = Fin 2 × Fin 3, the spacetime two-form part fixed by the wedge) and the (4,0) part is an alternating coframe four-form ω. Lorentz naturality = invariance of A under the actual induced bivector action inducedBivectorAction Λ (2×2 minors) of every proper orthochronous Lorentz matrix (IsProperLorentz). Results: (i) lorentz_natural_operator_classification (eq:supp-lorentz-commutant): the commutant is span{I, ⋆} (only two rotations and one boost of cosh = 5/4 are used, so the hypothesis is weaker than full naturality); (ii) lorentz_natural_pairing_classification: every invariant pairing is a·(metric = Holst η-contraction) + b·(ε = Palatini ε-contraction), coefficients unique (pairing_coefficients_unique); (iii) alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det: every alternating four-form is λ·det (L_vol); (iv) gravitational_score_classification: the pair (A, ω) has a unique coefficient triple (α, β, λ), i.e. eq:main-phv-coefficients / eq:main-phv-classification. Scoped modelling disclosed: the manuscript hypotheses 'scalar residual typed multiplicity commutant' and 'represents determinant coframe volume' are the assumptions of the model (no extra internal tensor; the volume term is an alternating four-form of the coframe); the class's comparison modulo exact terms and the exclusion of torsion polynomials / curvature derivatives / inverse coframes are the model's shape (A is linear in R and quadratic in e, ω is quartic in e) rather than separately proved exclusions. No Palatini, Holst or Einstein action is assumed, and nothing is claimed for first-order connection actions outside the class, as the new final sentence states. |

| Hypotheses are now the paper's: on a finite weighted graph (masses m, symmetric conductances c, cell scale h) with efficient capacities u: mu(V) <= V*, h^2 sum_u c_uv <= D m_v (= sum_u c_uv <= D h^-2 m_v), the comparison a_- h c_e <= u_e <= a_+ h c_e (eq:main-capacity-conductance) and I^eff >= I*^eff > 0, where I^eff, I^sp are the min over half-volume cuts of u(dA)/mu(A)^{2/3} and h c(dA)/mu(A)^{2/3} (efficientCutConstant/spatialCutConstant, attained by exists_minimizing_cut). spatialScreen_positiveBranch derives a_- I^sp <= I^eff <= a_+ I^sp (eq:main-cut-comparison, cutConstant_comparison), I^sp >= I*^eff/a_+ > 0, the L^6 Sobolev bound (as \|\|f\|\|_6^6 <= (C_S E(f))^3, C_S = 128 D/(I*^eff/a_+)^2), Poincare with C_P = V*^{2/3} C_S, the Weyl count N(R) <= 1 + 4 e^{3/2} V* (C_S R)^{3/2}, and the screen tail in the stated L^2 form \|\|(I-P_R)f\|\|^2_{L^2(mu)} <= R^-1 E(f) with P_R = 1_{[0,R]}(Delta) (screenTail_L2 via the new spectral identities energy_eq_sum_eigenvalue_mul_sq and weightedL2_spectralHighPart). spatialScreen_neckBranch: for a sequence of regulators with 0 < I^eff_j -> 0, minimizing cuts give potentials phi_j = 1_{A_j}/u(dA_j) with total u-variation 1 (ordered-pair form (1/2) sum_{x,y} u_xy \|phi(x)-phi(y)\|) and \|sum_v b_{A_j}(v) phi_j(v)\| = (I^eff_j)^-1 -> infinity (eq:main-neck-obstruction). zeroCapacity_cut_of_efficientCutConstant_eq_zero covers the finite zero-capacity remark. Non-vacuity: the two-point regulator (twoPoint, efficientCutConstant_twoPoint = eps) instantiates both branches (examples in the file). Constants depend only on D, V*, I*^eff, a_+ (regulator-uniform). AUDIT 5 Oct 2026: kept (constants and cut demand checked against the tex). |
| `thm:main-curvature-propagation` | theorem | Initial-energy curvature propagation certificate | [`RenewalGeometry.curvature_energy_propagation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/CurvatureEnergyPropagationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gramEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/CurvatureEnergyPropagationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gram_inner_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/CurvatureEnergyPropagationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gramEnergySq_deriv_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/CurvatureEnergyPropagationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.regularised_energy_deriv_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/CurvatureEnergyPropagationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.curvature_energy_uniform_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/CurvatureEnergyPropagationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.derived_l2_curvature_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/CurvatureEnergyPropagationExact.lean) | Proved. First box eq:main-curvature-energy-propagation on a finite-dimensional real inner-product space with a differentiable symmetric positive Gram M(t): for the writer y' = (K+L)y + f with M-skew K (<M x, K x> = 0) and any growth function a >= 0 with <x, (M' + M L + L^T M) x> <= 2 a <x, M x> (the manuscript's a_X = (1/2) max{0, lambda_max[...]} is the least such function, so the theorem applies to it), z(t) <= exp(int_0^t a) [z(0) + int_0^t exp(-int_0^s a) \|f(s)\|_{M(s)} ds] for t >= 0 (curvature_energy_propagation): energy differential inequality (z^2)' <= 2 a z^2 + 2 z \|f\|_M via exact skew cancellation and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality of the positive form (gram_inner_le, discriminant argument), then the integrating-factor/monotonicity argument on sqrt(z^2 + eps^2) and eps -> 0 (a genuinely time-dependent Gronwall bound; Mathlib's gronwallBound is constant-coefficient). Budgets: z <= exp(A*)(Z* + F*) on [0,T*] (curvature_energy_uniform_bound). Second box eq:main-derived-l2-curvature (derived_l2_curvature_bound): with slice norms rho(t) = \|I y(t)\|_{L^2(Sigma_t)} <= C_I z(t), lapse 0 <= N <= N*, and \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= sqrt(int_0^{T*} N rho^2) + C_zeta, one gets \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= C_I sqrt(N* T*) exp(A*)(Z* + F*) + C_zeta. Scoped/disclosed: the writer hypotheses are stated for all real times (only [0,t] is used), y and M differentiable, f and a continuous; the slice decomposition of L^2(K) (\|g\|^2_{L^2(K)} = int N \|g(t)\|^2_{Sigma_t}), the triangle inequality in L^2(K) and \|zeta\|_{L^2(K)} <= C_zeta are abstracted into the single hypothesis \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= sqrt(int N rho^2) + C_zeta. AUDIT 5 Oct 2026: kept; the slice hypothesis of derived_l2_curvature_bound is the definition of \|\|.\|\|_{L^2(K)} with volume N dt dvol_Sigma plus the triangle inequality, not an assumption of the conclusion. |

PeriodicGridAubinLions.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LogBCH.PlaquetteBCH.norm_plaquetteRecord_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PlaquetteBCHExpansion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LiteralLinkLimit.ae_eq_zero_of_cylPair_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/GaugeTheory/LiteralLinkLimitDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LiteralLinkLimit.ae_eq_of_cylPair_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/GaugeTheory/LiteralLinkLimitDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LiteralLinkLimit.ae_modeCoeff_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/GaugeTheory/LiteralLinkLimitDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LiteralLinkLimit.cylPair_eq_integral_modeCoeff`](../../RenewalGeometry/GaugeTheory/LiteralLinkLimitDensity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LiteralLinkLimit.modeCoeff`](../../RenewalGeometry/GaugeTheory/LiteralLinkLimitDensity.lean) | PROVED (fC3, 1 Oct 2026; accepted with disclosure) as LiteralLinkLimit.literal_link_compactness (GaugeTheory/LiteralLinkCompactnessLimit.lean; standard axioms only; non-vacuity example with the vacuum history A = A0 = 0 on N_m = 2m+1). Setting (disclosed): odd periodic grids (Z/N_m)^3, h_m = 1/N_m -> 0; complex n x n matrix coefficients with the Frobenius norm; links U_i = exp(h A_i) with A_i continuous in t and U_i C^1 in t (entrywise derivative U'_i, needed to define E); A0 only measurable; E_i = elecRec (literal record E_i U_i = (d_t U_i + A0 U_i - U_i S_i A0)/h), F_ij = magRec (h^-2 logarithmic plaquette coefficient); E, F measurable in t. Hypotheses: chart h\|A_i\| <= delta <= 1/16 and eq:main-link-energy with one constant B (sup_t \|\|A(t)\|\|_h^2, int (\|\|A\|\|_h^2 + sum_j \|\|D_j^+ A\|\|_h^2), int \|\|A0\|\|^2, int \|\|E\|\|^2, int \|\|F\|\|^2 <= B). No Coulomb, gauge-smallness, temporal-mean, D^+A0 or d_t A0 hypothesis. Conclusion: after extraction, I_h A_i -> A_i strongly in L^2((0,T] x T^3); I_h A0, I_h E_i, I_h F_ij converge weakly in L^2 (WeakL2); the limits satisfy d_t A_i = E_i + d_i A0 + A_i A0 - A0 A_i and F_ij = d_i A_j - d_j A_i + [A_i, A_j], tested against every chi(t) e_k(x), chi in C^1_c(0,T), k in Z^3 (cylPair), i.e. in the Fourier characterization of distributions on (0,T) x T^3; E^inf and F^inf are the unique L^2 functions with these tested identities (ae_eq_of_cylPair_eq, via ae_eq_zero_of_cylPair_eq_zero). Proof: Aubin-Lions for Z = (U-I)/h (negSobNorm2_linkTime, gridAubinLions_limit) and the log-chart transfer to A (exists_strong_link_limits); weak extraction (exists_weakSeq_entries); exact tested electric identity (gp_linkTime, electric_grid_identity) and weak-strong passage (tendsto_pairing_modChar, electric_identity); plaquette decomposition with summed BCH remainder -> 0 (gp_magRec, sum_norm_magRem_le, magnetic_identity). Not formalized (disclosed, standard): density of span{chi(t) e_k(x)} in C_c^infty((0,T) x T^3) in the C^1 topology, which converts the tested identities into identities against all test functions. | `thm:main-renewal-einstein` | theorem | General renewal-to-Einstein certification | | Proved as one theorem renewal_einstein_certification whose hypothesis packet RenewalEinsteinCertification is organised by (E1)-(E5), each field with a docstring quoting its (E#) item: standing regulator structure (mt:adm: metric-compatible Lorentz connections, represented L2_loc torsion); (E1) realized first variations of the reconstructed common action tend to zero on the determining metric-test core, bounded convergent physical lift coefficient tensors; (E2) Lorentz-natural classified score (thm:main-gravitational-classification) and the physical first-variation remainder condition eq:main-first-variation-reduction on the same tests; (E3) coefficient limits, beta != 0, vanishing spinless connection Euler residual, uniform Cartan nondegeneracy; (E4) the output of the spatial-screen/compact-screen packet: nondegenerate limit, e_X -> e strongly in L2_loc, sup \|\|e_X\|\|_{L6(K)} < oo (oriented regulator coframes); (E5) the curvature-certificate output sup \|\|R_X\|\|_{L2(K)} < oo and the ALTERNATIVE (Or) of two connection routes: FullConnectionCertificate = ass:main-connection-compactness after extraction (omega_X -> omega strongly in L2_loc, first item of eq:main-identified-curvature-hypotheses) + the two Cartan identification residuals c_X(K) -> 0 (eq:main-cartan-curvature-residual) and I_X(Phi) -> 0 (eq:main-cartan-incidence) for a finite Cartan writer encoded as typed data W (the manuscript describes the writer only in words) + THE AMENDMENT spatialConnectionL3Bound (uniform L3_loc spatial connection bound, disclosed manuscript correction accepted by the coordinator, supplied by eq:main-connection-hodge-budget of thm:main-hodge-connection); or LiteralLinkCertificate = the output of thm:main-literal-link-compactness on the cylinder (bounded weakly convergent connection, strongly convergent spatial coefficients, curvature records weakly convergent to the identified full nonlinear curvature d omega + omega^omega), with spatialConnectionL3Bound (= eq:supp-literal-integrability on this route). The two residuals are combined into eq:supp-curvature-identification (toHypotheses), the packet is assembled into the handoff packets, and the theorem instantiates renewal_palatini_handoff(_literalLink) plus the corollary's core/closure argument. Conclusion (EinsteinCertificationConclusion): zero limiting torsion, chi = beta != 0, on a compact neighbourhood K' of K an identified curvature RL = d omega + omega^omega, metric compatibility and orientation of the limit, Levi-Civita uniqueness of the limit connection, convergence of every core metric first variation to chi int_K sqrt(-g)(G + Lambda g)k (eq:main-einstein-variation, Lambda = -lambda/(2 beta)), and G + Lambda g = 0 distributionally on the determining test core and, under continuity in the declared test topology, on its closure (eq:main-vacuum-einstein). The whole selected cofinal sequence converges (the paper's 'a subsequence' is the selected sequence). The statement has no matter/stress term or limiting stationarity, so ConditionalLocalWeakEinsteinMatterHandoff is not needed; (E4) is stated directly in L2_loc/L6 form, so CoframeStrongInterpolation enters only through the handoff. The (E1) scaled-bound sufficient operational route is cor:supp-renewal-einstein's renewal_vacuum_equation_almost_surely. Non-vacuity: renewalEinsteinCertification_flat (full route, with the amendment, zero writer) and renewalEinsteinCertification_flat_literalLink, with constant nonzero tests; vacuity check: no field contains or trivially implies the conclusion (the Einstein identification comes from the handoff). Disclosed renderings: the operational reconstructions behind (E1) (determining kernel, scalar common action), (E4) (thm:main-spatial-screen, liminf I_eff > 0) and (E5) (propagation budgets; thm:main-hodge-connection and thm:main-literal-link-compactness, proved in their periodic lattice settings) enter through their stated outputs on the common-cylinder interpolants (transport of the periodic outputs to the cylinder interpolants is not formalised); Cartan residuals are tested on all smooth tests of Omega; pointwise hypotheses are indexed from the start of the cofinal tail; the connection-test part of the remainder condition enters through the (E3) Euler residual, as in the handoff. Sorry-free, standard axioms only. Build note: this file imports SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean. INTEGRABILITY (follow-up): the conclusions of renewal_vacuum_equation (both routes), renewal_vacuum_equation_almost_surely and renewal_einstein_certification now also export, for every core test, Integrable (einsteinInsertionDensity beta lam (cfm eL x) (k x) (rcf RL x)) and Integrable (einsteinCosmologicalDensity ...) on volume.restrict K, so the vanishing pairing is a genuine Lebesgue integral and not the Bochner junk value. Derived from the existing hypotheses, with nothing added to the packet: integrable_einsteinInsertionDensity uses the limit coframe in L6(K') (lpTendsto_four_of_two_six), the identified curvature in L2(K'), and the limit lift -1/2 k g, which is a.e. bounded as the a.e. limit of the uniformly bounded (E1) lifts. tendsto_liftVariations applied to constant sequences then gives integrability of the three limiting classified variation densities. ae_classifiedMetricVariation_eq_einstein (the pointwise form of MollifiedBianchi.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible: first Bianchi identity at limit regularity using the amendment's spatial L3 bound, so13 antisymmetry, form antisymmetry) identifies the classified variation with the Einstein insertion density a.e. on K. POINTWISE FORM: ae_einsteinCosmologicalDensity_symmUnit_eq_zero (corollary file) and renewal_einstein_certification_ae (main file) show that the symmetrized density sqrt(-g)((G+Lambda g)_ij + (G+Lambda g)_ji) vanishes a.e. on int K, provided the core contains the constant symmetric unit tests E_ij+E_ji and their localizations phi(E_ij+E_ji), phi in D(int K), via the fundamental lemma. The richness condition is stated on the core itself, not on its closure, because integrability is only certified on the core. Non-vacuity of the richness condition: renewalEinsteinCertification_flat_ae (flat data, rich core flatFieldCore of all bounded measurable test fields). AUDIT 5 Oct 2026 (first independent audit): kept. Checked (i) no field other than spatialConnectionL3Bound lies outside (E1)-(E5)/mt:adm (torsion_rep/antisymm/L2 are the represented-torsion standing structure needed to state the (E3) Euler residual; the lift limit -1/2 k g is the coframe lift of the inverse-metric test); (ii) no field contains the conclusion; (iii) every record whose output enters as a hypothesis is proved in the ledger (thm:main-gravitational-classification, prop:supp-compact-upgrade, thm:main-curvature-propagation, thm:main-discrete-curvature, thm:main-hodge-connection, thm:main-literal-link-compactness; thm:supp-renewal-palatini and cor:supp-renewal-einstein used as proved lemmas), ass:main-connection-compactness is an assumption environment. Remaining disclosed renderings: transport of the periodic literal-link output to the cylinder interpolants; Cartan residuals for all smooth tests of Omega instead of a countable determining bank; closure clause under continuity of the pairing in the declared test topology. On the literal-link route the L3 bound follows from the paper's own eq:supp-literal-integrability (an L^{10/3} bound), so it is an amendment only on the full-connection route. |

| Proved (with disclosed renderings). EinsteinCertificationProcedure encodes the certification procedure (E1)-(E5) on a selected cofinal sequence of finite records (scores, coframes, connections, curvature records, Cartan writer W, represented torsion, physical lifts, realized first variations, on an open cylinder with compact K and a determining test core): standing regulator structure (mt:adm) and finite-record regularity, plus typed record data; `cert`/`fullCert`/`LiteralLinkRoute.cert` compute one Certificate per item of the failure bank of subsec:supp-failed-certificates, of six kinds (algebraic/residual/budget/margin/compactness tail/calibrated comparison): common-action curl/period (algebraic), realized metric variation (residual), first-variation remainder eq:main-first-variation-reduction (residual), lift consistency with -1/2 k g in L^inf(K) (residual) and lift budget, calibrated Holst/Palatini/volume coefficients (comparisons), Palatini normalization (margin), efficient cut margin I^eff of the endpoint graphs (margin, computed by FiniteWeightedGraph.efficientCutConstant), face-linearity (summability budget), memory (algebraic), Lorentz naturality (algebraic), torsion = spinless Euler residual on every compact (residual), Cartan nondegeneracy (capped margin), initial/growth/transfer/differentiated-source/duration/lapse budgets, interpolation stability, reconstruction error and discrete step margin (CurvatureRoute, continuous or discrete), Cartan curvature mismatch and Cartan/interface incidence mismatch (residuals, entrywise; interfaces enter through the distributional derivative), spatial L^3 connection budget (the amendment), literal-link chart (algebraic) and energy (budget), common-cylinder coframe/connection tails outside declared screens and transported-screen comparisons, L^6 coframe budget, coframe noncollapse (capped margin of det e). certification_of_passes proves Passes => RenewalEinsteinCertification along a subsequence with the limits PRODUCED from the certificates: (alpha,beta,lambda) from the comparisons (completeness of R), beta != 0 from the normalization margin, e and omega from tails + transported-screen comparisons via the new compact-screen upgrade (cauchy_of_screens) and the new completeness of L^2_loc on an open set with an a.e.-convergent diagonal subsequence (exists_l2LocTendsto_of_cauchy), nondegenerate/oriented limit coframe and Cartan floor from the margins, lift convergence from lift consistency + a.e. coframe convergence, L^6/L^3/lift bounds from the budgets, the curvature bound from the propagation budgets through the proved thm:main-curvature-propagation (curvature_energy_uniform_bound, derived_l2_curvature_bound) or thm:main-discrete-curvature (discrete_curvature_certificate), Cartan residuals from the entrywise residuals. einstein_limit_or_failed_certificate: either P.Passes and a subsequence satisfies the packet and the Einstein conclusion of thm:main-renewal-einstein (EinsteinCertificationConclusion, through renewal_einstein_certification via a shape-tolerant wrapper), or not P.Passes and P.FailureWitness (a common certificate fails with its cofinal witness, or the full-connection route fails with a witness and so does the literal-link route when present); witnesses provably obstruct (not_passes_of_failureWitness), so the alternative is exclusive. Non-vacuity: flatProcedure_passes (flat Minkowski data, identity screens, trivial discrete route, unit two-point endpoint graph) and collapsingProcedure_fails (two-point graphs with conductance 1/(n+1): the cut margin fails with its witness). Disclosed renderings: certificates are computed on the common-cylinder interpolants (as in the parent packet); items the manuscript describes in words are typed as data with stated properties: curl/period, memory and face-subdivision defects (real sequences), declared transported screens (ScreenFamily: measurable, exhausting, nested), the Cartan writer W, propagation writers and stable interpolations (writer form, M-skew principal block, growth bound, slice decomposition R = I y + zeta). The curl/period, memory, face and cut-margin certificates belong to Passes and to the failure bank but are not used in deriving (E1)-(E5) (the common action enters via its realized first variations, the classified score via the gravitational class, the spatial screens via the declared screens). On the periodic literal-link route the chart/energy criterion is computed on the link records, and the output of thm:main-literal-link-compactness transported to the common cylinder is the stated property LiteralLinkRoute.compactness (same transport gap as the parent's literal-link hypothesis). The positive branch is along a subsequence starting beyond the cofinal tail on which margins/algebraic tests pass (the paper's 'subsequential limit'). AUDIT 5 Oct 2026 (first independent audit): kept. Checked that no certificate passing is assumed, that the amendment enters only as the connectionL3 budget certificate (disclosed), and that the cut-margin witness is the margin subsequence (the potential witness itself is spatialScreen_neckBranch of thm:main-spatial-screen). |

ONE g (fD3b, proved in Lean): main_open_finite_realization assembles (O4) on a single continuum jet (Q, V, A, dQ, dV, d2Q) of the vacuum development g (G(g) = 0, prescribed data): on a common slab [0,T1] (T1 <= min of the slabs of finite_words_einstein, finite_words_uniform, finite_words_curvature) the word records converge to (Q, V, A), and for n >= n0 the word histories satisfy the (O1) bound \|\|(Q,Q')\|\|_{X^s_h} <= C eps, the (O2) rates C h eps in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3} against this same g, the (O3) UniformO3 package with the actual time derivative of the subsidiary field, and \|\|Riem(g_h) - Riem(g)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps. The identification of the limit of finite_words_einstein (built from supp_law_einstein) with that of finite_words_curvature (built from supp_open_einstein) is done in the proof by tendsto_nhds_unique applied to the same word interpolants (finite_words_curvature now also proves convergence of Q_h', Q_h'' to V, A). Marks: all word statements hold for arbitrary marks B_n of the closed ball; the open writer is B_n = 0. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. | Proved. Previous content kept (renewalRateLagrangian_eq_homogeneous, eulerU_eq/eulerV_eq = eq:main-renewal-euler, renewal_stationary_iff_friedmann, firstVariation_integrand_eq + momentum_boundary_term_integral_eq_zero). NEW (the missing clause): Gravity/FriedmannLapseEinsteinExact.lean computes the Einstein tensor of the metric FIELD g = -N(x^0)^2 dt^2 + a(x^0)^2 delta (lapseMetric) from the metric itself: metricJetOf g x = ((g x)^{-1} at type Matrix, genuine coordinate partial derivatives d_c g_ij and d_d d_c g_ij via Mathlib deriv along coordinate lines, partialDeriv); Ricci = CoordinateCurvatureJet.ricciJet (Christoffel from the jet, product-rule derivative, Riem = dGamma - dGamma + GammaGamma - GammaGamma), einsteinOf = Ric - (1/2) R g. metricJetOf_lapseMetric evaluates the jet (nothing defined to be the answer: christoffel_lapse / dChristoffel_lapse / ricci_lapse are proved equalities with closed forms; d_0^2 g_00 is left as an opaque deriv value and cancels). Results: G_00 = 3 a'^2/a^2, G_kk = -(2 a a''/N^2 - 2 a a' N'/N^3 + a'^2/N^2), G_mu nu = 0 off-diagonal; G_00 + Lambda g_00 = N^2 (3 H^2 - Lambda) (lapse constraint), G_kk + Lambda g_kk = -a^2 (2 D_tau H + 3 H^2 - Lambda), with H = hubbleRate a a' N = a'/(N a) and D_tau = properTimeDeriv N (the same objects as in eulerU_eq/eulerV_eq); lapse_vacuum_einstein_iff: all 16 components of G + Lambda g vanish iff 3H^2 = Lambda and D_tau H = 0. For the metric reconstructed from the renewal variables by eq:main-isotropic-renewal-map (a = e^{v/3}, N = e^{u/2+v/3}): renewal_euler_einstein_components gives the exact identities G_00 + Lambda g_00 = (N/a^3) E_u and G_kk + Lambda g_kk = -(E_v/2 - E_u/3)/(N a) (the 00 equation IS the lapse Euler equation; the spatial equations are the density Euler equation given the constraint), and renewal_stationary_iff_vacuum_einstein: (E_u = E_v = 0) iff G + Lambda g = 0 (complete, lapse constraint included) iff eq:main-vacuum-friedmann. homogeneous_friedmann_complete assembles the record. Scoped regularity (disclosed): at the evaluation time t, u differentiable at t, v differentiable on a neighbourhood of t with derivative v', v' differentiable at t (the paper's smooth ansatz; the Euler-side hypotheses are those of renewal_stationary_iff_friedmann plus v differentiable near t, needed to differentiate the Christoffel symbols). As before, delta S = int (E_u du + E_v dv) is the pointwise first-variation identity plus vanishing of the boundary term (differentiation under the integral sign not formalised). Non-vacuity: desitter_lapseMetric_vacuum (a = e^{Ht}, N = 1 solves G + 3H^2 g = 0 through this machinery) and lapse_einstein_00_ne_zero_example (a = e^t, N = 2: G_00 = 3). Checklist: (a) no curvature object is defined to be the answer (Gamma, dGamma, Ric, G computed from the metric field); (e)/(l) the hypotheses are regularity only, no Einstein/Friedmann consequence assumed; (k) non-flat examples given. | Proved (es10b1, 1 Oct 2026). explicit_operational_family : ExplicitOperationalFamilyStatement T ω q₀ σ for all T, ω, q₀ > 0, σ = ±1. The cylinders are faithful encodings of Appendix supp:homogeneous-dynamics: AcceptRejectCylinder (M stages, finite alphabets, start, stage cost, temperature, retry cap); at each stage the source proposes uniformly from the next alphabet and accepts with exp(-E/ε); the capped trial loop is FiniteMarkovPaths.retryLaw, defined by its first-trial recursion (closed forms retryLaw_some/none, probability law retryLaw_total); a successful history has the product probability pathProb, failure has failureProb, outcomeLaw is the law on Option (ℕ → ℝ) (a probability law, outcomeLaw_univ). explicitCylinder uses exactly eq:supp-explicit-scales: s = T/M, rank-one cost eq:main-rank-one-cost (A₀ = 8/3), δ_M = M^-3, ε_M = M^-5/(1+log M), F₀ = {q₀}, F_k = δ_M ℤ ∩ [L_k, U_k] with L₀ = q₀/2, U₀ = 3q₀/2, L_{k+1} = rL_k − δ, U_{k+1} = rU_k + δ, retry cap ⌈7 log M / Z_M⌉ with the explicit row-wise acceptance lower bound Z_M. For M ≥ M₀ (explicit threshold): alphabets nonempty and positive, failure ≤ M^-6 (union bound), E[ℰ_M; success] ≤ C M^-4 (entropy-energy estimate: Gibbs mean ≤ softMin ≤ cost of nearest target + ε log #F via FiniteLaplace.softMin_le), E[max_j \|q_j − q₀e^{σωτ_j}\|; success] ≤ C M^-2 (path control eq:supp-homogeneous-path-control by a weighted induction, plus log_profile_error_le), the same bounds with 2C conditionally on success (E[·\|success] = E[·;success]/(1 − failure)); expected absolute first variation of S_d (B₀ = A₀ω²) — the true derivative ε ↦ S_d(q + εw, s + εsn) at 0, with w_j = φ(τ_j), n_j = ψ(τ_j), covering the lapse constraint — is ≤ C_φ M^-2 for every Lipschitz scale test with φ(0) = φ(T) = 0 and every bounded lapse test (this contains all smooth tests by ContDiffOn.exists_lipschitzOnWith). Under ANY common coupling (any measure space Ω and outcomes O M with P(O M⁻¹'S) = outcomeLaw S for all S, M ≥ M₀), via Borel–Cantelli (ae_eventually_notMem = measure_limsup_atTop_eq_zero) a.s.: failures only finitely often, ℰ_M → 0, the piecewise-affine paths (continuous, derivative = cell slope off nodes) converge uniformly on [0,T] and in H¹(0,T) (h1DistSq = ∫(f−g)² + ∫(f'−g')², f' = deriv f) to q₀e^{σωt}; the attached A₃ root race κ_j = q_j^{-4/3}, k = κ_j/(8h²), m = q_j²h³ is positive with bracket (k h²)Σαα^T = κ_j I and log(κ_j⁻¹/q₀^{4/3}) − 2Hτ_j → 0 uniformly in j (H = 2σω/3), and the limiting de Sitter metric solves Ric − (R/2)g + Λg = 0 with Λ = 3H² = 4ω²/3. Non-vacuity: example instantiating the statement at T = ω = q₀ = σ = 1; independent_coupling builds the infinite product coupling (Measure.infinitePi) satisfying the coupling hypothesis. Renderings disclosed: E is taken over the capped process restricted to (and conditioned on) success; the regulator-to-metric step uses the A₃ bracket identity 𝓑_j = κ_j I and the proper-time ADM inversion (spatial metric κ⁻¹ I, as in mt:adm, proved), not a re-derivation of the generator Taylor expansion; scale tests are Lipschitz (⊇ smooth); the threshold M₀ is explicit. CURVATURE RENDERING (audit 5 Oct 2026): the last two conjuncts of ExplicitOperationalFamilyStatement use RelationalDeSitterBranch.ricciTensor H t := 3H^2 g, scalarCurvature := 12H^2 (closed forms), so by themselves they are an unfolding; that these are the Ricci tensor and scalar curvature of the limiting metric lorentzMetric H t = flrwG(e^{Ht}), H = 2 sigma omega/3, is RelationalDeSitterBranch.coordinate_desitter_einstein_space (Christoffel symbols from the metric jet, flrw_christoffel; desitter_einstein_space), and FriedmannLapseEinstein.desitter_lapseMetric_vacuum computes G + 3H^2 g = 0 from the metric field. The convergence of the regulators to this metric is the uniform log kappa_j^{-1} -> log q0^{4/3} + 2 H tau_j clause (spatial metric kappa^{-1} I by the proper-time ADM inversion). Optional cleanup: replace the last conjuncts by the computed-curvature statement. |

| All clauses proved. Earlier passes: eq:main-hyperbolic-blocking (exact inf over z>0), invariance of mu^2=AB, C, D, Delta_rel under every finite sequence of blockings, the block map eq:main-homogeneous-block-map, Delta_rel>0 preserved, unit/rescaling invariance, the coth/csch dictionary, and the exact twist retention eq:supp-finite-twist-read (FiniteTwistRetention). NEW (Action/FiniteBlockingGridLimit.lean + Analysis/FiniteLaplaceSandwich.lean): the finite blocked cost E^eps_{G,n} is defined recursively by the finite-temperature grid blocking step softBlock = -eps log(m^{-1} sum_{z in G} exp(-(E1(x,z)+E2(z,y))/eps)); exp(-E^eps_{G,n}/eps) = m L^n for the subprobability kernel L = m^{-1} e^{-E/eps} (exp_neg_finiteBlockedCost_div); the grid kernel of E_{mu,theta,C} is D_C L_0 D_C^{-1} with symmetric positive L_0, so the finite blocked cost carries exactly the twist C (finiteBlockedCost_twist); eq:supp-finite-block-error 0 <= E^eps_{G,n}(x,y) - E_{mu,n theta,C}(x,y) <= 4u(n-1)d^2 + eps(n-1) log m for a grid G in (0,R] covering (0,R] with error d, x,y in (0,R], n>=1 (finite_block_error; the sharper 2u(n-1)d^2 is finiteBlockedCost_le_sharp; proof by induction with the two-edge Schur identity and the Laplace sandwich, bridge minimizers stay in (0,max(x,y)]); the schedule d_n = n^-2, m_n <= K n^2, eps_n = n^-3/(1+log n) at fixed total angle Theta = n theta gives error <= (4 mu cosh Theta/Theta + log K + 2) n^-2 (finite_block_error_schedule); the three-probe reconstruction (prop:supp-three-costs formulas at the total duration S) is exact on measured costs and continuous where AB != 0, so for A,B,S>0, BS^2<4A the reconstructed Delta_rel of the finite blocked costs tends to (AB-C^2)/(AB) and stays above Delta_rel/2 > 0 eventually (finite_blocking_relativeDefect_limit). Non-vacuity: exists_grid_schedule (uniform grids) and an example with A=B=1, C=1/2, S=1. DISCLOSED renderings: (i) the manuscript leaves E^eps_{F,n} implicit; the stated bound (lower bound 0, entropy term eps(n-1) log m) holds for the probability-normalized average over the n-1 intermediate grid amplitudes, i.e. E^eps = -eps log(m L^n) with L = m^{-1}e^{-E/eps}; for -eps log L^n itself the window is [eps log m, 4u(n-1)d^2 + eps n log m], and with the unnormalized counting sum the lower bound 0 fails (manuscript query: make the 1/m normalization explicit); (ii) 'covering error d' is rendered as: G is contained in (0,R] and every z in (0,R] has a grid point within d, with endpoints/probes in (0,R]; (iii) 'fixed total duration' is rendered as fixed total hyperbolic angle Theta = 2 artanh((S/2)sqrt(B/A)) split into n elementary angles Theta/n (blocking adds angles), and 'three-probe reconstruction' uses the costs E(u,u), E(u,v), E(v,u) at duration S with probes 0<u!=v<=R. AUDIT 5 Oct 2026: kept. Manuscript query stands: the 1/m normalization of the finite blocked cost E^eps_{F,n} must be made explicit (with the unnormalized sum the lower bound 0 of eq:supp-finite-block-error fails). |

(G3) pathwise_c1 + readout_full_curvature_of_envelope (eq:main-gowdy-full-curvature, Ric(g_h) = O(h), reference Ricci flat). (G4) Gibbs clause: GibbsPath.path_gibbs_bound, normalizer_ge_zero_offset (as before). (G4) action-test clause NOW PROVED. Gravity/GowdyActionTestDensity.lean encodes the manuscript's boundary-marked ADM density eq:supp-gowdy-adm-density with -2NR'' replaced by 2N'R' as the manuscript prescribes (admDensity; the six fields R, lambda, P, Q, N, beta independent; v_u = u_t - beta u'), its exact first variation (hasDerivAt_admDensity_line), the independent midpoint/centered discretization S_h = sum_{n<n0} sum_j h l L(gridJet) (midpoint time average, forward time quotient, centered space difference of the time average; N and beta independent) and delta S_h = d/ds S_h(u + s phi)\|_{s=0} (discreteFirstVariation), evaluated at the recorded areal-gauge fields of a scheme history (arealRecord: R = t_n, lambda, P, Q from the records, N = 1, beta = 0) in the direction of the grid-sampled test (testSample). Gravity/GowdyActionTestEulerLagrange.lean: the six Euler-Lagrange identities at the areal lift eq:supp-gowdy-areal-lift (liftVariation_eq_divergence: the first-variation density equals d_t F + d_theta G; lapse from eq_lam, shift from constraint_lam, lambda trivially, R from the stress identity (eq_a..eq_d), P from eq_a/eq_P/constraint_P, Q from eq_c/eq_Q/constraint_Q) and criticality continuumFirstVariation_arealLift_eq_zero (delta S_* = 0 for C^1 tests periodic in theta vanishing on the time edges; integration by parts RectangleIntegrationByParts.integral2_divergence_eq_zero from Mathlib's divergence theorem). Gravity/GowdyActionTest.lean: action_test (eq:supp-gowdy-action-test): for every smooth test of all six fields (lapse and shift varied before the areal restriction), 2pi-periodic in theta, supported in [ta,tb] x T^1 with t0 < ta <= tb < t1, there are C, l0 with \|delta S_h - delta S_*\| <= C (kappa_h + h) for h = l = 2pi/N <= l0, every window [tau0, tau0 + n0 h] in [t0,t1] whose interior contains [ta,tb], and every history realized by split steps in the enforced C^1_h envelope with kappa_h <= 1 (kappa_h = eps0 + h^2 + sqrt F_h, eq:supp-gowdy-kappa, in the C^1_h norm: actionKappa), and delta S_* = 0. action_test_pathwise: on the (G3) histories (offset cap c0 h^4, initial C^1_h error c1 h^2) kappa_h = O(h) (actionKappa_le_of_offset_cap) and \|delta S_h\| <= C h, i.e. the first-variation residual tends to zero, which is the (G4) assertion. Proof chain: state error r = 1 + nodal_time_difference give recorded-jet errors O(kappa_h) (exists_history_error_bounds); cell Taylor/mean-value comparison of discrete and continuum jets O(h) (exists_cellJet_error, exists_sampleField_jet_error); Lipschitz bound of the first-variation density on a ball; cell Riemann sums (abs_sum_cells_sub_integral2_le). Disclosed renderings: (i) the quantitative rate is C (kappa_h + h) instead of the manuscript's C kappa_h (first-order cell quadrature; (G4) itself only asserts that the residual tends to zero, and that is proved); (ii) smallness kappa_h <= 1 in action_test (automatic on the (G3) histories for small h); (iii) the time window of the discrete action must cover the test support; (iv) delta S_* is the integral of the pointwise first variation of the density. Non-vacuity: exists_nonzero_local_test (a nonzero smooth bump test satisfies the test hypotheses); the history packet is that of state_error_crNorm_flux/readout_full_curvature_of_envelope (constGrid_history_envelope, zeroReference_pathwiseHistory). All new declarations use only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. AUDIT 5 Oct 2026: kept (readout_full_curvature_of_envelope and pathwise_c1 signatures re-read). |
| `thm:supp-right-congruence` | theorem | History-level right congruence | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.branch_eq_of_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.step_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.futureEquivalent_equivalence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | Proved on the typed grammar law (FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw, TypedEventGrammar.lean): future equivalence of same-cut-type histories is an equivalence (futureEquivalent_equivalence, futureSetoid); equivalent histories have equal one-letter probabilities p(a\|h) = p(a\|h') for every letter a (prob_eq_of_futureEquivalent, eq:supp-one-letter-equality, using the one-letter future); on the positive-probability branch 0 < p(a\|h) both extensions are admissible and h a ~ h' a (futureEquivalent_snoc, eq:supp-right-congruence, by cancellation in the chain law wordProb_append). futureEquivalent_typedPredictionBisimulation packages this as 'future equivalence is a typed prediction bisimulation'. The untyped PredictiveSystem lemmas (DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean; total steps, untyped histories) are kept as secondary pointers only. |
| `thm:supp-minimal-record` | theorem | Canonical minimum predictive realization | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.derived_predictive_relation_carrier`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_branch`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_update_of_occurs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.generated_ledger_is_compiled_presentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | Proved in TypedPredictiveCategoryExact (typed, partial updates), over the grammar's conditional law. Realizations = TypedPredictiveCategory.Presentation (def:supp-predictive-realization: state sets per cut type, history maps, branch functions with p(a\|h) = r_a(s(h)), Option-valued updates required only on positive-probability branches; reads optional, take ReadValue = Unit). futureEquivalent_of_stateOf_eq DERIVES 'same record => equal complete futures' (bisimulation principle futureEquivalent_of_typedPredictionBisimulation, induction on word length). minimal = Z^min_X as a presentation (weight = kappa, transition = T on positive branches, transition_project). toMinimal S with toMinimal_spec: pi_x s_x = q_x, pi_x surjective at every cut type, and any two morphisms to Z^min coincide; Hom.ofStateOf shows every family of maps with q = pi s is a morphism, so pi is unique among all such maps; Hom fields map_rate / map_update give r_a = kappa_a pi_x and pi_y u_a = T_a pi_x on every positive-probability successor; minimal_isTerminal: Z^min is terminal (unique coarsest reachable realization), hence a generated ledger/hidden-state presentation has a unique quotient onto it. Renderings: state sets not required finite (generalization: terminality over a larger class); branch functions real-valued ([0,1] automatic at reachable states). The untyped DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal pointers are secondary only: their Realization assumes future_factor and total updates. |
| `thm:supp-persistence-derived` | theorem | Persistence derived from composition | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.minimalUpdate_mk_of_occurs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.step_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | eq:supp-persistence: ConditionalLaw.transition_project (typed) with h = H_n, a = A_{n+1}: on every realized (positive-probability) branch Z_{n+1} = [H_n A_{n+1}] = T_{A_{n+1}}(Z_n), well defined by the right congruence futureEquivalent_snoc; hence the packet (Z_n, A_{n+1}, Z_{n+1}) is a function of the event process (untyped analogue minimalUpdate_mk_of_occurs). 'Every other deterministic persistent predictive packet maps uniquely onto it': for any reachable presentation S (TypedPredictiveCategory.Presentation, partial updates on positive branches) the unique morphism toMinimal S (toMinimal_spec, minimal_isTerminal) satisfies pi(s(h)) = [h] and intertwines realized updates (Hom.map_update), so (pi, id, pi) carries (s(H_n), A_{n+1}, s(H_{n+1})) to (Z_n, A_{n+1}, Z_{n+1}), uniquely. Not a separately named Lean theorem; a direct instance of the cited typed results. 'Persistent relational event packet' is the paper's name for this triple (definitional). |
| `cor:supp-future-operational-transfer` | corollary | Future-operational transfer | [`RenewalGeometry.upstream_compiler_transfer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CertificateRecordBatch02.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.minimalPredictiveQuotient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CertificateRecordBatch02.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.minimalPredictiveProjection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/CertificateRecordBatch02.lean) | upstream_compiler_transfer: for any candidate state set with declared future-signature map sigma and any readout D with sigma s = sigma t => D s = D t, there is a unique Dbar on minimalPredictiveQuotient sigma (states modulo equal signatures) with D = Dbar o minimalPredictiveProjection sigma (exists-unique), i.e. eq:supp-compiler-factorization. Rendering: Z^min is represented as the signature quotient of the candidate states. For histories (sigma = ConditionalLaw.futureSignature) this quotient is literally Z^min_{X,x} = H_{X,x}/~_X (futureEquivalent_iff_futureSignature, project_eq_iff). For a reachable typed presentation S the signature of a state is that of any representing history (well defined by futureEquivalent_of_stateOf_eq), and toMinimal_spec (pi(s(h)) = [h], pi surjective) shows the fibres of the universal surjection pi_X are exactly the equal-signature classes, so the signature quotient is Z^min_X with projection pi_X; this short identification is not a separately named theorem. The untyped toMinimal_eq_iff_futureLaw_eq is not used (its Realization assumes future_factor). |
| `prop:supp-finite-prediction` | proposition | Finite predictive reconstruction with an explicit horizon convention | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar.typedFinitePrediction_horizon`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.TypedFinitePresentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.TypedFinitePresentation.typedFinitePrediction_refinement`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.TypedFinitePresentation.stable_round_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureEquivalent_of_typedPredictionBisimulation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedFinitePredictionCountermodel.typedFinitePrediction_countermodel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedFinitePredictionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar.finite_history`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammarHorizon.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MinimalRecordPartitionRefinement.refinement_stabilizes`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/MinimalRecordPartitionRefinementExact.lean) | All four clauses proved on the typed grammar of def:supp-event-grammar (FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw). (1) Finite horizon: typedFinitePrediction_horizon — on a FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar (remaining horizon carried by the cut type, strictly decreasing along letters; complete bank of typed continuations) the future quotient MinimalState x is finite at every cut type, future equivalence is a right congruence on every positive-probability branch and T_a[h]=[ha]. (2) Unbounded horizon: TypedFinitePresentation = reachable finite presentation with m = card Record records (cut type per record, history map, branch functions, partial updates required only on positive-probability branches); refineOut/refineUpdate encode the refinement of the proof (initialized by cut type and branch probabilities/domains, successor blocks required for positive-probability letters; zero-probability letters totalized by the identity, which does not change the partition). typedFinitePrediction_refinement: every strict round k satisfies k < m-1 and every round j >= m-1 equals round m-1; round m-1 identifies the records of two histories iff they are future equivalent; and round m-1 identifies two records iff they represent future-equivalent same-type histories (= coarsest predictive partition). (3) stable_round_futureEquivalent: any stable round is sufficient for equality of all finite futures (via the bisimulation principle futureEquivalent_of_typedPredictionBisimulation, induction on word length with the chain rule). (4) Negative clause: typedFinitePrediction_countermodel — the binary renewal law with renewal probability 1/(n+2) after n nonrenewals on a grammar with one cut type and alphabet Bool: finite cut types and letter sets, no strictly decreasing horizon function exists, and Infinite (MinimalState ()). Renderings: terminal Reads are event letters of the typed grammar (def:supp-operational-datum allows reads as terminal event outcomes), so 'immediate read values' are part of the branch probabilities; record counts m are total over all cut types. |
| `prop:supp-predictive-category` | proposition | Terminality and naturality of predictive reduction | [`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedOperationalLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.Presentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.Hom`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.instCategoryPresentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.minimal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.minimal_isTerminal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.toMinimal_spec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.futureEquivalent_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_weight`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_transition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_read`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_refl`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedLawIso.minimalStateEquiv_trans`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedPredictiveCategoryExact.lean) | Proved in the typed probabilistic setting with reads. TypedOperationalLaw = finite typed event grammar + conditional law (FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw) + terminal Reads of histories, which belong to the declared future test bank and are therefore constant on future classes (read_congr; scoped hypothesis, needed for Z^min to carry reads at all). Presentation = reachable predictive presentation (state sets per cut type, history maps, reads, branch functions, partial updates on positive-probability branches); Hom = maps with s' = f o s, preserving reads and branch probabilities and intertwining every positive-probability successor; instCategoryPresentation is the category (thin by reachability). minimal = Z^min (MinimalState/project/weight/transition, reads descended) as a presentation; minimal_isTerminal : IsTerminal (minimal L) (Mathlib CategoryTheory), via futureEquivalent_of_stateOf_eq (same record => same complete futures, by the bisimulation principle of TypedFinitePredictionExact); toMinimal_spec: the unique map sends s(h) to [h] and is surjective. Naturality: TypedLawIso (bijections of cut types, letters, reachable histories and read values commuting with extension, preserving branch probabilities and reads) with refl/trans; futureEquivalent_iff (equal future signatures carried to equal ones and reflected); minimalStateEquiv : Z^min_x ≃ Z'^min_(e x), preserving weights, transitions and reads; minimalStateEquiv_refl and minimalStateEquiv_trans give compatibility with identities and composition. Rendering: state sets are not required finite (the category contains the finite presentations of def:supp-predictive-realization as a full subcategory, in which Z^min is terminal whenever it is finite, e.g. finite horizon or certified finite index); the [0,1] bound on branch functions is automatic at reachable states. |
| `thm:supp-hankel` | theorem | Canonical typed Hankel realization | [`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.typed_hankel_realization_field`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.HankelSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelState`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelEffect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelEffect_state`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelSpace_reachable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelSpace_separated`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.precomp_mem_hankelSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelTransition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelTransition_state`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelEffect_comp_hankelTransition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankelTransition_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.responseMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankel_reach_quot_unobs_equiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankel_reach_quot_unobs_equiv_state`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankel_finrank_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HankelField.hankel_finrank_eq_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelRealizationEqualityCase.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankel_minimality`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelMinimality.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.minimalRealizationSimilarity_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HankelMinimality.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.typed_hankel_realization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedHankelRealization.lean) | Proved over an ARBITRARY field K (in particular the paper's R; the earlier C-valued files remain). Per typed sector with Hankel table p(f,p): HankelSpace = M = span_K{h_p}; hankelState h_p in M and hankelEffect lambda_f(m) = m(f) with lambda_f(h_p) = p(f,p) (hankelEffect_state); M is reachable (hankelSpace_reachable: the states span M) and future separated (hankelSpace_separated). For a primitive event a with past map p -> a o p and future map f -> f o a satisfying p'(f, a o p) = p(f o a, p): hankelTransition A_a : M -> M' (precomp_mem_hankelSpace shows precomposition maps M into M'), A_a h_p = h_{a o p} (hankelTransition_state), lambda_f A_a = lambda_{f o a} (hankelEffect_comp_hankelTransition), unique (hankelTransition_unique). For every linear realization (states n_p in N, effects mu_f with mu_f(n_p) = p(f,p)): N^reach/N^unobs ~= M (hankel_reach_quot_unobs_equiv, sending [n_p] to h_p), dim M <= dim N (hankel_finrank_le, N finite-dimensional), and hankel_finrank_eq_iff: dim N = dim M <-> (span{n_p} = N and the effects separate N) -- BOTH directions of 'equality exactly on the reachable future-separated branch', with the forward direction by the finrank argument (dim M <= dim reach <= dim N forces reach = top; rank-nullity of the response map forces trivial kernel). Bundled in typed_hankel_realization_field; non-vacuity example: identity table on Fin 2 over R (dim M = 2) and a padded R^3 realization which, by the equality case, is not reachable-and-separated. Rendering: realizations are given by states and effects (transition maps of N are not needed for the minimality clauses, so the statement is more general). |

cessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PhysicalChoiTomographyFrame`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.causalCombPrefixes_unique_from_terminal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.krausMap_reducedKraus`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.SequentialIsometricComb.combAmp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.SequentialIsometricComb.trace_realizedChoi_mul_combProd_choi`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.SequentialIsometricComb.typedBranches`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.typedBranches_op_covariant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.hsBlockUnitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.rep_eq_hsBlockUnitary_conj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.causalReadout`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.causalReadout_hsBlockUnitary_conj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.typedBranches_realization_independent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean) | Proved (repair of the audit defects (c), missing V_k / eq:supp-comb-unitary, and (l) frame independence). Tomography: for spanning physical slot frames (PhysicalChoiTomographyFrame with span = top) the dual-frame tensor combFrameTensor F p represents ALL multilinear intervention probabilities (combPairing_combFrameTensor: Tr(R (M_N x ... x M_1)) = p(M_1,...,M_N) for every tuple, via the frame-expansion representation lemma combPairing_combDualTensor), the representing tensor is unique (combPairing_injective, combPairing_eq_iff), probabilities on physical trace-normalized positive probes already determine it (multilinear_eq_of_physical, combTensor_eq_of_physical), frame independence is DERIVED rather than assumed (combFrameTensor_frame_independent: both frames represent the same functional), and the tensor is Hermitian when the probabilities are real (combFrameTensor_isHermitian). Process notion defined independently: SequentialIsometricComb (memories A_k, A_0 a point, link maps V_k : H_in (x) A_k -> H_out (x) A_{k+1} with V_k^H V_k = 1); its Choi tensors realizedChoi k = W_k W_k^H with W_k the sequential link (cumulative) - i.e. link followed by final memory discard. 'Deterministic finite quantum process exactly when' is the non-tautological equivalence exists_sequentialIsometricComb_iff / exists_realizing_comb_iff_terminal / exists_sequentialIsometricComb_iff_conditions: necessity (realizedChoi_isDeterministic) is computed from the isometries; sufficiency constructs the links V_k by Gram matching of consecutive minimal rank factors (exists_rank_factor, exists_isometry_of_gram_eq, exists_canonicalLink, canonicalComb); the paper's literal conditions (R^(N) psd, Tr_out R^(k+1) = I (x) R^(k), R^(0) = 1) force prefix positivity (isDeterministicCombThrough_iff_terminal). Support-minimal branch: canonicalComb realizes every R^(k) (canonicalComb_realizedChoi) with card A_k = rank R^(k) (canonicalComb_card) and is prefix-support minimal (IsPrefixSupportMinimal = the span condition eq:ncg-prefix-minimality, canonicalComb_minimal); every realization has dim A_k >= rank R^(k) with equality iff prefix minimal (realizedChoi_rank_le_card, isPrefixSupportMinimal_iff_card_eq_rank); A_k = supp R^(k) via W_k (memory_equiv_support). Sequential uniqueness: sequentialComb_unitary_covariance - two prefix-support-minimal realizations of the same terminal tensor are related by memory unitaries U_k with U_0 = I, W'_k = (I (x) U_k) W_k and V'_k = (I (x) U_{k+1}) V_k (I (x) U_k)^H, the family being unique among isometric families with U_0 = I satisfying the link relation (proof: per-cut minimal-purification unitary exists_memory_unitary, then cancellation against the spanning input-extended prefix vectors). Named retained operators: the reduced Kraus operator of every fixed external intervention is transported by the same unitaries, K' = U_{k+1} K U_k^H (reducedKraus_covariant, bundled in supportMinimal_sequential_realization), and so is the whole represented word algebra through the Hilbert-Schmidt unitary (+)U_k (x) conj U_k (typedBranches_realization_independent, rep_eq_hsBlockUnitary_conj); trace_realizedChoi_mul_combProd_choi shows the realization reproduces every intervention probability as a sum of squared sequential amplitudes. Bundled: finite_process_comb_tomography, supportMinimal_sequential_realization. Scoped renderings (disclosed): the same finite slot spaces O = H_out, I = H_in at every slot (CombCarrier convention); tables are complex-multilinear functionals of the slot Choi matrices (determined by their values on physical probes); [Nonempty I] in the uniqueness clause to derive the prefix tensors from the terminal one. The last two sentences of the statement (minimum predictive state remains a separate classical quotient; sharp readability not inferred) are remarks and are not formalized. Non-vacuity: qubitIdentityComb with the examples at the end of SequentialIsometricCombRealization.lean (covariance theorem instantiated on its canonical realization). |

| Proved for one and the same set. Reads are matrix coefficients: matrixCoefficientRead pi v (i,j) x = <v i, pi(x) v j> for any *-representation pi : A ->*a[C] (E ->L[C] E) on a complex Hilbert space and any family v of terminal vectors; matrixCoefficientRead_adjointClosed DERIVES (no extra hypothesis) the adjoint closure ReadsAdjointClosed (every Read R_k has a reversed Read R_k' with R_k'(x*) = conj R_k(x); Hermitian Reads are the special case k' = k, readsAdjointClosed_of_hermitian). Under adjoint closure: contextualNull_star_mem (the paper's 'reversing the context shows a* is null'), contextualNull_eq_contextualFutureNull (the manuscript's star-free null set J^ctx = the complete star-tested null set); contextualNull_isGreatest: J^ctx (as a TwoSidedIdeal) is the GREATEST element of {two-sided *-ideals invisible to every Read} (it contains every invisible two-sided ideal, star-closed or not; le_contextualNullTwoSidedIdeal_iff: invisibility in every context = inclusion). Envelope: ContextualHistoryEnvelope = A / J (RingCon quotient) with induced star (StarRing), C-algebra structure, *-algebra quotient map historyQuotientMap and descended Reads historyRead; it is context preserving (historyRead_comp) and separated (historyEnvelope_separated; for adjoint-closed Reads also for the star-free tests, historyEnvelope_separated_starFree). historyEnvelope_minimum: for EVERY context-preserving envelope (surjective *-algebra map pi : A ->*a[C] B with R = R' o pi): ker pi <= J; there is a *-algebra map psi : B -> A/J with psi o pi = q, unique among ring maps; if B is separated psi is bijective and transports the Reads, and ContextPreservingEnvelope.historyEquiv gives B ~=*a A/J over A. Bundled: contextual_ideal_hermitianReads (largest *-ideal, J = complete null ideal, A/J separated, every separated envelope *-isomorphic to A/J over A). Non-vacuity: M_2(C) with entry Reads (matrix coefficients between basis vectors; adjoint closed but not Hermitian, identity visible so J is proper) and B(C^2) with all matrix coefficients. Rendering: admitted contexts u, v range over the whole represented algebra (represented words span it and nullity is linear); Reads are additive maps to C. Checklist: (a) J is defined by the paper's nullity condition, maximality/envelope properties are proved; (b) no stored conclusions, envelope built from (A, R); (e) adjoint closure is derived from the matrix-coefficient definition; (k) non-trivial M_2(C) example. |

| Proved (new file Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean on top of WordGramPositivityExact/FlatWordReconstructionExact, compiled, standard axioms). flat_word_reconstruction is thm:supp-word-flatness in full, for a represented generator family gen on a nonempty finite carrier closed under a letter involution inv (star (gen g) = gen (inv g)): (i) every panel K_{X,r} is positive semidefinite; (ii) there is a least r_X with rank K_{X,r_X} = rank K_{X,r_X+1} (eq:supp-flat-stop, strict growth below); (iii) W_{X,<=r_X} = A^hist and the spans are constant from r_X on; (iv) the panel through depth r_X+1 reconstructs the object: for any represented family gen' over the same alphabet and involution on any nonempty finite carrier M' with gramPanel gen (r_X+1) = gramPanel gen' (r_X+1) there is a star-algebra isomorphism psi : A^hist ~=* A'^hist with psi [w] = [w]' for EVERY history word w (units, generator multiplication, involution and every represented mixed history), and psi matches the predictive core algebras StarAlgebra.adjoin (gen '' Delta) of every sub-alphabet Delta (in particular the minimum predictive state algebra). Construction (exists_starAlgEquiv_of_panel_eq): the word coordinate map T_d c = sum_v c_v [v] has kernel exactly the Gram kernel of the panel, c in ker T_d <-> c^H K_{X,d} c = 0 (mem_ker_wordCombination_iff, faithfulness of the regular trace), so equal panels give equal kernels; at a flat depth T_r and T_{r+1} are onto A^hist (exhaustion clause) and flatness transfers to gen' via the depth-r sub-panel (gramPanel_eq_submatrix); panelEquiv is the induced linear isomorphism (first isomorphism theorem, quotients by the common kernel); multiplicativity comes from the depth-(r+1) relation [g] sum_v c_v [v] = sum_v c_v [g v] (genElt_mul_wordCombination) plus Algebra.adjoin_induction; the involution from [v]^* = [reverse (map inv v)] (star_wordCombination). Disclosed rendering: 'reconstructs' = reconstruction up to a canonical star-isomorphism compatible with the word labels (the paper's proof: 'the one-step extension reconstructs the generator multiplication matrices and reversed words reconstruct the involution'). Scoped hypotheses: nonempty finite carriers, finite alphabet (Fintype (Words Gamma d)), the letter involution inv is part of the data (equivalent to the earlier star-closure hypothesis by choice). |

alExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.indepProduct`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.indepProduct_futureEquivalent_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.indepProductMinimalEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.indepProductMinimalEquiv_branch`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.contextualIdeal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.historyAlgebra_starAlgEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.productContexts_null_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.compositeHistoryAlgebraEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GrandTensorMonoidal.compositeHistoryMoment`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/GrandTensorMonoidalExact.lean) | Proved (completing the earlier partial GrandTensorMonoidalExact; new file GrandTensorMonoidalCoherence imports it, no existing file modified). (1) Coordinate rule: gram_coordinate_change (c = C c~ gives K~ = C^H K C). (2) Unitary covariance, now concrete: a unitary e : H ~=_li H' induces adjoinConjStarAlgEquiv, the *-isomorphism a -> U a U^* from the *-algebra generated by the represented operators S (raw algebra) onto the one generated by U S U^*; admitted contexts = recordContexts (record matrix coefficients <u, x a y v> of left/right history contexts x,y between record vectors u,v; context-closed); a record-preserving unitary (records -> e''records) carries contexts to contexts (recordContexts_conj_iff), so unitary_history_covariance gives the induced isomorphism of history algebras [a] -> [U a U^*], compatible with the involution and preserving every word moment tau_reg([v]^*[w]) (via historyAlgebra_starAlgEquiv). (3) Z12^min = Z1^min x Z2^min, typed: TypedPredictiveSystem (predictive law + cut type of histories; histories of different cut type never identified; inadmissible letters = branch probability 0), typedIndepProduct (cut types O1 x O2, product histories = product reachability, joint letters (a,b) incl. identity operations, factorized future law), typedIndepProductMinimalEquiv [(h1,h2)] -> ([h1],[h2]) preserving cut types, fibrewise typedIndepProductFibreEquiv Z12,(x1,x2) = Z1,x1 x Z2,x2, branch laws factor; untyped indepProductMinimalEquiv kept. (4) A12^hist = A1^hist (x) A2^hist with the product contextual test family itself: SpanContextClosed relaxes ContextClosed, null set of C = null set of span C, productContexts of span-closed families is span-closed (so composites ITERATE), compositeSpanEquiv [r1(x)r2] -> [r1](x)[r2]; generatedCompositeEquiv justifies the composite raw algebra: for represented matrix algebras R1 <= M_n, R2 <= M_m the algebra generated by a(x)I, I(x)b is R1 (x) R2 (Kronecker realization injective over a field). (5) eq:supp-grand-gram-product: compositeSpanMoment (and wordGram_tensor, K12 = K1 (x)_Kron K2). (6) Coherence: interchange and reassociation squares for the A identification (spanHistoryComm/Assoc induced by Algebra.TensorProduct.comm/assoc on raw algebras; compositeSpanEquiv_comm, compositeSpanEquiv_assoc with Algebra.TensorProduct.comm/assoc and congr) and for the Z identification (minimalSwap/minimalAssoc with Equiv.prodComm/prodAssoc, untyped and typed squares; IdentityOperation.prod; composite laws invariant under interchange/reassociation, indepProduct_comm/assoc_probability), plus wordGram_tensor_comm/assoc. Packaged: grand_monoidal_composite. Renderings disclosed: the support-minimal realization is a Hilbert space with a generator set S and record-vector set rec (contexts = record matrix coefficients of history contexts); raw algebras are abstract finite-dimensional K-algebras for the composition part (justified by generatedCompositeEquiv in the matrix case); typed grammar rendered by cut-typed predictive systems rather than the full FiniteTypedEventGrammar record (ports/reads/verdicts play no role in this proposition). The negative clause (no factorization for correlated processes / diagonal grammars) is a non-assertion and needs no proof. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

(K4) for the paper's root race with its twelve directed rates: RootRaceScore.orientedRootRace_score_faithful (OrientedRoot = Fin 6 x Bool) and the general-index lemmas winnerProb_smul (winner law invariant under common rescaling c != 0) and rates_eq_of_winner_waiting / rate_eq_winner_div_waiting (winner law plus mean waiting time 1/K recover every rate, so the waiting-time scale separates the common-rate direction); calibration_fibre_anchor_bank itself also records the Fin 6 version with the exponential mean 1/K. Disclosed renderings: 'anchor bank' = linear map to R^k, 'determines one calibration' = injectivity on the direction space; U assumed injective (d = rank U; only Im U enters the quotient). |
| `lem:supp-pair-completion` | lemma | Pair-homogeneous completion | [`RenewalGeometry.dimension_pairwise_completion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/DimensionPairwise.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FutureMinimalSerialCell.dimension_pairwise_completion_exact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/DimensionPairwiseCompletionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FutureMinimalSerialCell.boundary_is_oriented_edge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/DimensionPairwiseCompletionExact.lean) | MinimalFaithfulRelationBranch.skeleton_complete derives lem:supp-pair-completion from the encoded assumption ass:supp-relational-branch: the off-diagonal pair of any letter (exists_offDiag_relation, from source != target and the nonempty alphabet), transitivity of the symmetry group on unordered distinct pairs and invariance of the relation set put every unordered pair of distinct endpoints in the relation set, i.e. the colour-free skeleton is K_N (via dimension_pairwise_completion). dimension_pairwise_completion_exact adds the +1/-1 oriented-edge incidence of each relation (boundary_is_oriented_edge) and that future-minimality only identifies parallel labels, not endpoint pairs. |
