| Signed-edge realization of C_1(K_N) = Lambda^2 R^N: edgeSpace N = antisymmetric N x N real matrices (a Submodule), wedge x y = x y^T - y x^T, boundary = column sums so that boundary (e_i ^ e_j) = e_j - e_i exactly as in the paper (boundary_wedge_single); W_N = meanZero N = ker of the coordinate sum; cutSpace N = image of W_N under w \|-> u0 ^ w; cycleSpace N = Lambda^2 W_N := span of wedges of two mean-zero vectors. For N >= 1, cut_cycle_decomposition proves: cutSpace inf cycleSpace = bot and cutSpace sup cycleSpace = edgeSpace (eq:supp-cut-cycle), the two summands orthogonal for the Frobenius edge pairing; boundary(u0 ^ w) = sqrt N w on W_N; Ker d = edgeSpace inf ker boundary = Lambda^2 W_N (edgeSpace_inf_ker_boundary, by the explicit expansion A = 1/2 sum A_ij (e_i - e_0)^(e_j - e_0)); Ran d = W_N; u0 ^ . injective on W_N (u0 ^ W_N iso W_N, finrank N-1). Dimensions DERIVED from the objects: finrank W_N = N-1 (rank-nullity of the sum functional), finrank edgeSpace = C(N,2) (linear equivalence with functions on ordered pairs i<j, Fintype.card_product_filter_lt), finrank Ran d = N-1, finrank Ker d = C(N-1,2) (rank-nullity of d restricted to the edge space), and finrank Lambda^2 W_N = finrank of the Mathlib exterior square of W_N. Scoped: N >= 1 (u0 requires it). Non-vacuity example: finrank cycleSpace 4 = 3. |

k_four`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4SelectorFromFaithfulGrams.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.K4SelectorFaithful.exists_faithfulGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4SelectorFromFaithfulGrams.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.K4SelectorFaithful.perm_transitive_offDiag`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4SelectorFromFaithfulGrams.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CutCycleExterior.finrank_cycleSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/CutCycleExteriorFinrank.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CutCycleExterior.finrank_meanZero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/CutCycleExteriorFinrank.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CutCycleExterior.finrank_edgeSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/CutCycleExteriorFinrank.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CutCycleExterior.edgeSpace_inf_ker_boundary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/CutCycleExteriorFinrank.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.dimension_pairwise_completion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/DimensionPairwise.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.dimension_K4_selector`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/DimensionK4Selector.lean) | k4_selector_of_faithful_grams derives the conclusion from the hypotheses themselves: ass:supp-relational-branch (relation set R on Fin N, nonempty off-diagonal, invariant under a group Gamma transitive on unordered pairs) gives G_rel = K_N (dimension_pairwise_completion); (D1) a nondegenerate alternating LinearMap.BilinForm on R t (+) W_N = R x meanZero N, whose finrank is N (finrank_temporalSpatial, from finrank W_N = N-1), forces N even (even_finrank_of_isAlt_nondegenerate: Gram matrix in a basis is skew with nonzero det) and N >= 1; (D2) faithful = positive definite Grams G0 on W_N (= Ran d, the endpoint source space) and G1 on C_1(K_N) (edgeSpace); their ranks gramRank = finrank of the range are DERIVED: r0 = N-1, r1 = C(N,2) (gramRank_endpoint/_edge via thm:supp-cut-cycle finrank lemmas); (D3) a nonzero C in Ker d = Lambda^2 W_N forces N >= 3 since finrank Ker d = C(N-1,2); (D4) Pareto-minimality (no admissible N with faithful Grams whose rank pair is strictly smaller in the product order -- weaker than minimum of any cost strictly increasing in both ranks) over Admissible N (D1 and D3 hold at N). N = 4 is shown admissible by an explicit symplectic form (admissible_four), hence N = 4, r0 = 3, r1 = 6, skeleton complete, finrank W_4 = 3. k4_selector_hypotheses_satisfiable: the full packet (S_4 transitive on pairs, symplectic form, faithful Grams, nonzero cycle, Pareto-minimality at N = 4) is satisfiable. Scoped: the endpoint Gram is placed on the endpoint source space W_N = Ran d; admissibility quantifies over simplex sizes N (the declared admissible simplex cells). |

Geometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.k4TwistedHodgeMatrixReal_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.k4TwistedExteriorSquare_real_intertwiner_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsK4TwistedIsometry`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.isK4TwistedIsometry_smul_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.k4TwistedExteriorSquare_isometric_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.k4TwistedExteriorSquare_isometric_intertwiner_unique_up_to_sign`](../../RenewalGeometry/Dimension/K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean) | Proved. Clause 1 (existence iff N = 4): in ambient centred coefficient coordinates every S_N-equivariant tensor Lambda^2 W_N -> W_N (x) sgn vanishes for N != 4 (standardTwistedExteriorTensor_zero_of_ne_four; Lambda^2 W_N != 0 for N >= 3 so no equivariant isomorphism exists), and at N = 4 the explicit integral Hodge matrix is an equivariant isomorphism (k4TwistedHodgeMatrix_intertwines, det = -16). Clause 2 (one-dimensional at N = 4): k4TwistedExteriorSquare_intertwiner_unique over C, now also over R (k4TwistedExteriorSquare_real_intertwiner_unique, transported through Complex.ofRealHom). Clause 3 (new, K4TwistedExteriorSquareIsometry.lean): with the standard invariant Euclidean forms -- the Gram matrix <f_i,f_j> = delta_ij + 1 of W_4 in the basis f_i = e_i - e_3 (k4StandardGram_apply) and the induced form <u^v,u'^v'> = <u,u'><v,v'> - <u,v'><v,u'> on the wedge basis of Lambda^2 W_4 (k4ExteriorSquareGram_eq_induced), both verified S_4-invariant on the generators -- the Hodge matrix satisfies H^T G_W H = 4 G_{Lambda^2} (k4TwistedHodgeMatrixReal_gram), so a real equivariant map B is an isometry B^T G_W B = G_{Lambda^2} iff B = +-(1/2) H (k4TwistedExteriorSquare_isometric_iff); the bundled statement k4TwistedExteriorSquare_isometric_intertwiner_unique_up_to_sign exhibits the nonsingular isometric equivariant identification B_0 = (1/2) H and shows every isometric equivariant map is +-B_0. Scoped: S_4-equivariance is tested on the generating transposition (01) and four-cycle (0123) in the explicit coordinates of K4CutCycleIsotypicSchur.lean / K4TwistedExteriorSquareMultiplicity.lean; the N != 4 vanishing is over C (which implies the real statement). |

| Proved for an ARBITRARY finite index type iota (DecidableEq) with positive rates k, race law P(A=a, T in dt) = k_a e^{-Kt} dt (raceDensity, raceExpectation; raceExpectation_one: probability law; raceDensity_factor: density = p_a * K e^{-Kt}, i.e. winner independent of T ~ Exp(K); winner probability k_b/K, E T = 1/K, E T^2 = 2/K^2). hasDerivAt_log_raceDensity: s_b = 1_{A=b} - k_b T is exactly the derivative of log(k_A e^{-KT}) in log k_b (the log-rate score). scoreGram_eq: E[s_b s_c] = delta_bc k_b/K (eq:supp-race-score-gram), scoreGram_posDef: diagonal positive definite. winnerCovarianceGram_eq: E[(1_{A=b}-p_b)(1_{A=c}-p_c)] = (diag(p) - p p^T)_{bc}; winnerCovariance_mulVec_one / _det_eq_zero / _not_posDef: singular in the all-ones direction; winnerProb_smul: invariant under common rescaling (scale loss), while rate_eq_winner_div_waiting / rates_eq_of_winner_waiting recover k_b = P(A=b)/E T (faithful), exists_rates_ne_winnerProb_eq: winner law alone is not faithful. orientedRootRace_score_faithful instantiates everything at the twelve oriented roots OrientedRoot = Fin 6 x Bool (orientedRootVector (a,true)=r_a, (a,false)=-r_a; card_orientedRoot = 12). Rendering: expectations are computed against the explicit race density (the joint law of independent exponential clocks), not via an abstract probability space. |
| `thm:supp-adm-frame` | theorem | Complete finite ADM frame | [`RenewalGeometry.adm_frame_complete`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ADMFrameUniqueness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.adm_inversion_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ADMFrameUniqueness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.relational_scheduler_ADM`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalSchedulerADM.lean) | adm_frame_complete bundles: (A1) the six root dyadics r_a r_a^T are a basis of Sym_3 (rate sums <-> B_h); (A2) the rate-difference map is onto R^3 with kernel exactly the span of the three tetrahedral face cycles, of dimension 3; existence: the boxed formula g = rho^{2/3}(det B)^{1/3} B^{-1}, N = rho^{1/3}(det B)^{1/6} satisfies sqrt(det g) = rho and N^2 g^{-1} = B; uniqueness (adm_inversion_unique, new): every N > 0, g with det g > 0 solving B = N^2 g^{-1}, rho = sqrt(det g) equals the boxed pair (determinants give N^6 = rho^2 det B, positivity fixes N, then g = N^2 B^{-1}). Scoped: 'B succ 0' is used only through det B > 0 (the positive-definite hypothesis implies it); positivity of g is used through det g > 0. |
| `lem:supp-positive-krein-distinct` | lemma | Positive geometry and Krein signature are distinct layers | [`RenewalGeometry.positive_krein_distinct_layers`](../../RenewalGeometry/Krein/PositiveKreinDistinct.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.wordGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Krein/PositiveKreinDistinct.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.wordGram_positive`](../../RenewalGeometry/Krein/PositiveKreinDistinct.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.wordGram_adjoin_positive`](../../RenewalGeometry/Krein/PositiveKreinDistinct.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rootBracket_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Krein/PositiveKreinDistinct.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.eq_one_of_involution_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Krein/PositiveKreinDistinct.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.kreinForm_indefinite`](../../RenewalGeometry/Krein/PositiveKreinDistinct.lean)<br>[`NCG.IsFundamentalSymmetry.eq_one_of_krein_nonneg`](../../NCG/Krein/FundamentalSymmetry.lean) | positive_krein_distinct_layers: for a positive functional tau (tau(a* a) >= 0 in real part) on a complex *-algebra, the word Gram K(v,w) = tau(v* w) of any word family with the orientation record theta adjoined (index type iota + Unit) is positive semidefinite and its old block is unchanged (wordGram_adjoin_positive, via sum conj(c_v) c_w K(v,w) = tau(a* a)); every positive combination sum c_a r_a r_a^T of the A_3 root dyadics a3SchedulerRoot has no negative direction (its quadratic form is sum c_a (r_a.v)^2) and, if it is an involution, it is the identity (eq_one_of_involution_nonneg) - so it cannot replace the Krein involution; the Krein form <u, Ju> is positive on the +1 eigenspace and negative on the -1 eigenspace (indefinite when both are nonzero), and a positive Krein form forces J = 1. Scoped: the word-Gram hierarchy is modelled abstractly as the Gram of a word family for a positive functional (the paper's proof), not tied to a specific finite history algebra. |

| Proved. supp_signature_minimality_with_floor (Krein/SignatureMinimalityOrientationFloor.lean) bundles (a) the independence premise, now a theorem rather than a hypothesis: for every finite typed operational-predictive model M over any anchored grammar (Predictive/OperationalEntranceCategory.lean), reversing the protected orientation keeps occurrence, complete predictive law and calibration, is an anchored separating pair for the orientation row, and stays separating under every common conservative refinement (the orientation separating pair of the proof of thm:supp-relative-primitive-floor, proved directly as separating_reverseOrientation + refinement_preserves_separation; the rest of that open record, in particular its R1 sufficiency content, is not used); (b) the positive packet has no negative direction (quadForm D >= 0, negInertia D = 0); (c) the one-line signed extension (def:main-signed-extension: negative-definite line form c < 0 adjoined orthogonally to the positive spatial form D on W_4 = Fin 3) has c = -a^2 for a unique a > 0, q(tau t + w) = -a^2 tau^2 + g(w,w), inertia (1,3,0), (3,1,0) for -q, and Sylvester invariance under every invertible change of basis (supp_signature_minimality, unchanged). No gamma matrices are used. Non-vacuity example with c = -1, D = 1 included. thm:supp-relative-primitive-floor itself is conditional_interface (open R1 sufficiency), but the theorem uses only the orientation separating pair, which is proved here directly. Rendering (disclosed here): the real positive spatial form g is encoded as a positive definite complex Hermitian 3x3 matrix D (real symmetric g is the special case), inertia is posInertia/negInertia/nullInertia of SignedHaynsworthInertia, and Sylvester invariance is proved for every invertible complex change of basis, which contains every real one. |

Disclosed: inertia is expressed by this explicit Sylvester normal form rather than through posInertia/negInertia of SignedHaynsworthInertia.lean; 'g succ 0' is used as positive definiteness of g^{-1}. |

>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.ProximalChart.actionDrop_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.ProximalChart.coupling_bregman_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.ProximalChart.coupling_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.ProximalChart.coupling_envelope_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.ProximalChart.coupling_stationarityForm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.ProximalChart.coupling_physical_test_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean) | chart_action_stationarity proves the theorem on the compact convex chart with all hypotheses on the chart. Data (ProximalChart = the manuscript's hypotheses eq:main-fisher-metric, eq:main-action-hessian, nothing else): compact convex Theta, open U ⊇ Theta carrying gradients gPsi, gA of Psi, A; Hessians HPsi, HA (derivatives of the gradients) at every point of Theta with the Loewner bounds m\|v\|^2 <= <HPsi v,v> <= M\|v\|^2, -lam<HPsi v,v> <= <HA v,v> <= L<HPsi v,v> on Theta, lam, L >= 0, eta > 0, eta*lam < 1. All gradient-level inequalities are DERIVED (HessianLoewnerGradientBounds: mean-value bounds, Schwarz symmetry of the Hessians, polarization operator-norm bound, Lipschitz on the convex chart), as are mu_Phi-strong convexity and Lambda_Phi-smoothness of Phi_theta on Theta. y*_theta enters through its definition IsMinOn Phi_theta Theta (existence exists_isMinOn_proxΦ and uniqueness eq_of_isMinOn_proxΦ are proved; canonical choice argminProx, chart_action_stationarity_argmin); interiority is the paper's hypothesis y*_theta ∈ interior Theta for nu-a.e. / every supported source (chart_action_stationarity_support uses theta ∈ supp nu literally) and grad Phi(y*) = 0 is derived (gradΦ_eq_zero). The kernel hypothesis is the paper's: the lazy kernel (1-th) I + th K^acc, 0 < th <= 1 state independent, has stationary law nu (lazyKernel ∘ₘ nu = nu); nu K^acc = nu is DERIVED (comp_eq_of_lazyKernel_comp_eq); nu is supported in Theta. Conclusions: nu K^acc = nu; eq:supp-bregman-bound int D_Psi d(nu⊗K) <= eta Dbar; eq:supp-displacement-bound int \|y-theta\|^2 <= (2eta/m) Dbar; eq:supp-stationarity-bound int S dnu <= C Dbar with the paper's constant C = stationarityConstant = 4 Lambda_Phi^2/(m mu_Phi) + 4M^2(1+eta l)^2/(m^2 eta) (C₁ + C₂, eq:supp-stationarity-constant), where S(theta) = <grad A, G^{-1} grad A> is the actual inverse-Fisher form (stationarityForm, G^{-1} the inverse operator, G^{-1} ⪯ m^{-1} I proved in inverse_form_bounds, finite-dimensional E); and Dbar = 0 forces grad A = 0 nu-a.e. (thm:main-action-stationarity). The proof is the paper's (no descent lemma): \|y-y*\|^2 <= 2Delta/mu_Phi, \|grad Phi(y)\|^2 <= Lambda_Phi^2 \|y-y*\|^2, reconstruction of eta grad A(theta). Integrability of A, D_Psi, \|y-theta\|^2, \|grad A\|^2 is derived from compactness. Standing technical assumptions (disclosed): the gap integrand is (nu⊗K)-integrable (Dbar is a number) and S is a.e.-strongly measurable. Non-vacuity: exampleChart (Theta = [-1,1] ⊂ R, Psi = A = x^2/2, eta = 1/2, L = 1) with an example showing the proximal minimizer 2theta/3 is interior. Supersedes the whole-space AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact versions. |

onStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.ProximalChart.C₂`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.stationarityForm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChartStationarity.inverse_form_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChartActionStationarityExact.lean) | chart_nonstationary_action_gap: on the compact convex chart of thm:supp-action-stationarity (ProximalChart, hypotheses only on Theta, see that record), for any finite coupling pi supported in Theta x Theta with source alpha = pi∘fst^{-1}, target beta = pi∘snd^{-1}, y*_theta minimizing Phi_theta over Theta and interior for pi-a.e. source (no invariant law): (1) E_pi D_Psi(y,theta) <= eta(Dbar_pi + d_A); (2) E_pi \|y-theta\|^2 <= (2eta/m)(Dbar_pi + d_A); (3) E_alpha S <= (C1 + C2) Dbar_pi + C2 d_A with the displayed constants C1 = 4 Lambda_Phi^2/(m mu_Phi), C2 = 4M^2(1+eta l)^2/(m^2 eta) and S the inverse-Fisher form; (4) the same with (d_A)_+; (5) eq:supp-nonstationary-test: for every represented test lift v with budget <v, G v> <= B^2 (alpha-a.e., G = Fisher metric HPsi), E_alpha \|DA[v]\|^2 <= B^2[(C1+C2) Dbar_pi + C2 (d_A)_+]; (6) equal marginals give d_A = 0. The pointwise gradient argument (envelope_le: m^{-1}\|grad A(theta)\|^2 <= C1 Delta + (2(1+eta l)^2 M^2/(m eta^2)) \|y-theta\|^2) does not use stationarity, exactly as in the paper. Integrability of A under both marginals, of D_Psi, \|y-theta\|^2 and \|grad A\|^2 is derived from compactness; disclosed technical assumptions: gap integrable, S (resp. the test pairing) a.e.-strongly measurable. |

<= B^2 with G = HPsi the Fisher metric; chart_physical_test_stationarity_support states the budget literally as eq:main-test-lift-budget (sup over theta ∈ supp nu, via Measure.support_mem_ae) together | `cor:supp-gibbs-gap` | corollary | Finite Gibbs/KL certificate | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGibbsSimplex.finite_gibbs_simplex_gap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/FiniteGibbsSimplexGap.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGibbsSimplex.entropyProximalObjective_gap_of_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/FiniteGibbsSimplexGap.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGibbsSimplex.mul_log_ratio_gibbsRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/FiniteGibbsSimplexGap.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gibbsRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/FiniteGibbsActionGap.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gibbsPartition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/FiniteGibbsActionGap.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finiteKL`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/FiniteGibbsActionGap.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.entropyProximalObjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/FiniteGibbsActionGap.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gibbsRow_probability`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/FiniteGibbsActionGap.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AcceptedActionInformationPythagoras.finiteKL_nonneg_eq_iff_of_nonnegative`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/AcceptedActionInformationPythagoras.lean) | finite_gibbs_simplex_gap: for a finite reference row p > 0 (normalisation not needed), any cost c and eta > 0, q* = gibbsRow p c eta (eq:main-gibbs-row) is a strictly positive probability row; for EVERY probability row q >= 0 (zero entries allowed, 0 log 0 = 0 as implemented by finiteKL) the row variational gap is exactly Obj(q) - Obj(q*) = eta^{-1} D_KL(q \|\| q*) (eq:supp-gibbs-gap, Obj(q) = sum q c + eta^{-1} D_KL(q\|\|p)), Obj(q*) <= Obj(q) with equality iff q = q*, and q is a minimizer of Obj over the simplex iff q = q* (q* is the unique entropy-proximal row). The key identity entropyProximalObjective_gap_of_nonneg (Obj(q) = eta^{-1} KL(q\|\|q*) - eta^{-1} log Z for q >= 0) is proved termwise, terms with q(y) = 0 vanishing on both sides; Gibbs' inequality with equality case for q >= 0, q* > 0 is finiteKL_nonneg_eq_iff_of_nonnegative. Applied with q = K^acc(.\|theta) this is the corollary. Non-vacuity example on Fin 2 with a zero-entry competitor row. |
| `prop:supp-restricted-classification` | proposition | Classification in the prescribed incidence and degree class | [`RenewalGeometry.lorentz_natural_pairing_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairing_coefficients_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gravitational_score_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.inducedBivectorAction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsProperLorentz`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean) | Proved in the same finite-dimensional model as thm:main-gravitational-classification (this proposition is its core classification clause, stated within def:main-gravitational-class). An element of the class is modelled pointwise as a real bilinear pairing A of the coframe bivector with the curvature (the bidegree-(2,1) part, carrier Fin 2 × Fin 3) plus an alternating coframe four-form ω (the degree-four part). Lorentz naturality = invariance of A under the induced bivector action of every proper orthochronous Lorentz matrix. lorentz_natural_pairing_classification: A = a·metricPairing + b·epsilonPairing, i.e. a·L_Holst + b·L_Palatini with unique coefficients; alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det: ω = λ·det = λ·L_vol; gravitational_score_classification packages the unique triple. The connection enters only through R, so nothing about torsion is assumed: the classification holds before torsion is set to zero, as the statement says. Scoped modelling disclosed: 'scalar multiplicity commutant' and 'represented determinant volume' are the model's assumptions; comparison modulo exact terms is not modelled at the pointwise algebraic level. |
| `lem:supp-variation-transfer` | lemma | Transfer of a classified variational representative | [`RenewalGeometry.classifiedVariationRemainder`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.completeVariation_eq_classified_add_remainder`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.classifiedVariation_limit_eq_zero_of_complete`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ae_tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified_subseq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ae_classifiedVariation_limit_eq_zero_of_complete_subseq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/VariationTransferExact.lean) | Proved. The complete first variation on a test is written as the classified representative plus the remainder r_X of eq:main-action-variation-remainder (classifiedVariationRemainder, completeVariation_eq_classified_add_remainder). On any index filter (the cutoff sequence or a realized subsequence): if the classified Holst/Palatini/volume variations converge on the test, the three calibrated coefficients alpha_X, beta_X, lambda_X converge, and r_X -> 0 (eq:main-first-variation-reduction), then the complete variation converges to the limiting classified variation a L_H + b L_P + c L_V (tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified); if the complete variation tends to zero, the limiting classified variation vanishes (classifiedVariation_limit_eq_zero_of_complete, tendsto_nhds_unique). Stochastic clause: on one probability space carrying all cutoff records, along one common strictly increasing realized subsequence phi of cutoffs on which the hypotheses hold almost surely for every determining test, both conclusions hold almost surely for every test (ae_tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified_subseq, ae_classifiedVariation_limit_eq_zero_of_complete_subseq); the deterministic transfer is applied pointwise after the common subsequence is selected, no independent subsequences are combined. Scoped: the coefficients are deterministic sequences in the cutoff (as in eq:main-phv-coefficients); the tests are an arbitrary index type. Rendering note: coefficient convergence (alpha_X, beta_X, lambda_X) -> (a, b, c) is taken from the lemma's proof / standing assumption (E3) of the main Einstein theorem; the manuscript's statement leaves it implicit. |

iationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniHolstVariation.firstVarDensity_eq_sum_basis`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniHolstConnectionVariationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniHolstVariation.eulerResidual_eq_covD`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniHolstConnectionVariationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.torsion_eq_zero_of_euler`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.norm_torsion_le_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.norm_torsion_le_residual'`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.eulerResidual_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.eulerResidual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.covD_palatini_bivector`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.covD_bivectorField`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.cartan_injective_of_isUnit_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.inverseC_palatiniC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.palatiniC_inverseC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.star_star`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.star_act_comm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.palatiniC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.inverseC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.starC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.torsion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.cartan`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.covD`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.inverseApplyCLM`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PalatiniTorsionModel.IsLorentz`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CartanInjectivity.cartan_injective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/CartanInjectivityExact.lean) | Closed. New file PalatiniHolstConnectionVariationExact (on top of the coefficient model PalatiniTorsionCoefficientModelExact, same conventions: pd, act, starC, palatiniC, leviCivita) derives the Euler equation from the action instead of assuming it. Action: lagrangian = (1/4) eps^{mu nu rho sigma} eta_A (P_{a,b}B)^{AB}_{mu nu} R^A_{B rho sigma} + lam det e, with curvature R = d omega + omega^omega (quadratic in omega); in the model's star convention (eps^{0123}=1, eps_{0123}=-1) <B,R> is L_Holst and <*B,R> is L_Palatini, so this is alpha L_Holst + beta L_Palatini + lambda L_vol (eq:main-phv-densities/-coefficients). relAction = int_U (L(omega+t delta) - L(omega)) over an open chart domain U; IsLorentzVariation = smooth, compactly supported, tsupport in U, so(1,3)-valued at every point; IsConnectionStationary = deriv of relAction at t=0 vanishes for every such delta. Proved: exact expansion R(omega+t delta) = R + t D_omega delta + t^2 delta^delta (curvArr_add_smul, lagrangian_add_smul), so the relative action is the quadratic t*int V + t^2*int Q (relAction_eq, deriv_relAction); integration by parts on R^4 from Mathlib integral_mul_fderiv_eq_neg_fderiv_mul_of_integrable (integral_pd_mul); along delta = psi*c the first variation is (1/4) int psi E_c (integral_firstVarDensity_smul_const); for Lorentz omega E_c = -2 sum eta_A c^A_{B sigma} Zf^{AB}_sigma and sum eps D(PB) = 3 Zf (eulerDensity_eq_Zf, sum_eps_covD); fundamental lemma on open U (Mathlib IsOpen.ae_eq_zero_of_integral_contDiff_smul_eq_zero + continuity) with elementary Lorentz test coefficients testCoeff; eps-contraction injective on 3-forms (eq_zero_of_eps_contract) with the total antisymmetry of D(PB). Main: eulerResidual_eq_zero_of_isConnectionStationary (stationarity => D(P_{a,b}B) = 0 on U), converse isConnectionStationary_of_eulerResidual_eq_zero (via basis decomposition of general variations), torsion_eq_zero_of_isConnectionStationary ((a,b) != (0,0), det e != 0 on U => T = 0 on U), and the packaged palatini_torsion_exact (inverse formula both sides, stationarity <-> Euler equation, Cartan injectivity for nondegenerate coframes, stationarity => T = 0, finite-cutoff estimate \|\|T\|\| <= kappa^{-1} \|\|P^{-1}\|\| \|\|D(PB)\|\| from norm_torsion_le_residual'). Rendering (disclosed): chart = R^4 with C^1 coframe and C^1 connection on it, connection Lorentz everywhere, action varied on an arbitrary open chart domain U with variations compactly supported in U; only action differences are integrated (no integrability of L itself needed). Non-vacuity: identity coframe with zero connection is stationary on U = univ (via the converse) and satisfies the conclusions; nonzero admissible variations psi*testCoeff exist for every bump psi. Sorry-free, standard axioms only (checked with #print axioms), no native_decide. |
| `lem:supp-half-volume-sobolev` | lemma | Cut inequality implies the half-volume $L^1$ Sobolev bound | [`RenewalGeometry.finite_coarea_sobolev_three_halves`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weightedLThreeHalves`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finitePositiveSupport`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean) | For g >= 0 and the cut inequality I mu(A)^{2/3} <= h cap(A) on every subset A of supp g, I \|\|g\|\|_{L^{3/2}(mu)} <= (h/2) sum_u sum_v c_uv \|g u - g v\| (ordered pairs, i.e. h times the sum over edges), via the layer-cake expansion, weighted Minkowski at exponent 3/2 and the finite coarea identity (finite_coarea_sobolev_three_halves). Rendering: the Lean hypothesis is implied by the paper's (eq:supp-cut-isoperimetry on half-volume sets plus mu(supp g) <= mu(V)/2), because every subset of supp g is then a half-volume set and mu(A) = 0 is trivial; the Lean statement is therefore more general. |
| `lem:supp-l6-poincare` | lemma | Discrete $L^6$ Sobolev and Poincar\'e inequalities | [`RenewalGeometry.finite_meanZero_L6_sobolev`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finite_meanZero_poincare`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finite_meanZero_eigenvalue_floor`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finite_half_support_L6_sobolev`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finite_weightedMedian_centered_L6_sobolev`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.positiveEigenvalue_floor`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylFiniteGraph.lean) | With the cut inequality I·min(μ(A), μ(A^c))^{2/3} ≤ h·cap(A), the degree bound h^2 Σ_u c_uv ≤ D μ_v and μ(V) ≤ V*, every mean-zero f satisfies Σ μ f^6 ≤ ((128D/I^2) E(f))^3 (i.e. ‖f‖_6^2 ≤ C_S E), Σ μ f^2 ≤ V*^{2/3} C_S E(f), and every positive eigenvalue of the weighted Laplacian is ≥ C_P^{-1}; the half-support constant 32D/I^2 and the weighted-median centering step are separate lemmas. Rendering: the hypothesis I*min(mu(A), mu(A^c))^{2/3} <= h cap(A) for all A is equivalent to eq:supp-cut-isoperimetry on half-volume sets (complement case by cap(A) = cap(A^c) for symmetric c; derivation in spatialCutMargin_of_le). positiveEigenvalue_floor assumes D, V* > 0 (D > 0 is forced on nontrivial graphs, degreeConstant_pos). |
| `lem:supp-nash-counting` | lemma | Nash inequality, ultracontractivity, and spectral counting | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.meanZeroHeat_ultracontractive`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.abs_meanZeroHeatKernel_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.meanZeroHeatKernel_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.meanZeroHeat_eq_kernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.meanZeroHeat`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.meanZeroProjection`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.meanZeroHeatKernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.degreeConstant_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.twoVertexGraph`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylUltracontractive.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finite_nash_from_sobolev`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHeatColumn.l2_decay`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteSpectralHeatCertificate.heatTrace_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finite_weyl_count_from_heatTrace`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.positiveHeatTrace_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylFiniteGraph.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.eigenvalueCount_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylFiniteGraph.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.positiveHeatColumn_l1_le_two`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/OperationalSobolevWeylFiniteGraph.lean) | All three conclusions are now formalised on the actual weighted-graph objects under the hypotheses of lem:supp-l6-poincare (cut inequality with constant I, degree bound h^2 deg <= D m, volume <= V*; C_S = 128 D/I^2). (1) eq:supp-nash: finite_nash_from_sobolev with finite_meanZero_L6_sobolev. (2) NEW eq:supp-ultracontractive: T_X(t) = e^{-t Delta} P_0 is defined concretely as meanZeroHeat t f = heatApply t (P_0 f) with P_0 f = f - <f>_mu (meanZeroProjection); meanZeroHeat_eq_kernel proves T(t)f(u) = Sum_v mu(v) K0_t(u,v) f(v) with the mu-kernel K0_t(u,v) = Sum_{lambda_j>0} e^{-t lambda_j} phi_j(u) phi_j(v) (heat kernel = mu(v)(K0 + 1/V), stationary part killed by P_0); meanZeroHeatKernel_sq_le is the semigroup/self-adjointness identity K0_t(u,v) = <col_u(t/2), col_v(t/2)>_mu in spectral form plus Cauchy-Schwarz; abs_meanZeroHeatKernel_le combines it with the proved Nash column decay \|\|col_v(t/2)\|\|^2 <= 4(3C_S/(2t))^{3/2} (FiniteHeatColumn.l2_decay on positiveHeatColumnCertificate, l1 <= 2 by positiveHeatColumn_l1_le_two) to get sup_{u,v}\|K0_t\| <= 4(3C_S/(2t))^{3/2}; meanZeroHeat_ultracontractive: \|T_X(t)f(u)\| <= 4(3C_S/(2t))^{3/2} Sum_v mu(v)\|f(v)\| for all f, u, t > 0, i.e. \|\|T_X(t)\|\|_{L1(mu) -> Linf} <= 4(3C_S/(2t))^{3/2} (subsingleton graphs handled separately; D > 0 derived on nontrivial graphs, degreeConstant_pos). (3) eq:supp-global-weyl-count: eigenvalueCount_bound N(R) <= 1 + 4e^{3/2} V* (C_S R)^{3/2} via positiveHeatTrace_bound. Non-vacuity: the hypotheses are verified on the nontrivial two-vertex graph (twoVertexGraph, I = D = h = 1, V* = 2) in an example. |
| `thm:supp-spatial-screen` | theorem | Operational Sobolev, Poincar\'e, and compact-screen bounds | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.setMass`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.IsHalfVolumeCut`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutRatio`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutConstant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.efficientCutConstant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.spatialCutConstant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutConstant_le_cutRatio`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.exists_minimizing_cut`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutConstant_comparison`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.spatialCutMargin_of_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.spatialCutConstant_ge_of_efficient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.renewalSpatialPositiveScreen_of_efficientCut`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.cutDemand`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.neckPotential`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.neck_witness`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.neck_witness_of_minimizing_cut`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.tendsto_inv_atTop_of_tendsto_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.renewalSpatialPositiveScreen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen.lean) | Proved on a finite weighted graph (masses m_v > 0, symmetric nonnegative conductances c, cell scale h > 0, efficient capacities u). The cut constants of eq:main-cut-constant are encoded as cutConstant w = sInf of the finite family of half-volume cut ratios w(∂A)/μ(A)^{2/3} (efficientCutConstant u = I^eff, spatialCutConstant h = I^sp with weights h c; the infimum is attained, exists_minimizing_cut, and equals 0 when no half-volume cut exists). cutConstant_comparison derives a₋ I^sp ≤ I^eff ≤ a₊ I^sp (eq:main-cut-comparison) from the edgewise comparison a₋ h c ≤ u ≤ a₊ h c; spatialCutConstant_ge_of_efficient gives I^sp ≥ I_*/a₊ from I^eff ≥ I_*; spatialCutMargin_of_le converts I^sp ≥ I into the two-sided cut margin I·min(μ(A), μ(Aᶜ))^{2/3} ≤ h c(∂A) for every A (eq:supp-cut-isoperimetry, complement case by cut-capacity symmetry). The main theorem renewalSpatialPositiveScreen_of_efficientCut then assembles, under μ(V) ≤ V_*, the weighted degree bound h² Σ_u c_uv ≤ D m_v, the comparison and I^eff ≥ I_* > 0, the full RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen package with constant I_*/a₊: L⁶ Sobolev (C_S = 128 D/(I_*/a₊)²), Poincaré (C_P = V_*^{2/3} C_S), positive spectral floor C_P⁻¹, Weyl count N(R) ≤ 1 + 4 e^{3/2} V_* (C_S R)^{3/2}, and the compact-screen tail Σ_{λ_j > R} \|⟨φ_j, f⟩\|² ≤ R⁻¹ 𝓔(f) (eq:supp-screen-count-tail, the spectral-coefficient form of ‖(I − P_R) f‖² ≤ R⁻¹ 𝓔(f)). Neck obstruction: neck_witness shows that for a half-volume cut of positive capacity the potential φ = 1_A/u(∂A) has ordered-pair total variation (1/2)ΣΣ u \|dφ\| = 1 and pairs with the demand b_A of eq:main-cut-demand to μ(A)^{2/3}/u(∂A); neck_witness_of_minimizing_cut gives exactly (I^eff)⁻¹ for a minimizing cut, and tendsto_inv_atTop_of_tendsto_zero sends it to +∞ along any family with I^eff → 0⁺ (eq:main-neck-obstruction). Scoped hypotheses: 0 ≤ a₋, 0 < a₊, 0 < D, V_*, h. Stated-form tail: screenTail_L2 proves \|\|(I - P_R) f\|\|^2_{L^2(mu)} <= R^{-1} E(f) literally, with (I - P_R) f = spectralHighPart (P_R = 1_[0,R](Delta)), via energy_eq_sum_eigenvalue_mul_sq (E(f) = sum_j lambda_j c_j^2) and weightedL2_spectralHighPart. spatialScreen_positiveBranch packages the comparison, I^sp >= I_*/a_+, Sobolev, Poincare, Weyl and the L^2 tail; spatialScreen_neckBranch packages eq:main-neck-obstruction along a sequence; zeroCapacity_cut_of_efficientCutConstant_eq_zero: if I^eff = 0 at finite cutoff, a minimizing half-volume cut has zero capacity (retained as disconnected support). |
| `prop:supp-compact-upgrade` | proposition | Compact positive-screen upgrade | [`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.compact_positive_screen_upgrade`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.compact_positive_screen_upgrade_L2`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.norm_bounded_of_weakTendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.tendsto_of_isCompactOperator`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.norm_inv_sqrt_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.re_inner_ge_of_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.nonneg_of_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.norm_sqrt_apply_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.weakTendsto_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.weakTendsto_of_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.weakTendsto_map`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.norm_bilinear_diag_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.tendsto_bilinear_diag`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.eLpNorm_quadratic_insertion_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactPositiveScreen.tendsto_quadratic_insertion_L1`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactPositiveScreenUpgradeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SMSTChannel.screen_strong_convergence`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/SMSTPositiveScreenExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SMSTChannel.strong_convergence_transfer`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/SMSTPositiveScreenExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SMSTChannel.WeakTendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/SMSTPositiveScreenExact.lean) | compact_positive_screen_upgrade: complex Hilbert space Y (complete), Y_X weakly convergent to Y (inner products against every vector), A_X with m I <= A_X in the Loewner order of Y->L Y (m > 0; positivity of A_X derived, nonneg_of_le), A_X^{1/2} = CFC.sqrt A_X (Mathlib continuous functional calculus), A_X^{-1/2} the two-sided inverse of CFC.sqrt A_X, A_X^{-1/2} -> T strongly (T = A^{-1/2}), Z_X = A_X^{1/2} Y_X weakly convergent to Z, screens S_{X,R} -> S_R in operator norm with S_R compact (IsCompactOperator), lim_R sup_X \|\|(I - S_{X,R})Z_X\|\| = 0 and lim_R \|\|(I-S_R)Z\|\| = 0. Conclusions: Z_X -> Z strongly, Y_X -> Y strongly, and Y = T Z. All three gaps of the audit are closed: (ii) sup_X \|\|Z_X\|\| < infinity is DERIVED from weak convergence by Banach-Steinhaus (norm_bounded_of_weakTendsto); (iii) \|\|A_X^{-1/2}\|\| <= m^{-1/2} is DERIVED from m I <= A_X via \|\|A^{1/2}v\|\|^2 = Re<Av,v> (norm_inv_sqrt_le), and the limit is identified, Y = T Z, by uniqueness of weak limits; compactness of S_R is the genuine IsCompactOperator and complete continuity is PROVED (tendsto_of_isCompactOperator: compact closure of the image of a ball, subsequence extraction, limit identified through the adjoint); (i) the quadratic clause: norm_bilinear_diag_sub_le / tendsto_bilinear_diag (\|\|B(Y_X,Y_X) - B(Y,Y)\|\| <= \|\|B\|\| \|\|Y_X - Y\|\|(\|\|Y_X\|\| + \|\|Y\|\|) for any bounded real-bilinear B), and compact_positive_screen_upgrade_L2: with Y realised in the cylinder space L^2(K;E) by a bounded map iota (e.g. the identity), every pointwise bounded real-bilinear density b gives b(Y_X,Y_X) -> b(Y,Y) in L^1(K) via the explicit Hoelder estimate \|\|b(f,f)-b(g,g)\|\|_1 <= \|\|b\|\| \|\|f-g\|\|_2(\|\|f\|\|_2+\|\|g\|\|_2) (eLpNorm_quadratic_insertion_le). Scoped: screen radii R and cutoffs X indexed by N; the upper bound A_X <= M I and the finite rank of S_{X,R} are not needed and omitted (statement more general). Non-vacuity example with a nonzero packet on Y = C. |
| `lem:supp-coframe-interpolation` | lemma | Strong interpolation for the reconstructed coframes | [`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.coframe_interpolation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.eLpNorm'_limit_le_of_tendsto_L2`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.coframe_interpolation_explicit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.coframe_strong_Lq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.eLpNorm'_bilinear_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.coframe_bivector_strong`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteMeasureL2L6Interpolation.eLpNorm'_interpolate_two_six`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/FiniteMeasureL2L6InterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StrongInterpolationConvergence.palatini_conjugate_exponent_range`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/StrongInterpolationConvergenceExact.lean) | Proved. Setting: a finite measure space (K, mu) (the compact cylinder with its finite reconstructed volume measure), a sequence of cutoffs e_n : K -> E (E a normed group; a real normed space for the bivector clause), the limit e, Mathlib eLpNorm' norms with real exponents. coframe_interpolation: from eq:supp-coframe-l2-l6 (\|\|e_n - e\|\|_2 -> 0, sup_n \|\|e_n\|\|_6 <= M_6 < infinity) one gets (i) e in L^6 with \|\|e\|\|_6 <= M_6 (eLpNorm'_limit_le_of_tendsto_L2: instead of weak compactness, convergence in measure gives an a.e.-convergent subsequence and Fatou is applied to \|e_n\|^6); (ii) eq:supp-interpolation-explicit \|\|e_n - e\|\|_q <= \|\|e_n - e\|\|_2^{theta_q} \|\|e_n - e\|\|_6^{1 - theta_q}, theta_q = 3/q - 1/2, for 2 <= q <= 6 (coframe_interpolation_explicit, from the existing Holder interpolation); (iii) eq:supp-strong-lq: \|\|e_n - e\|\|_q -> 0 for every 2 <= q < 6 (coframe_strong_Lq, using \|\|e_n - e\|\|_6 <= 2 M_6 and theta_q > 0); (iv) for p > 3/2 and p' = p/(p-1): 2 < 2p' < 6 and, for every bounded bilinear map B (the exterior product on the finite-dimensional coframe fibres), B(e_n, e_n) -> B(e, e) strongly in L^{p'} (coframe_bivector_strong: splitting B(e_n,e_n) - B(e,e) = B(e_n - e, e_n) + B(e, e_n - e), Holder with 1/p' = 1/(2p') + 1/(2p') via eLpNorm'_bilinear_le, uniform L^{2p'} bounds from the L^6 bounds on the finite measure, and (iii) at q = 2p'). Scoped hypotheses disclosed: cutoffs indexed by N (a sequence), all functions AEStronglyMeasurable, M_6 given as an extended real with M_6 < infinity. |

m t i a (internal coordinates a : Fin d of a positive coefficient inner product, complex-valued, one common trivialization), weak time derivatives G0, G (IsGridW12Rep: every grid value W^{1,2}(0,T), continuous representative), incidence curl (A m) t i j a = R - Q + E for i < j at every t in (0,T] (eq:main-connection-hodge-incidence), R, Q, E in L^2_{t,h} (IntegrableOn of the squared grid norms = finiteness of the norms), product bound l2t(Q) <= CQ * \|\|A\|\|^2_{L^4_{t,h}} (l4Sq), and the budget eq:main-connection-hodge-budget l2t(A0,A) + l2t(R) + \|\|A\|\|^2_{L^4} + l2t(eps) + l2t(delta_h A) + l2t(D^+A0) + \|\|d_t I_h(A0,A)\|\|_{L^2_t H^{-1}_x} <= C_K uniformly in m (dtNegSq: trigonometric H^{-1}(T^3) norm of the interpolated grid time derivative). Conclusion: a subsequence of the trigonometric interpolants of the full connection (fullConn, all components) converges in L^2((0,T] x T^3) (Lebesgue x normalized Haar) to limits Omega, i.e. relative compactness in L^2(K). hodge_connection_exhaustion gives ass:main-connection-compactness: if the packet (HodgeTemporalBudget) holds on each cylinder (0,T_j] x T^3 of a countable family with constants CQ_j, C_K(j), one common subsequence converges on every cylinder (diagonal extraction exists_strictMono_forall_of_diag). Proof as in the paper: exact discrete Hodge identity (hodge_identity, bridged from PeriodicHodge.periodic_discrete_hodge_identity) + incidence give the L^2_t H^1_h bound (energy_le_of_incidence); uniform norm equivalence of forward-difference and trigonometric H^1 norms (sum_tW_le); grid Aubin-Lions-Simon theorem (PeriodicGridAubinLions: per-mode W^{1,2} time coefficients, Arzela-Ascoli over countably many modes, uniform Fourier tail, Parseval on T^3 + Tonelli back to space-time L^2, completeness of L^2). Renderings disclosed: the distributional time-derivative hypothesis is encoded by grid time-derivative arrays with the W^{1,2} representation (equivalent on a fixed grid up to a.e. choice of representative, which does not affect L^2 compactness); the standard product measure is used for the 'uniformly comparable physical measures'; the identification/no-gauge-transformation clauses of the assumption hold trivially since everything is in one common trivialization. Non-vacuity: zero-connection example for HodgeTemporalBudget (C_K = 0). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-connection-hodge` | lemma | Exact periodic discrete Hodge identity | [`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicHodge.discrete_hodge_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PeriodicConnectionHodgeIdentity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicHodge.periodic_discrete_hodge_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PeriodicConnectionHodgeIdentity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicHodge.sum_inner_backwardDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PeriodicConnectionHodgeIdentity.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicHodge.hodge_algebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/PeriodicConnectionHodgeIdentity.lean) | eq:supp-discrete-hodge-identity proved exactly: sum_{i,j} \|\|D_i^+ A_j\|\|_h^2 = sum_{i<j} \|\|D_i^+ A_j - D_j^+ A_i\|\|_h^2 + \|\|sum_i D_i^- A_i\|\|_h^2, with D^+/D^- and \|\|.\|\|_h the library's LawMultiplier.forwardDiff/backwardDiff/gridNormSq, for any finite additive group G (periodic grid), any finite linearly ordered set of directions with steps e i, any h, and A with values in any real inner product space (the 'same positive inner product'; a complex coefficient space is covered via its underlying real inner product, which has the same norm). periodic_discrete_hodge_identity is the paper's (hZ/Z)^3 = PeriodicGrid N instance with unit steps. Proof is physical-space (summation by parts from translation invariance, sum_inner_backwardDiff: sum <D_i^- u, D_j^- v> = sum <D_j^+ u, D_i^+ v>), not the paper's Fourier/Parseval route; same statement. |
| `prop:supp-connection-screens` | proposition | Transported-screen version of connection compactness | [`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.connectionScreens_L2`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.transportedScreen_relativelyCompact`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.totallyBounded_range_of_screens`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.totallyBounded_of_coefficients`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TransportedScreenCompactness.compLp_sub_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/TransportedScreenCompactnessExact.lean) | Proved. connectionScreens_L2: in Lp H 2 (volume.restrict (Ioc 0 T)) with H a complete inner-product space over an RCLike field, for Omega : N -> L^2(0,T;H) with sup_X \|\|Omega_X\|\| <= B, time-independent operators P X R, P0 R : H ->L H acting pointwise in time (ContinuousLinearMap.compLp) with \|\|P X R - P0 R\|\|_op -> 0 as X -> infinity for every R, the tail condition eq:supp-connection-screen-tail in the form 'for every eps>0, eventually in R, \|\|Omega_X - P_{X,R} Omega_X\|\| < eps for all X', and P_R Omega_X = sum_i c_i(R,X) e_i(R) in a fixed finite family of vectors with each coefficient family {c_i(R,X)}_X relatively compact in L^2(0,T), the closure of {Omega_X} is compact. transportedScreen_relativelyCompact is the same statement for any measure space, any 1 <= p and any index type with the cofinite filter. Proof = the paper's: screen differences controlled by the operator norm and the uniform bound (compLp_sub_norm_le), finitely many exceptional X absorbed as a finite set, screened families totally bounded as continuous images of the compact product of coefficient closures (totallyBounded_of_coefficients), total boundedness by uniform approximation (totallyBounded_range_of_screens), compact closure by completeness. Scoped renderings: the finite family e_i(R) need not be a basis of Ran P_R and finite rank of P_{X,R}, P_R is not used (both only weaken the hypotheses); relative compactness of the coefficient family is stated coordinatewise (equivalent to relative compactness of the vector family in L^2(0,T)^n). |
| `thm:supp-curvature-compactness` | theorem | Weak curvature compactness with an identified connection limit | [`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.curvature_weak_compactness`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.isDistributionalCurvature_of_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.isDistributionalCurvature_of_finiteWriter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.FiniteWriterCertificate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.IsDistributionalCurvature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.curvaturePairing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.tendsto_curvaturePairing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LpDuality.exists_subseq_tendsto_weak_family_vec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LpDualityWeakCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LpDuality.exists_subseq_tendsto_weak`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LpDualityWeakCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LpDuality.exists_lpPairing_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LpDualityWeakCompactness.lean) | Proved in full generality 1 < p < infinity. curvature_weak_compactness: for K a compact subset of R^d (any compact set, cylinders included), Hoelder-conjugate p,q with p,q < infinity, and curvature components R_n a b : R^d -> A (A any finite-dimensional real normed algebra, e.g. a matrix Lie algebra) uniformly bounded in L^p(K), there is a subsequence and limits Rlim a b in L^p(K) with int_K h . R_(phi n) -> int_K h . Rlim for every scalar h in L^q(K) (eq:supp-curvature-reflexive-limit; for finite-dimensional A this is weak L^p(K;A) convergence), and (ii) for every omega_n -> omega strongly in L^2_loc(int K) (StrongL2LocTendsto) whose tested residual int phi R_n - <d_dist omega_n + omega_n wedge omega_n, phi> -> 0 for every phi in Mathlib's test-function space D(int K, R) (eq:supp-curvature-identification), Rlim = d omega + omega wedge omega in distributions on int K (IsDistributionalCurvature, pairing -int d_a phi omega_b + int d_b phi omega_a + int phi (omega_a omega_b - omega_b omega_a)); (iii) the same conclusion from a FiniteWriterCertificate: finite writer W_n, banks T_n with Cartan residual \|\|int Phi.(R_n - W_n)\|\| <= c_n \|\|Phi\|\|_2, c_n -> 0 (eq:main-cartan-curvature-residual), a determining core D of test functions represented in the banks with vanishing test-interpolation errors, incidence discrepancy int psi.W_n - <d omega_n + omega_n wedge omega_n, psi> -> 0 on D (eq:main-cartan-incidence), and D C^1-uniformly dense with supports in a fixed compact. Weak compactness rests on new infrastructure not in Mathlib: the Riesz representation of (L^q)* for finite measures (exists_lpPairing_eq, via the signed measure A \|-> Lambda(1_A), Radon-Nikodym and a truncation dual-norm bound) and weak sequential compactness of bounded L^p sets (exists_subseq_tendsto_weak, sequential Banach-Alaoglu in (L^q)*). Renderings: the L^p(K) bound is imposed componentwise (equivalent for finite index sets); the scalar-test normalization of the Cartan residual is implied by the paper's sup over matrix tests Phi = phi (x) a; test interpolation is encoded by bank representatives with vanishing tested-residual difference; the diagonal over a countable compact exhaustion is not formalized (single compact K). Non-vacuity: example in the file (zero curvature on the unit cube of R^4, p = q = 2). The p = 2 special case is the instance p = q = 2. Related new infrastructure also used by the open literal-link and Hodge records: RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoefficientAubinLionsCompactness.lean (Aubin-Lions-Simon in coefficient form). |
| `lem:supp-interface-lifting` | lemma | Interface lifting and its distributional error | [`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.interface_lifting_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.eLpNorm_interfaceLift_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.lintegral_interfaceLift_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.norm_integral_interfaceLift_sub_faceMeasure_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.sum_integral_norm_le_sqrt_mul_sqrt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.card_mul_pow_le_volume`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.eq_of_faceLift_ne_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.lintegral_faceLift_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.lintegral_normalLift_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.norm_integral_normalLift_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.interfaceLift`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.faceLift`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.faceMeasurePairing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.interfaceBudgetSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.IsLiftingProfile`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.exists_smooth_liftingProfile`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean) | Proved on the uniform cubical mesh of side h>0 in R^{n+1} (paper: n+1 = 4), compact-subdomain version. Faces f=(i,k) (normal direction i, integer label k) are parametrized by their tangential box; the face measure delta_f is Lebesgue (product) measure on the coordinate face (faceMeasurePairing = Sum_f int_f Phi J_f). The lifting L_f J_f(y + r e_i) = h^{-1} eta(r/h) J_f(y) (faceLift/interfaceLift, eq:supp-interface-lifting) for any profile eta >= 0 continuous, supported in [-1/4,1/4], int eta = 1 (IsLiftingProfile; a smooth one supported in (-1/4,1/4) exists: exists_smooth_liftingProfile). J_h^2 = Sum_f h^{-1}\|\|J_f\|\|^2_{L2(f)} (interfaceBudgetSq, eq:main-interface-budget). interface_lifting_bounds: for any finite face family F whose cells lie in a set U of finite volume and J_f in L^2(f) with values in any Banach space, (1) \|\|L_h J\|\|_{L2} <= C J_h with C = ((n+1)\|\|eta\|\|_2^2)^{1/2} (dimension and eta only); (2) for every bounded L-Lipschitz test Phi, \|\|<L_h J - Sum J_f delta_f, Phi>\|\| <= C_U h J_h L with C_U = (1/4)((n+1) vol U)^{1/2}. The proof follows the paper: exact integration in normal coordinates (lintegral_normalLift_sq via Mathlib's piFinSuccAbove Fubini), bounded overlap (eq_of_faceLift_ne_zero: at most one prism per normal direction contains a point, so at most n+1 overlap) + pointwise Cauchy-Schwarz, the h/4 displacement estimate (norm_integral_normalLift_sub_le), Cauchy-Schwarz Sum_f \|\|J_f\|\|_{L1(f)} <= (#F h^{n+1})^{1/2} J_h, and face counting #F h^{n+1} <= (n+1) vol U (card_mul_pow_le_volume; O_K(h^{-4}) faces of area h^3). Renderings disclosed: \|\|nabla Phi\|\|_inf is rendered as the Lipschitz constant of Phi for the sup norm on R^{n+1} (smooth compactly supported tests are Lipschitz and bounded); the periodic-domain variant is not separately formalized (the compact interior subdomain alternative of the paper is); faces are half-open so the face family is a disjoint decomposition. Non-vacuity example included (one face of the unit mesh of R^4, J = 1). |

CubicalJumpFormula.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CubicalJump.IsCellwiseC1`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalJumpFormula.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceLifting.interface_lifting_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CubicalInterfaceLifting.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DistributionalCurvature.isDistributionalCurvature_of_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/DistributionalCurvatureCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InterfaceCurvature.heavisideConnection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/InterfaceCurvatureCriterion.lean) | Proved for cellwise C^1 connections on the uniform cubical mesh of side h_m in R^{n+1} (paper: n+1 = 4; polynomial pieces are C^1), coefficients in any complete normed real algebra A (e.g. a matrix Lie algebra), on a compact K with test functions in D(int K). Setup formalized: omega_b = cellwise h (P b) (u(x) = P_{floor(x/h)}(x), finitely many nonzero pieces, IsCellwiseC1), broken derivative (d_b omega)_{ab} = d^b_a omega_b - d^b_b omega_a (brokenExteriorDeriv), face jumps J_f = n_f^flat wedge [omega]_f (connectionJump), lifted curvature R^lift = d_b omega + omega wedge omega + L_h J (liftedCurvature, eq:main-lifted-curvature). The jump formula eq:supp-connection-jumps d_dist omega = d_b omega + Sum_f J_f delta_f is PROVED (connection_jump_formula, from the Gauss-Green formula partial_jump_formula: Fubini in normal coordinates + one-dimensional integration by parts and summation by parts integral_deriv_smul_floor), for any finite face family F containing every face that meets int K. interface_curvature_criterion_cellwise: under the budget eq:supp-connection-interface-budget (componentwise \|\|d_b omega\|\|_{L2(K)} + \|\|omega_a\|\|_{L4(K)}^2 + J_h <= C, faces' cells in a fixed U of finite volume), (1) \|\|R^lift_{ab}\|\|_{L2(K)} <= C(3 + ((n+1)\|\|eta\|\|_2^2)^{1/2}) uniformly (Holder \|\|omega wedge omega\|\|_2 <= 2\|\|omega\|\|_4^2 + lem:supp-interface-lifting); (2) the discrepancy of R^lift from d_dist omega + omega wedge omega is exactly the lifting error, bounded by C_U h J_h Lip(Phi) (norm_integral_liftedCurvature_sub_curvaturePairing_le; <= C_U h C Lip(Phi) in the packaged theorem); (3) if h_m -> 0 and omega_m -> omega strongly in L^2_loc(int K), every weak L^2(K) cluster point R (limit of a subsequence tested against all g in L^2(K)) equals d omega + omega wedge omega in distributions on int K (via isDistributionalCurvature_of_tendsto, i.e. thm:supp-curvature-compactness with p = 2). interface_curvature_exists_cluster additionally gives existence of the identified weak cluster point for finite-dimensional A. The abstract version interface_curvature_criterion takes the jump formula as hypothesis HasBrokenDerivativeJumps (used by the cellwise theorem). Renderings disclosed: compact-subdomain version (not the periodic domain); 'weak cluster point' = subsequential weak limit; \|\|nabla Phi\|\|_inf = Lipschitz constant (sup norm); the budget is imposed componentwise. Non-vacuity: the zero connection for the abstract packet, and heavisideConnection (the indicator of the unit cell in the dx^1 component of a connection on R^2, a genuinely discontinuous cellwise connection whose jump across the face x^0 = 0 is 1) satisfies the jump formula through connection_jump_formula (examples in the file). |

xact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.WeakPalatiniPassage.eLpNorm'_wedge_tendsto_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/WeakPalatiniPassageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.WeakPalatiniPassage.eLpNorm_fourLinear_tendsto_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/WeakPalatiniPassageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeInterpolation.eLpNorm'_limit_le_of_tendsto_L2`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/CoframeStrongInterpolationExact.lean) | Concrete Lebesgue-space statement (checklist (m)). supp_weak_strong_palatini: on a finite measure space (alpha, mu) (the compact cylinder K with finite volume measure), with the actual coframe exterior products coframeWedge ((a wedge b)^{IJ}_{mu nu} = a^I_mu b^J_nu - a^I_nu b^J_mu, bounded bilinear on the coefficient fibre Fin 4 -> Fin 4 -> R) and coframeWedgeFour (the dx^0123 component Sum_sigma sgn sigma e1^I_{sigma0} e2^J_{sigma1} e3^K_{sigma2} e4^L_{sigma3}, bounded four-linear; nonzero: coframeWedgeFour_identity), assuming eq:supp-coframe-l2-l6 (e_X -> e in L^2(K), sup_X \|\|e_X\|\|_{L^6} <= M6, cutoffs indexed by N), p > 3/2 and eq:supp-curvature-weak literally (R_X, R elements of Lp(K) = MeasureTheory.Lp Curv p mu, and phi(R_X) -> phi(R) for every continuous linear functional phi on Lp, LpWeakTendsto): (1) eq:supp-weak-strong-pairing int_K <Phi(e_X wedge e_X), R_X> -> int_K <Phi(e wedge e), R> for every bounded measurable coefficient tensor field Phi (x -> Biv->L Biv, \|\|Phi x\|\| <= C_Phi) and bounded fibre pairing; (2)-(3) the same with an internal Hodge star (any fixed bounded linear fibre map) on the bivector factor or on the curvature factor; (4) eq:supp-volume-strong e_X^{wedge4} -> e^{wedge4} strongly in L^1(K). As in the paper's proof, the uniform L^p bound on R_X is DERIVED from weak convergence by Banach-Steinhaus (lp_norm_bounded_of_weakTendsto), the pairing R -> int <B,R> is a continuous functional on Lp by Hoelder (lpPairingCLM), and e in L^6 is DERIVED (Fatou, eLpNorm'_limit_le_of_tendsto_L2); B_X -> B in L^{p'} by the explicit interpolation of lem:supp-coframe-interpolation (proved). General versions for any bounded bilinear/four-linear pointwise product: weak_strong_pairing_tendsto, volume_strong_L1. Non-vacuity example (constant coframes and curvature on [0,1], p = 2). Scoped: compact support of Phi is replaced by boundedness on the finite-measure domain (only boundedness enters); Phi is required a.e.-strongly measurable. |
