[`RenewalGeometry.LpTendsto.bilin`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MollifierLpConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LpTendsto.memLp_of_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MollifierLpConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.AnisotropicIntegrability`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.classicalTorsion_mollify`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.classicalCurvature_mollify`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.bianchiPairing_eq_zero_of_ne`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.isDistributionalBianchi_of_isTorsionFree`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.isDistributionalBianchi_of_regulator_limit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.ae_curvature_add_swap_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.ae_map_curvature_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_isTorsionFree`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MollifiedBianchi.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/MollifiedFirstBianchi.lean) | PROVED as two theorems (one per connection route of the statement), UNDER A DISCLOSED AMENDMENT of the hypothesis list. AMENDMENT (manuscript correction for the author): field `spatialConnectionL3Bound` of the structure `RenewalGeometry.RenewalPalatiniHandoff.RenewalPalatiniCommon` (inherited by `RenewalPalatiniHypotheses` and `RenewalPalatiniLiteralLinkHypotheses`): a uniform L^3_loc bound on the SPATIAL connection interpolants (every connection coefficient except one temporal index t): for every compact C of the cylinder, sup_X \|\|omega_{X,a}\|\|_{L^3(C)} < infinity for a != t. It is NOT in the manuscript's hypothesis list of thm:supp-renewal-palatini; it is needed for the first Bianchi identity R wedge e = 0 of the limit at the available regularity (MollifiedFirstBianchi), which the manuscript's one-line argument ("the first Bianchi identity removes the Holst coframe variation") uses without justification at L^2 connection regularity (the commutator omega_t^rho (omega_t e)^rho is only L^{6/7}). Both concrete realizations of (E5) in the manuscript supply it: eq:main-connection-hodge-budget contains \|\|A_h\|\|_{L^4}^2, and the literal-link route has the L^{10/3} bound eq:supp-literal-integrability. Suggested manuscript fix: add "with sup_X \|\|A_X\|\|_{L^3(K)} < infinity for the spatial connection interpolants" to (E5) / ass:main-connection-compactness (or to the hypotheses of thm:supp-renewal-palatini). Nothing else is weakened.  FILES: Gravity/RenewalPalatiniHandoffExact.lean (assembly, both routes, non-vacuity) and Gravity/PhysicalLiftVariationLimit.lean (the (E1) passage and general strong/weak convergence lemmas).  HYPOTHESIS PACKET (RenewalPalatiniCommon, open cylinder Omega in R^4, regulator interpolant fields indexed by X = n): natural (regulator scores are Lorentz-natural members of the gravitational class: GravitationalClassDensity model of def:main-gravitational-class = polynomial local class, real Lorentz naturality, scalar residual commutant, represented determinant volume); calibrated coefficient limits holst_tendsto / palatini_tendsto / volume_tendsto for the coefficients read off the classified score (holstCoeff, palatiniCoeff, volumeCoeff) and palatini_ne_zero (beta != 0); coframe_L2 + coframe_L6 (eq:supp-coframe-l2-l6 on every compact); coframe_oriented (oriented regulator coframes; the class is defined on oriented coframes); limit_nondegenerate; connection_lorentz (metric-compatible Lorentz connections); spatialConnectionL3Bound (AMENDMENT); torsion_rep / torsion_antisymm / torsion_L2 (the regulator torsion two-form d e_X + omega_X wedge e_X is represented by an L^2_loc function); cartan_floor (uniform Cartan inverse of thm:supp-palatini-torsion: kappa \|\|v\|\| <= \|\|K_{e_X} v\|\| on antisymmetric torsion arrays, with the concrete PalatiniTorsionModel.cartan); euler_residual_tendsto (the spinless connection Euler residual P_{alpha_X,beta_X} K_{e_X}(T_X) tends to zero in L^2_loc; the inverse (alpha I - beta star)/(alpha^2+beta^2) is used with converging coefficients); curvature_L2 / curvature_bound (output of the continuous or discrete curvature certificate, thm:main-curvature-propagation / thm:main-discrete-curvature, both proved). Full-connection route (RenewalPalatiniHypotheses extends Common): connection_L2 (omega_X -> omega strongly in L^2_loc, ass:main-connection-compactness after extraction) and curvature_identification (eq:supp-curvature-identification). Literal-link route (RenewalPalatiniLiteralLinkHypotheses extends Common): connection coefficients bounded and weakly convergent in L^2_loc, spatial ones strongly convergent, curvature records weakly convergent to RL with RL = d omega + omega wedge omega in distributions (the identification output of thm:main-literal-link-compactness, proved; the bridge from its periodic-grid/Fourier encoding to this cylinder encoding is not formalised). (E1) physical lifts: coefficient tensors H_X relative to the coframe (delta e_X = e_X H_X), uniformly bounded and a.e. convergent on K to the limit lift metricTestGenerator = -1/2 k g of (e, k). Remainder condition eq:main-first-variation-reduction: classifiedVariationRemainder deltaA realizedHolst realizedPalatini realizedVolume alpha_X beta_X lambda_X -> 0, with the realized variations int_K delta_{e_X H_X} L_H(e_X, R_X), int_K delta_{e_X H_X} L_P(e_X, R_X), int_K det e_X tr H_X.  CONCLUSIONS (renewal_palatini_handoff, renewal_palatini_handoff_literalLink): (1) eq:supp-phv-classification: every regulator score equals ofCoefficients(alpha_X, beta_X, lambda_X) and the scores converge to alpha L_Holst + beta L_Palatini + lambda L_vol (pairing and volume parts); (2) limiting torsion vanishes (IsTorsionFree on Omega; strong passage, resp. weak temporal passage); (3) for every compact K in Omega there is a compact K' with K in int K', K' in Omega, such that the limit connection is metric compatible and the limit coframe oriented on K' (derived: linear constraints pass to strong/weak limits; det e_X -> det e in L^1 with det e_X > 0 and nondegeneracy), the limit connection is the unique torsion-free metric-compatible (Levi-Civita) L^2_loc connection of the limit coframe on int K', and for every inverse-metric test k with bounded convergent lifts and every sequence of complete first variations deltaA_X satisfying the remainder condition, deltaA_X -> int_K beta sqrt(-g) k^{gamma b} (G_{b gamma} + Lambda g_{b gamma}), Lambda = -lambda/(2 beta), chi = beta != 0 (eq:supp-einstein-insertion, einsteinInsertionDensity), G the Einstein tensor of the coordinate curvature of the identified limit curvature R = d omega + omega wedge omega. On the full-connection route the WHOLE sequence converges (stronger than the manuscript's subsequence): the identified curvature is determined a.e. by omega (ae_eq_of_isDistributionalCurvature), so every subsequence has a further subsequence (curvature_weak_compactness) with the same limit.  PROOF CHAIN: (E1) passage tendsto_liftVariations (L^2 + L^6 -> L^4 coframe convergence by lem:supp-coframe-interpolation; e tensor e -> strongly in L^2; bounded a.e.-convergent multipliers via Vitali, LpTendsto.bilin_of_bounded_ae; weak-strong pairing with the curvature, tendsto_integral_apply_weak_strong; exact rewriting of holstVariation / palatiniVariation / det as multilinear forms in (H, e tensor e, R)); variation transfer tendsto_completeVariation_of_classified (lem:supp-variation-transfer); Einstein insertion at limit regularity MollifiedBianchi.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible (Bianchi identity via the amendment); torsion via the concrete Cartan floor and P^{-1}; classification via GravitationalClassDensity.classification. Non-vacuity: renewalPalatiniHypotheses_flat and renewalPalatiniLiteralLinkHypotheses_flat (Omega = R^4, flat basis coframe, zero connection/curvature/torsion, the Palatini score with alpha = 0, beta = 1, lambda = 0, including the amendment; Lorentz naturality of the epsilon pairing proved for every unimodular Lambda, epsilonPairing_invariant; Cartan floor at the basis coframe by finite-dimensional antilipschitz, cartan_floor_basis). Standard axioms only (propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound); no sorry; no native_decide; both modules build with lake build.  DISCLOSED RENDERINGS: regulators are represented by common-cylinder interpolant fields on Omega (as throughout the library); the physical lift is encoded by its coefficient tensor relative to the coframe (delta e = e H, the form of the inverse-metric test lift); the regulator Euler residual is the represented P K_e(T) (equal to D(P B) for C^1 fields, PalatiniTorsionModel.eulerResidual_eq; the variational derivation "Euler equation of the action = D(P B)", the open clause of thm:supp-palatini-torsion, is not used); the curvature certificates enter through their proved output (uniform L^2 bounds); the bivector-carrier coefficients of the classified score are tied to the concrete eps-index densities through the remainder condition; G is the Einstein tensor of the coordinate curvature E E R (agreement with the classical Riemann tensor of g for C^2 coframes is the classical tetrad computation, not formalised, as in LimitEinsteinInsertion); the face-linearization and memory-short branches are abstracted into the classified score (as in the proved classification record). |
| `cor:supp-renewal-einstein` | corollary | Vacuum equation from certified accepted-action variations | [`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.renewal_vacuum_equation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.renewal_vacuum_equation_literalLink`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.renewal_vacuum_equation_almost_surely`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.CertifiedMetricTests`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.einsteinCosmologicalPairing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.cosmologicalConstant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.integral_einsteinInsertionDensity_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.insertion_vanishes_on_core`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.insertion_vanishes_on_closure`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.certifiedMetricTests_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.CertifiedMetricTests.mono`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalPalatiniHandoff.renewal_palatini_handoff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RenewalPalatiniHandoffExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalPalatiniHandoff.renewal_palatini_handoff_literalLink`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RenewalPalatiniHandoffExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.physical_test_stationarity_core`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.integrable_einsteinInsertionDensity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.ae_classifiedMetricVariation_eq_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.integrable_einsteinCosmologicalDensity_of_insertion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.ae_einsteinCosmologicalDensity_symmUnit_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.symmUnit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RenewalEinstein.einsteinCosmologicalDensity_smul`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementVacuumEquationInstantiated.lean) | Proved by instantiating the now-proved parent thm:supp-renewal-palatini (RenewalPalatiniHandoff.renewal_palatini_handoff / renewal_palatini_handoff_literalLink), not by assuming its conclusion. 'Under the preceding hypotheses' = RenewalPalatiniHypotheses (full-connection route) or RenewalPalatiniLiteralLinkHypotheses (literal-link route), which INCLUDE THE DISCLOSED AMENDMENT spatialConnectionL3Bound (uniform L3_loc bound on the spatial connection coefficients; not in the manuscript's hypothesis list, accepted as a manuscript correction; supplied by eq:main-connection-hodge-budget on the Hodge-temporal realization and by eq:supp-literal-integrability on the literal-link route), plus CertifiedMetricTests on a compact cylinder K (for each test of the determining metric-test core, an arbitrary set in a test type with test fields kOf: bounded convergent (E1) lifts de_X = e_X H_X, H_X -> -1/2 k g a.e., and the physical first-variation remainder condition eq:main-first-variation-reduction), and vanishing of the realized common-action first variations on the core along a selected cofinal subsequence. Conclusion (renewal_vacuum_equation): torsion-free limit, chi = beta != 0, an identified limiting curvature RL = d omega + omega^omega on a compact neighbourhood of K, convergence of the certified first variations to chi <G + Lambda g, k>_K (Lambda = -lambda/(2 beta), G the Einstein tensor of the coordinate curvature of RL), vanishing of <G + Lambda g, k>_K = int_K sqrt(-g) k^{gb}(G + Lambda g)_{bg} on the core (uniqueness of limits + division by chi != 0), and on the closure of the core whenever this pairing is continuous in the declared test topology (Set.EqOn.closure) -- exactly the corollary's proof. Stochastic clause (renewal_vacuum_equation_almost_surely): on a common coupling, mean-square bounds E\|dA_X(k_m)\|^2 <= b_{X,m} with the scaled test gap b_{X,m} -> 0 (eq:supp-scaled-test-gap) on a countable core give, via the proved prop:supp-physical-test-stationarity (physical_test_stationarity_core: Tonelli + diagonal selection), mean-square convergence and a deterministic subsequence of cutoffs on which all core variations vanish a.s.; if the compactness/identification budgets (handoff packet + certified tests) hold a.s. on the realized sequence along that subsequence (rendered: along every deterministic strictly increasing choice of cutoffs, which follows from the budgets holding along the whole realized path sequence since all items are subsequence-stable), then G + Lambda g = 0 a.s. (core and closure). Non-vacuity: certifiedMetricTests_flat + renewalPalatiniHypotheses_flat instantiate renewal_vacuum_equation (constant nonzero tests k with lifts -1/2 k eta), and a one-point coupling instantiates the almost-sure theorem. Disclosed renderings: G + Lambda g is tested against the inverse-metric test fields (its part detected by the test class); the closure clause is conditional on continuity of the pairing in the declared test topology, as in the manuscript's proof. The corollary's core argument is re-proved directly (insertion_vanishes_on_core / insertion_vanishes_on_closure) since the injective-panel interface of vacuum_equation_from_certified_variations is not needed. Sorry-free, standard axioms only. INTEGRABILITY (follow-up): the conclusions of renewal_vacuum_equation (both routes), renewal_vacuum_equation_almost_surely and renewal_einstein_certification now also export, for every core test, Integrable (einsteinInsertionDensity beta lam (cfm eL x) (k x) (rcf RL x)) and Integrable (einsteinCosmologicalDensity ...) on volume.restrict K, so the vanishing pairing is a genuine Lebesgue integral and not the Bochner junk value. Derived from the existing hypotheses, with nothing added to the packet: integrable_einsteinInsertionDensity uses the limit coframe in L6(K') (lpTendsto_four_of_two_six), the identified curvature in L2(K'), and the limit lift -1/2 k g, which is a.e. bounded as the a.e. limit of the uniformly bounded (E1) lifts. tendsto_liftVariations applied to constant sequences then gives integrability of the three limiting classified variation densities. ae_classifiedMetricVariation_eq_einstein (the pointwise form of MollifiedBianchi.integral_classifiedMetricVariation_of_metricCompatible: first Bianchi identity at limit regularity using the amendment's spatial L3 bound, so13 antisymmetry, form antisymmetry) identifies the classified variation with the Einstein insertion density a.e. on K. POINTWISE FORM: ae_einsteinCosmologicalDensity_symmUnit_eq_zero (corollary file) and renewal_einstein_certification_ae (main file) show that the symmetrized density sqrt(-g)((G+Lambda g)_ij + (G+Lambda g)_ji) vanishes a.e. on int K, provided the core contains the constant symmetric unit tests E_ij+E_ji and their localizations phi(E_ij+E_ji), phi in D(int K), via the fundamental lemma. The richness condition is stated on the core itself, not on its closure, because integrability is only certified on the core. Non-vacuity of the richness condition: renewalEinsteinCertification_flat_ae (flat data, rich core flatFieldCore of all bounded measurable test fields). |

| Proved on the odd periodic grid (Z/N)^3, N = 2m+1. Non-coercivity for \|\|∇ I_h u\|\|^2_{L^2} as stated: rootDirichlet_not_uniformly_coercive_interp (for every c > 0 there are m and u with D_F(u) < c \|\|∇ I_h u\|\|^2_{L^2}); here I_h u = interp u is the genuine trigonometric interpolant on Mathlib's UnitAddTorus (Fin 3) with frequencies in the signed cube {-m..m}^3 and \|\|∇ f\|\|^2_{L^2} = trigGradSq f = sum_n 4π^2\|n\|^2 \|mFourierCoeff f n\|^2 (Parseval form of the L^2 norm of the gradient). It follows from the coordinate-form obstruction of RootParityConnectorExact (parity test u_h = exp(2πi m(x_1+x_2+x_3)): D_F(u_h) ≤ 12π^2, D_C(u_h) ≥ 6N^2) and the uniform comparison (2/π)^2\|\|∇ I_h u\|\|^2 ≤ D_C(u) ≤ \|\|∇ I_h u\|\|^2 (coordDirichlet_equiv_interp, from lem:supp-open-calculus, now proved in PeriodicGridInterpolation). parityTest_gradient_ge: \|\|∇ I_h u_h\|\|^2 ≥ 6N^2. Root-graph distance N-1 (root_graph_distance), connector coercivity (1/5)D_C ≤ D_+ ≤ 9D_C (connector_coercivity; also transported to the interpolant gradient, connector_coercivity_interp) and the even-parity necessity clause (shiftEnergy_parityTest_even_le: every fixed even-coordinate-sum direction 2k contributes ≤ 4π^2k^2 on the parity test) as before. The test function is complex valued (the paper's own u_h = exp(...)); arrays are complex valued. |

./RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.gridNormSq_mul_le_two_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.term_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.sobSq_mul_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.commutator_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.commutator_diff_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.uniform_sobolev_calculus`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.Kprod`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.c1`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.interp_sample`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.mFourierCoeff_interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.trigSobSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.trigSobSq_interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.sobSq_le_trigSobSq_interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.trigSobSq_interp_le_sobSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridInterpolation.lean) | Proved on the periodic grid (Z/N)^3, h = 1/N (every N >= 1, in particular odd N), for complex-valued arrays (real arrays are the special case; vector/matrix-valued coefficients reduce componentwise with dimension-dependent constants - scalar rendering disclosed). Adjoints eq:supp-open-adjoints: gridInner_Dp_left ((D_i^+)* = -D_i^-), gridInner_D0_left ((D_i^0)* = -D_i^0) for <u,v>_h = h^3 sum conj(u) v. Shifted product rule eq:supp-open-shifted-product: Dα_mul, D^α(fw) = sum_{β ≤ α} binom(α,β) (S^{α-β}D^β f) D^{α-β} w with D^α = Π_i (D_i^+)^{α_i}, S^α = Π_i S_i^{α_i}, from the iterated one-direction Leibniz rule Dp_pow_mul. Norm equivalence: the trigonometric interpolant interp u = sum_k û(k) e_{k~} is a genuine continuous function on Mathlib's UnitAddTorus (Fin 3) (signed frequencies k~ = valMinAbs, the cube {-m..m}^3 for N = 2m+1), interpolating the grid values (interp_sample), with Mathlib Fourier coefficients û(k) on the cube and 0 elsewhere (mFourierCoeff_interp); the trigonometric H^r norm trigSobSq r f = sum_{n in Z^3} sum_{\|α\|≤r} \|(2πi n)^α\|^2 \|mFourierCoeff f n\|^2 (= sum_{\|α\|≤r} \|\|∂^α f\|\|^2_{L^2} by Parseval) satisfies (2/π)^{2r} \|\|I_h u\|\|^2_{H^r} ≤ \|\|u\|\|^2_{r,h} ≤ \|\|I_h u\|\|^2_{H^r} for every r and N (trigSobSq_interp_le_sobSq, sobSq_le_trigSobSq_interp), from the symbol comparison 4\|k_i\| ≤ \|d_i(k)\| ≤ 2π\|k_i\| (four_mul_le_norm_sym, norm_sym_le) and Parseval for the normalised DFT. Product algebra (r ≥ 2, so in particular r ≥ 3): sobSq_mul_le. Commutator bounds eq:supp-open-commutator-bounds for r ≥ 3, \|α\| ≤ r (implicit in the paper; false for \|α\| > r) and constant f_*: commutator_norm_le (\|\|C_α(f,w)\|\|_h ≤ C \|\|f-f_*\|\|_{r,h}\|\|w\|\|_{r-1,h}) and commutator_diff_norm_le (\|\|D_i^- C_α\|\|_h + \|\|D_i^0 C_α\|\|_h ≤ C\|\|f-f_*\|\|_{r+1,h}\|\|w\|\|_{r,h}). All constants are explicit and N-independent (K = 2 c1^3 with c1 = sum_{n in Z}(1+16n^2)^{-2/3}, binomial masses, the finite multi-index set); uniform_sobolev_calculus packages product algebra and both commutator lines with a single constant C_r valid for all N. Method: products are convolutions on the finite frequency group (dft_mul); a weighted Cauchy-Schwarz convolution estimate (conv_bound) with the uniform lattice sum gives \|\|ab\|\|_h^2 ≤ K\|\|a\|\|_{1,h}^2\|\|b\|\|_{1,h}^2 and \|\|ab\|\|_h^2 ≤ K\|\|a\|\|_{2,h}^2\|\|b\|\|_h^2; every term of the product/commutator expansions is then bounded by term_le. The proof's remark on analytic coefficient compositions is not part of the statement and is not formalised here. |
| `prop:supp-open-locality` | proposition | Locality and continuum consistency of the writer | [`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writerRhs_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writerAcceleration_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.grid_stencil_within_two_hops`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writer_continuum_consistency`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.tendsto_flux_term`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.tendsto_skewTransport`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.tendsto_compensator`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.tendsto_mixed_second_difference`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writerRhs`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.writerAcceleration`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.skewTransport`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.compensator`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.InStencil`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicWriter.ReachTwo`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/HarmonicWriterLocalityExact.lean) | Proved. The writer eq:main-open-writer is defined for grid functions on an arbitrary additive group of sites with step vectors s_i and mesh h (fwd/bwd/ctr = D_i^±, D_i^0 of eq:main-open-differences; skewTransport = 𝖪_b; compensator = 𝖦_h with abstract coefficient derivatives Dc, Db; writerRhs / writerAcceleration = second row, a(g) v_t = Σ D_i^-(c^{ij}(g) D_j^+ q) − 𝖪_b v + 𝖦_h); the periodic grid (s_i = e_i, h = 1/N) and the lattice hℤ³ ⊂ ℝ³ (s_i = h e_i) are instances. Locality (writerRhs_local, writerAcceleration_local): the acceleration at x depends only on q, v at x and the twelve stencil neighbours x ± s_i, x − s_i + s_j (i ≠ j; InStencil). Two hops (grid_stencil_within_two_hops): on (ℤ/N)³ every stencil neighbour is x + d with d a sum of at most two steps of the execution graph of lem:supp-open-parity (A3 roots ∪ {±e_1}; ReachTwo, using IsRoot/ε/toGrid of RootParityConnectorExact). Continuum consistency (writer_continuum_consistency): for q ∈ C², v ∈ C¹, C¹ coefficients b^i, c^{ij}, continuous first-jet source F, with Dc^{ij}, Db^i the Fréchet derivatives, the right-hand side of the second row at x ∈ ℝ³ with steps h e_i tends as h ↓ 0 to Σ c^{ij}(g) D²q(x)(e_i)(e_j) − 2 Σ b^i(g) Dv(x)e_i + F(g,(v, Dq(x)e_·)), i.e. the harmonic normal row eq:main-harmonic-normal-row a q_tt = c^{ij}∂_i∂_j q − 2b^i∂_i q_t + F; the divergence contributes (∂_i c^{ij})∂_j q and the skew transport (∂_i b^i) v, cancelled exactly by the compensator via the chain rule (tendsto_flux_term, tendsto_skewTransport, tendsto_compensator); mixed second differences via Convex.taylor_approx_two_segment. Scoped: consistency is pointwise for the lattice writer on ℝ³ (same formula as the periodic grid for N-periodic smooth fields sampled on it); regularity q ∈ C², v, b, c ∈ C¹. |
| `thm:supp-open-energy` | theorem | All-component shifted nonlinear energy | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.open_energy_inequality`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.shiftedEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.energyRate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.Xsq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.Xnorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.hasDerivAt_shiftedEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.static_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.Rrow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.cx_Rrow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.writer_row`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.harmonicWriterAcceleration_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.harmonicSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.harmonicWriterAcceleration`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.compensatorMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.analyticAt_compensatorMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.compensator_series_one_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.analyticAt_harmA`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterChart.analyticAt_harmC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.CommutedRow.commuted_row`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridCommutedRow.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.CommutedRow.rowRem`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridCommutedRow.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.CommutedRow.rowRem_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridCommutedRow.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.CommutedRow.sobSq_succ_le_shifted`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridCommutedRow.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.Moser.moser_composition_order`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridMoserComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.Moser.norm_le_sobNorm_two`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridMoserComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterEnergy.hasDerivAt_energy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterEnergyIdentity.lean) | Proved (open_energy_inequality), for every s >= 3 (paper: s >= 11), every N >= 1 (odd grid N = 2m+1 included), constants and chart radius independent of the mesh. Encoding: ten-component records = symmetric 4x4 arrays q, v on (Z/N)^3, energy and X^s_h norm summed over the ten upper components; the writer is OpenWriterChart.harmonicWriterAcceleration = eq:main-open-writer with a = -g^{00}, b^i = -g^{0i}, c^{ij} = g^{ij} (matrix inverse of g = eta + q), compensator differentials the Frechet derivatives of c^{ij}, b^i, and the EXPLICIT first-jet source F(g,Y) = -2 Q(g, g^{-1}, dg) with Q = HarmonicDefect.qRem the first-order part of the reduced Ricci identity R - nabla_(mu C_nu) = -1/2 g^{ab} d_a d_b g + Q (ricci_sub_symDefect), dg_0 = v, dg_i = D_i^+ q; this is the paper's 'analytic first-jet source of the harmonic-reduced vacuum Ricci equation' (the normal row a q_tt + 2 b^i d_i q_t - c^{ij} d_ij q - F = 0 is exactly -2 times that equation). Statement: there are delta > 0, C >= 0 such that for every N and every history with q_t = v, v_t = writer acceleration (ten components), symmetric records at time t and \|\|X(t)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= delta (the fixed chart), the shifted energy E_{s,h} of eq:supp-open-energy (shifted coefficients a_alpha = S^alpha a, c_alpha = S^alpha c) is differentiable at t with derivative energyRate (the exact identity eq:supp-open-energy-identity, with R_alpha the commuted-row remainder), \|\|X\|\|^2/4 <= E_{s,h}, and dE/dt <= C E + C E^{3/2}. Proof: exact identity per multi-index (hasDerivAt_energy); R_alpha = rowRem (commuted_row, eq:supp-open-commuted-row); \|\|R_alpha\|\|_h <= K \|\|X\|\|^2 (eq:supp-open-remainder) from the commutator bounds (rowRem_norm_le), the discrete Moser estimates (moser_composition_order: order one for a, a^{-1}, b, c; order two for the compensator, whose linear term vanishes since it is quadratic in the first jet) and \|\|v_t\|\|_{s-1,h} <= K\|\|X\|\|; coefficient time derivatives bounded pointwise through the uniform embedding H^2_h in l^infty (norm_le_sobNorm_two); energy equivalence via a >= 1/2, c >= (5/6) I in the chart and the counting lemma sobSq_succ_le_shifted. Non-vacuity: the flat history q = v = 0 satisfies all hypotheses (harmonicWriterAcceleration_zero, example in the file). |

[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.open_writer_lifespan`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.forced_energy_inequality`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterForcedEnergy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.open_writer_difference`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsWriterSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.apriori_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.energy_upper`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.analyticAt_accel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.exists_lipschitz_writerField`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLifespan.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.hasDerivAt_shiftedEnergy_forced`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterForcedEnergy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.Fnorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterForcedEnergy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsForcedPair`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.diffSource_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.diff_static_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.genEnergy_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ODECutoff.exists_solution_of_apriori`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ODECutoffContinuation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ODECutoff.firstExit_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ODECutoffContinuation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ODECutoff.sqrt_le_of_forced_deriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ODECutoffContinuation.lean) | Proved, all three clauses, with constants independent of the mesh h=1/N, for every s>=3 (covers the manuscript's s>=11); parent thm:supp-open-energy is proved (open_energy_inequality). (1) Common lifespan, eq:main-open-uniform-time: open_writer_lifespan gives eps,T,C>0 such that every symmetric (ten-component) initial record with \|\|X(0)\|\|_{X^s_h}<=eps has a solution of the open writer (explicit harmonic coefficients and source) on [0,T] with sup_t \|\|X(t)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= C \|\|X(0)\|\|_{X^s_h}, and any two solutions coincide on [0,T]. Proof: finite-dimensional ODE; the field is real-analytic on a fixed sup-ball (analyticAt_accel, analyticAt_compensatorMap_of_det), hence Lipschitz/bounded there (compactness); cut-off field + Picard-Lindelof + continuous induction (Analysis/ODECutoffContinuation.lean: exists_solution_of_apriori, firstExit_le); the a priori bound is the scalar comparison for eq:supp-open-energy-inequality with the new upper energy bound E<=8\|\|X\|\|^2 (energy_upper); the mesh-uniform Sobolev embedding keeps the history inside the fixed chart; symmetry is preserved by uniqueness. Scoped renderings: \|\|X\|\|_{X^s_h} = sqrt(sum over ten components of \|\|q\|\|^2_{s+1,h}+\|\|v\|\|^2_{s,h}) (equivalent to \|\|q\|\|_{H^{s+1}_h}+\|\|v\|\|_{H^s_h}); a solution (IsWriterSolution) has two-sided time derivatives at every t in [0,T] and the evolution equation on the ten upper components; uniqueness is within that class. (2) eq:supp-open-forced-energy: forced_energy_inequality: for v_t = V_{0,h}(q,v)+f on a fixed chart, dE/dt <= 2 E^{1/2}(C E^{1/2}+C E+C\|\|f\|\|_{s,h}) and, where E>0, (E^{1/2})' <= C E^{1/2}+C E+C\|\|f\|\|_{s,h}. (3) eq:supp-open-difference: open_writer_difference: for 3<=r<=s-1, two forced records X=(q,v), Y=(p,w) with forces f, f~ continuous on [0,T], both in the top chart \|\|.\|\|_{X^s_h}<=delta on [0,T]: \|\|X(t)-Y(t)\|\|_{X^r_h} <= 2 e^{Kt}(3\|\|X(0)-Y(0)\|\|_{X^r_h} + K int_0^t \|\|f-f~\|\|_{r,h}) for all t in [0,T]; this gives eq:supp-open-difference with C_s = 6 e^{K T_s}(1+K) (no inverse-mesh exponential). Proof as in the manuscript: difference energy with the shifted coefficients of q (genEnergy, uniformly equivalent to \|\|dX\|\|^2), exact difference row with source eq:supp-open-difference-source (diffSource_eq), commuted row + rowRem_norm_le, Lipschitz Moser estimates for a,b,c and the compensator (moser_lipschitz_coefficients/compensator), accel_bound for V_{0,h}(p,w), and a forced square-root Gronwall (sqrt_le_of_forced_deriv). Disclosed scoped hypothesis: the forces are continuous in time on [0,T] (needed for the integral and the FTC). Non-vacuity examples (flat data / flat pair) included. |

cGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.difference_consistency_Dp_iteratedDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.difference_consistency_Dm_iteratedDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.difference_consistency_D0_iteratedDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.productError`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.hasDerivAt_productError`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.leibnizError`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.hasDerivAt_leibnizError`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.sn_productError_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.product_consistency_iteratedDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetPos`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetInit`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetTail`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetMap_succ`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.analyticOnNhd_jetMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetScale`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jetMap_scale`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.hasDerivAt_jetMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.jet_scaling_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.HigherTimeConsistency.composition_consistency_iteratedDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean) | PROVED. All five displayed estimates (sampling error with the h^j / H^{r+j} refinement, sampling stability, difference consistency for D_i^+, D_i^-, D_i^0, product consistency, analytic composition consistency) are proved with mesh-independent constants for complex arrays and every N >= 1 (odd grid N = 2m+1 included): DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSampling.lean, PeriodicGridCompositionConsistency.lean (A analytic at the constant base point c, rendered as HasFPowerSeriesOnBall A p c R on C^iota). NEW (fD1): the time-differentiated versions of EVERY order k, DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridHigherTimeConsistency.lean. A history with k controlled time derivatives on an open time set O is a jet v_0 = u_h, v_1, ..., v_k with d_t v_i = v_{i+1} on O (IsJet; the iteratedDeriv jet of any C^k history is one, isJet_iteratedDeriv); the general principle iteratedDeriv_eq_of_chain turns derivative chains into iteratedDeriv identities. (i) Linear errors: d_t^k(P_h f - f) = P_h f^(k) - f^(k) and d_t^k(I_h D u_h - d_i I_h u_h) = same error of u_h^(k), with the undifferentiated bounds applied to the k-th derivative (sampling_error_iteratedDeriv, sampling_stable_iteratedDeriv, difference_consistency_{Dp,Dm,D0}_iteratedDeriv). (ii) Products, general Leibniz rule (product_consistency_iteratedDeriv, r >= 1): d_t^k[I_h(u w) - I_h u I_h w] = sum_{i<=k} C(k,i) [I_h(u^(i) w^(k-i)) - I_h u^(i) I_h w^(k-i)] in C(T^3), and \|\|.\|\|_{H^r} <= C h sum_i C(k,i) \|\|u^(i)\|\|_{r+1,h} \|\|w^(k-i)\|\|_{r+1,h}, C independent of h, k and the histories. (iii) Compositions, Faa di Bruno (composition_consistency_iteratedDeriv, r >= 1, A analytic at c, history in the small chart sum_j \|\|u_j(s) - c_j\|\|_{r+1,h} <= delta for s in O): the jet map Phi_0 = A, Phi_{k+1}(z_0..z_{k+1}) = D Phi_k(z_0..z_k)(z_1..z_{k+1}) (jetMap; the (z,w) \|-> DA(z)w trick iterated) is analytic on {z_0 in the ball} (analyticOnNhd_jetMap) and weighted homogeneous Phi_k(z_0, lam z_1, ..., lam^k z_k) = lam^k Phi_k(z) (jetMap_scale); by the chain rule (hasDerivAt_jetMap) at every torus point d_t^k[I_h A(u_h) - A(I_h u_h)] = I_h Phi_k(u_h, ..., u_h^(k)) - Phi_k(I_h u_h, ..., I_h u_h^(k)), a composition error of the analytic map Phi_k at the base jet (c, 0, ..., 0), so composition_consistency applies; homogeneity removes every smallness requirement on the time derivatives and gives \|\|d_t^k[...]\|\|^2_{H^r} <= C h^2 (sum_{i=1}^k \|\|u^(i)\|\|_{r+1,h}^{1/i})^{2k} (each time derivative with its Faa di Bruno weight i; k = 1 is the earlier composition_consistency_deriv). In every case the right-hand side is the undifferentiated estimate applied to the time derivatives, so one spatial order is spent on each controlled time derivative exactly through the norm in which u^(i) is controlled. Scope disclosed: product/composition are proved for r >= 1 (the statement has r >= 2); the composition chart constrains the position only; constants depend on k (and A, r) but not on h or the history. Non-vacuity: the constant jet (c, 0, 0, ...) (example in the file). |
| `lem:supp-open-time-jets` | lemma | Uniform physical time jets | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_time_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.jetDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.hasDerivWithinAt_lawAccel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.jetDeriv_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.moser_rate_coefficients`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.moser_rate_compensator`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.accel_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterDifferenceEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsAccSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsMark`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean) | Proved for every s>=4 (covers the manuscript's s>=11), uniformly in the mesh and in the mark B (B=B^T, \|\|B\|\|_op<=1/48; the open writer is the mark B=0 by lawAccel_zero): law_time_jets gives delta>0, C>=0 such that every solution of the law-family writer eq:main-open-law-family (B constant during the history) on [0,T] staying in the top ball \|\|X\|\|_{X^s_h}<=eps<=delta satisfies at every t in [0,T], componentwise on the ten components: q_ttt exists (tau -> q_tt(tau) = V_{B,h}(q,v) has derivative jetDeriv within [0,T]) and \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}+\|\|q_t\|\|_{s,h}+\|\|q_tt\|\|_{s-1,h}+\|\|q_ttt\|\|_{s-2,h} <= C eps (eq:supp-open-time-jet-bound; q_t = v and q_tt = V_{B,h} are the defining equations of the solution). Proof as in the manuscript: the q_tt bound is the exported acceleration bound (accel_bound, previously internal to static_bounds) plus \|\|a^{-1} h^2 B Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-1} <= C\|\|q\|\|_{s+1} (sobNorm_bTerm_le); q_ttt is obtained by differentiating the mass row once (jetDeriv: a q_ttt = sum D_i^-(cdot D_j^+ q + c D_j^+ v) - K_bdot v - K_b q_tt + DG[(v,q_tt,D^+v)] - h^2 B Lambda^2 v - adot q_tt), and each term is bounded in H^{s-2}_h by the product calculus, Moser bounds for the coefficient rates adot=Da[v], cdot, bdot and for the compensator differential (analytic maps of the pair vanishing at the origin), and h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 v\|\|_{s-2} <= 36\|\|v\|\|_s; no time derivative of B occurs. The C_t norms are the sup over [0,T] of the pointwise-in-time bounds. Non-vacuity example (flat history) included. |

[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.supp_open_reduced_consistency`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.supp_open_reduced_consistency_law`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.interp_reduced_residual_deriv_law`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.interp_rowDeriv_small`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.interp_row_small_law`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.errDeriv_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.hasDerivWithinAt_errRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.errRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.errDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.row_identity_interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.rowDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.rowDeriv_sn_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.hasDerivWithinAt_normalRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.prodT_fields`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.isLineDeriv_prodT`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.hasDerivWithinAt_prodD`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.moser_lipschitz_compRate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.rate_mul_family`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.rate_family_gen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.coef_mul_family_gen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.sn_le_of_sample`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.reducedEinstein_eq_trForm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.accRec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.accDRec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.interpRowDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidual.reducedEinstein_eq_traceRev_normalRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidual.reduced_residual_of_rows`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidual.interp_reduced_residual_small`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidual.interp_reduced_residual_small_law`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidual.trCoef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterReducedResidual.analyticAt_trCoef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_time_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.jetDeriv_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterTimeJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_row_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.interp_row_small`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumLimit.lean) | PROVED (fD4), both halves of eq:supp-open-reduced-rate, central writer and B-writer, in Gravity/OpenWriterReducedResidualDeriv.lean. supp_open_reduced_consistency (central, unforced): for s >= 5 (manuscript s >= 11) there are delta, C independent of the mesh h = 1/N, of the history and of T such that every writer solution (IsWriterSolution) on [0,T] with \|\|X(t)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= eps <= delta satisfies at every t in [0,T] and every component (a,b): \|\|r_{h,ab}(t)\|\|_{H^{s-2}} <= C h eps and the time derivative of tau -> r_{h,ab}(tau,y) within [0,T] exists at every y in T^3 and is an H^{s-3} field with norm <= C h eps. Here r_h = G(g_h) - H(g_h, c(g_h)) (reducedEinstein) is the complete reduced residual of the interpolant jet of g_h = eta + I_h q with the actual finite acceleration I_h V_{0,h}(q,v) in the acceleration slot (as in fC2b). supp_open_reduced_consistency_law: the same for the B-writer (IsAccSolution (lawAccel B)) with constants uniform over IsMark (1/48) B, the library's rendering of the closed ball of radius b0 = 1/48 (as in law_time_jets; IsMark.mono covers every smaller closed ball). Route (the manuscript's 'differentiate these decompositions once in physical time'): (i) the sampled row identity a(q)(W - V_0) - S_h rho = errRow (row_identity_interp, from sampled_row_identity) holds along the straight record path (q + tau v, v + tau q_tt); differentiating at tau = 0 (uniqueness of derivatives) gives S_h rho' = -h^2 B Lambda_h^2 v - errDeriv q v v q_tt, where rho' = interpRowDeriv is the linearized normal row (rowDeriv) of the interpolant jet in the direction (I v, I q_tt, I q_ttt, dI v, dI q_tt, d^2 I v), q_ttt = jetDeriv; (ii) errDeriv_bound: the differentiated flux, interpolation, transport and compensator errors are O(h X) in H^{s-3}_h (the time and space derivatives of coefficient products commute by symmetry of D^2 of the analytic coefficients, isLineDeriv_prodT; Lipschitz Moser for the compensator differential); (iii) rowDeriv_sn_le: rho' in H^{s-2} with norm <= C X (jetDeriv_bound, lawAccel_bound); aliasing sn_le_of_sample gives \|\|rho'\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h X (interp_rowDeriv_small); (iv) along the history the jet slots have the derivatives used in (i) (hasDerivWithinAt_lawAccel, interpolation commutes with d_t), r_h = rho_ab/2 - 1/2 sum g_ab g^cd rho_cd/2 on [0,T] (reducedEinstein_eq_trForm), and its derivative is bounded with coef_mul_family / rate_mul_family for trCoef and the O(h) rows interp_row_small_law. Rendering/scope: the derivative is HasDerivWithinAt on Icc 0 T (unique when T > 0; one-sided at the ends, as in supp_open_subsidiary); norms are componentwise trigonometric H^r norms. Non-vacuity: the flat history (example at the end of the file). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. |
| `lem:supp-open-initial-gauge` | lemma | Initial harmonic and normal-constraint errors | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.supp_open_initial_gauge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.supp_open_subsidiary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.subsidiary_data_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.gauge_hasDerivWithinAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.interpJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.initial_gauge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ContractedBianchiJet.SliceData.gc_harmonicInit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SliceGaussCodazziJet.lean) | Proved (supp_open_initial_gauge), s >= 6: for (gamma,K) in D_s(eps), eps <= eps_s, the harmonic initialization eq:supp-open-harmonic-initial and its sampled preparation q_h(0) = S_h Q0, v_h(0) = S_h V0, the interpolated metric g_h(0) with the actual finite acceleration I_h(symRec(harmonicWriterAcceleration)) of the sampled data satisfies \|\|c_b(g_h)(0)\|\|_{H^{s-2}} <= C h eps and \|\|d_t c_b(g_h)(0)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps componentwise, d_t c(g_h)(0) being the one-sided time derivative at 0 along the writer solution. Obtained as the t = 0 case of supp_open_subsidiary (whose proof does not use this lemma: it compares with the limit, where c = 0 and d_t c = 0 by C3Field.Hyp.initial_gauge / gc_eq_zero). The paper's own route (Gauss-Codazzi constraints + reduced residual) is not followed; the exact-data form C3Field.Hyp.initial_gauge remains cited. |
| `lem:supp-open-subsidiary` | lemma | Forced subsidiary energy on the common interval | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.supp_open_subsidiary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.subsidiary_data_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.gaugeOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.dtGaugeOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.einOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.pjJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.analyticAt_gaugeOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.analyticAt_dtGaugeOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.analyticAt_einOf`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.pjet_moser_fin`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.writer_jet_rates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.interpJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.interpJet_time_derivs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.gauge_hasDerivWithinAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.hyp_dgc_time_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterSubsidiary.interp_det_ne_of_small`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterSubsidiaryRate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.subsidiary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.gc_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.waveSolves`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ContractedBianchiJet.Jet3.metric_forced_subsidiary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugeCovectorJet.lean) | Proved (supp_open_subsidiary), for every s >= 6 (the paper takes s >= 11): there are eps_s, T_s > 0, C >= 0 such that for every (gamma,K) in D_s(eps) (DsData, dataSize <= eps <= eps_s) with the harmonic initialization V0 = prepFun, the open writer has for every mesh h = 1/(n+1) a unique solution on [0,T_s] from the samples, and for every t in [0,T_s] the interpolated metric g_h = eta + I_h q_h (jet field interpJet; its slots are the actual derivatives: interpJet_time_derivs, isContJet_interp) satisfies, componentwise, \|\|c_b(g_h)\|\|_{H^{s-2}} <= C h eps, \|\|d_t c_b(g_h)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps, \|\|G_{mu nu}(g_h)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps, where c_b = g_{b mu} g^{ab} Gamma^mu_{ab} (gaugeOf = Jet3.gc), G = Ric - (1/2) g R (einOf = Jet3.einM), and d_t c(g_h) is the jet expression dtGaugeOf which is proved to be the time derivative of tau -> c_b(g_h)(tau,y) within [0,T_s] (gauge_hasDerivWithinAt; one-sided at the ends). Route (differs from the paper's commuted wave energy, same statement): c, d_t c and G are analytic functionals of the pointwise 2-jet at the flat jet (analyticAt_*), so the Lipschitz continuum Moser estimate (pjet_moser_fin, from cont_moser_lipschitz) bounds c(g_h) - c(g) in H^{s-2} by the 1-jet rates (metric H^{s-1}, velocity and spatial gradient H^{s-2}) and d_t c, G differences in H^{s-3} by the 2-jet rates (writer_jet_rates, from writer_limit_solves); the limit g has c(g) = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.gc_eq_zero), d_t c(g) = 0 on the closed slab (hyp_dgc_time_eq_zero) and G(g) = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.einM_eq_zero), the limit being identified with limField as in supp_open_einstein. Constants absorb exp(K T_s)(1+T_s). The exact forced subsidiary identity eq:supp-open-exact-subsidiary is proved separately (metric_forced_subsidiary). Non-vacuity: subsidiary_data_nonvacuous (flat data in D_s(0)). No other open ledger statement is assumed. |
| `lem:supp-open-continuum-comparison` | lemma | Lower-order Cauchy comparison and acceleration recovery | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.continuum_comparison`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.IsContSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.normalRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_force_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_row_consistency`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_row_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.normalRow_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.isContSolution_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.sample_Dp_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.sample_Dm_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.sample_D0_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.cont_moser`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.memH_mul`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusSobolevTransfer.mFourierCoeff_of_isLineDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean) | PROVED (Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean, continuum_comparison). Statement: for s >= 5 (manuscript s >= 11) there are delta > 0 and K, C >= 0, independent of the mesh h = 1/N (every N >= 1, odd grid included), such that for every T, every solution X_h = (q, v) of the central (open) writer eq:main-open-writer on [0,T] (IsWriterSolution, explicit harmonic source) in the grid top ball \|\|X_h(t)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= delta, and every continuum solution g = eta + Q of the harmonic normal row eq:main-harmonic-normal-row on [0,T] in the common top ball \|\|(Q,V)\|\|_top = sum_k \|\|Q_k\|\|_{H^{s+1}} + sum_k \|\|V_k\|\|_{H^s} <= eps <= delta, with Y_h = S_h(g - eta, d_t g) and e_{0,h} = \|\|X_h(0) - Y_h(0)\|\|_{X^{s-2}_h}: for every t in [0,T], \|\|(q - S_h(g-eta), v - S_h d_t g)(t)\|\|_{X^{s-2}_h} (which controls \|\|q - S_h(g-eta)\|\|_{s-1,h} and \|\|v - S_h d_t g\|\|_{s-2,h}) and, componentwise on the ten upper components, \|\|q_tt - S_h d_t^2 g\|\|_{s-3,h} (q_tt = V_{0,h}(q,v)) are <= C e^{K t} (e_{0,h} + (1 + t) h eps); on the common slab [0, T_s] this is eq:supp-open-sampled-comparison with C_s = C e^{K T_s}(1 + T_s). Encoding of 'continuum solution' (IsContSolution s T Q V W Qd Vd Qdd): continuous symmetric 4x4 record fields on Mathlib's UnitAddTorus (Fin 3); Q_t = V, V_t = W pointwise (two-sided time derivatives on [0,T], the same convention as the grid IsWriterSolution), W continuous in time, Qd_i, Vd_i, Qdd_ij the classical coordinate derivatives (IsLineDeriv: t -> F(x + t e_i) differentiable with the given derivative), components of Q in H^{s+1} and of V in H^s (trigonometric Fourier norms), and normalRow = a(g) W + 2 b^i(g) d_i V - c^{ij}(g) d_i d_j Q - F(g, dg) = 0 pointwise, with a = -g^{00}, b^i = -g^{0i}, c^{ij} = g^{ij} and the explicit harmonic source F = harmonicSource (the same chart and source as the writer). Proof as in the manuscript: sampling the continuum row gives the finite row with an acceleration force of H^{s-2}_h size <= C h eps (sampled_force_bound, from sampled_row_consistency: the pointwise chain-rule identity normalRow_identity and sampled_row_identity split the error into flux, transport and compensator terms, each bounded by the sampled difference consistency sample_Dp/Dm/D0_sub_le (\|\|D S_h F - S_h dF\|\|_{r,h} <= C h \|\|F\|\|_{H^{r+2}}), the continuum Moser/product bounds cont_moser/memH_mul for c(g) dQ, b(g) V, and the grid Lipschitz Moser bound for the compensator); then the difference estimate open_writer_difference at r = s-2 (no inverse-mesh exponential) gives the first two terms, and the acceleration is recovered by the Lipschitz bound dAcc_bound (subtract the mass rows). Non-vacuity: isContSolution_flat and the example after continuum_comparison (flat grid and continuum histories satisfy all hypotheses). Scoped renderings: s >= 5; joint X^{s-2}_h norm for the first two terms (equivalent to their sum); e^{Kt}(1+t) dependence of the constant made explicit. New infrastructure (es9b3, 1 Oct 2026): Continuum/TorusSobolevTransfer.lean (grid-to-continuum transfer on T^3: Riemann-sum convergence of the DFT of samples tendsto_dft_sample, Fatou transfer trigSobSq_le_of_proj_le / transfer_of_grid (every mesh-uniform grid bound for samples of a continuous F gives F in H^r with the same bound), continuum product algebra memH_mul and continuum Moser estimates cont_moser / cont_moser_lipschitz for analytic compositions of general continuous H^r fields, H^2 in C^0 norm_apply_le_sn_two, classical line derivatives IsLineDeriv with Fourier rule mFourierCoeff_of_isLineDeriv (F'^(n) = 2 pi i n_i F^(n), via translation invariance and dominated convergence), sampled-difference consistency sample_Dp/Dm/D0_sub_le: \|\|D_i S_h F - S_h d_i F\|\|_{r,h} <= C h \|\|F\|\|_{H^{r+2}}); Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean (continuum harmonic normal row normalRow = eq:main-harmonic-normal-row with the explicit harmonic source, continuum jets IsContJet, continuum solutions IsContSolution, sampled consistency of the writer sampled_row_consistency: \|\|a(q)(S_h W - V_{0,h}(S_h Q, S_h V)) - S_h rho\|\|_{s-2,h} <= C h \|\|(Q,V)\|\|_top for every continuum jet (rho its normal-row residual), sampled_force_bound, and lem:supp-open-continuum-comparison continuum_comparison). |
