eEnergyUniqueness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusWaveEnergy.integral_lineDeriv_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusWaveEnergyUniqueness.lean) | PROVED (es10b4, with the paper's hypotheses): supp_open_einstein (Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicPreparation.lean). For s >= 6 there are eps_s, T_s > 0 and constants K, C, C_eps such that for every (gamma, K) in D_s(eps) (DsData, \|\|gamma - I\|\|_{H^{s+1}} + \|\|K\|\|_{H^s} <= eps <= eps_s): the harmonic preparation V0 (prepFun, eq:supp-open-harmonic-initial) is a continuous field in H^s with \|\|(Q0, V0)\|\|_top <= C (\|\|gamma - I\|\| + \|\|K\|\|) (prep_moser: cont_moser for the inverse-metric entries and memH_mul); for every mesh the open writer has a solution on [0, T_s] from the samples of (Q0, V0), unique among writer solutions with those data (open_writer_lifespan + Xnorm_sample_le); and, for every t in [0, T_s], the whole sequence of real interpolants of the writer solutions (q_n, v_n, d_t v_n and their first/second spatial derivatives) converges uniformly on T^3 (Tendsto in C(T^3)) to the fields (Q, V, A, Qd, Vd, Qdd) of a classical solution of the harmonic reduced equation with Q(0) = Q0, V(0) = V0 (whole-sequence convergence; uniqueness in the continuum solution class: contSolution_eq_limit), with the metric rate eq:supp-open-metric-rate (C e^{Kt}(1+t) h eps in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3}) and the curvature rate eq:supp-open-curvature-rate, and the Einstein tensor of the limit metric vanishes on [0, T] x T^3 (eq:supp-open-vacuum). Proof (route B1-B5): B1 writer_limit_hyp (OpenWriterLimitRegularity.lean): the limits of the interpolant derivative families derivRec q [alpha] (\|alpha\| <= 3), v (<= 2), d_t v (<= 1) exist uniformly on [0, T] x T^3 (FamRate: one Sobolev order per spectral derivative, derivRec_cauchy, interp_cauchy), are jointly continuous, their time derivatives are the next families (hasDerivAt_derivRec_time, FamRate.hasDerivAt), spatial derivatives commute (specD_comm), d_t^3 g = D rowAcc[...] by the analytic row (analyticOnNhd_rowAcc), the limit is in the chart \|g^{mu nu} - eta\| <= 1/10, and the normal row vanishes in all components (normalRow_symm, via the symmetry of the Ricci tensor JetFam.ricM_symm) - so the limit is a C3Field satisfying C3Field.Hyp; B2 jet chain rule (MetricJetChainRule.lean, PathDeriv) with the derivative of the inverse metric mhasDeriv_inv; B3 TorusWaveEnergy.WaveData.eq_zero_of_zero_data applied to the gauge covector c(g) (C3Field.Hyp.waveSolves: a = -g^{00}, beta = g^{0i}, gamma = g^{ij}, lower-order bound by homogeneity and compactness); B4 InitData from HarmonicPrep: c(0) = 0 by gc_harmonicInit and d_t c(0) = 0 by hM_zero_zero/succ, einM_zero_zero/succ (Gauss-Codazzi) and r = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.initial_gauge); B5 the reduced residual vanishes (normalRow_eq + harmonic_residual_trace_reversal: reducedEinstein_eq_zero_of_normalRow), hence Box c + Ric c = 0 (Jet3.subsidiary_of_residual_zero), c = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.gc_eq_zero) and G = H(c) + r = 0 (C3Field.Hyp.einM_eq_zero, einstein_eq_zero). writer_limit_vacuum combines this with writer_limit_curvature_rate_sym (writer_limit_curvature_rate exporting the symmetric limit acceleration). Scoped renderings (disclosed): (1) s >= 6 (paper: s >= 11; the proof needs uniform convergence of third spatial derivatives of the interpolants, H^{s-4} in C^0); (2) D_s(eps) is record-encoded (DsData): Q0 = g^0 - eta with spatial block gamma - I and Kr with spatial block K (vanishing time rows, symmetric), gamma - I in H^{s+1} and K in H^s componentwise, the size \|\|gamma - I\|\|_{H^{s+1}} + \|\|K\|\|_{H^s} is the component-sum norm dataSize, the classical derivative fields d gamma, dd gamma, dK are part of the data (they exist by Sobolev embedding for s >= 4; not re-derived), gamma^{-1} in the constraints is the spatial block of (eta + Q0)^{-1} (= gamma^{-1} by inv_block); (3) convergence is stated for each fixed t in C(T^3); uniformity on the slab [0, T_s] x T^3 follows from the sup_t H^{s-1} rate and H^2 in C^0 but is not a separate clause; the conclusion G(g) = 0 is HarmonicDefect.einstein of the limit 2-jet at every point of [0, T_s] x T^3; time derivatives of the limit are two-sided on (0, T_s); rates are C e^{Kt}(1+t) h (C_eps eps). Non-vacuity: dsData_zero (flat data). |
| `cor:supp-open-direct-route` | corollary | Direct constructive Einstein limit | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_open_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHarmonicPreparation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.HarmonicPrep`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterVacuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.writer_limit_vacuum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterVacuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.harmonicPrep_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterVacuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.writer_limit_curvature_rate_sym`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterVacuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HarmonicGaugePropagation.C3Field.Hyp.einM_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HarmonicGaugePropagation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.writer_limit_curvature_rate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.contSolution_eq_limit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.continuum_comparison`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterContinuum.sampled_row_consistency`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterContinuumComparison.lean) | PROVED (es10b4) as the implication established by the Lean proof of thm:supp-open-einstein (supp_open_einstein): its hypotheses are only the data class D_s(eps) (DsData, dataSize <= eps <= eps_s); its proof uses the cutoff-uniform nonlinear energy (open_writer_lifespan, top ball), and consistency of the writer (interp_row_small, sampled_row_consistency via interp_cauchy), the harmonic subsidiary estimate in the exact form (C3Field.Hyp.gc_eq_zero: wave-energy uniqueness for the gauge covector) and uniqueness of the limiting reduced Cauchy problem (contSolution_eq_limit); no certification condition (E1)-(E5) of thm:main-renewal-einstein appears among its hypotheses. Same scoped renderings as thm:supp-open-einstein. The remaining sentences of the corollary (certification conditions for the broader class; independent positive population) are interpretive. |
| `lem:supp-open-local-jets` | lemma | Inverse-mesh time jets for a local finite writer | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.supp_open_local_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.local_vector_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.lawField`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.XS`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.norm_le_Xnorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.Xnorm_le_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.fieldF_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.norm_lawField_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.jet_q_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.inverse_time_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.jets_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.lawField_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.iteratedDeriv_clm_eq_vfJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet_proj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IteratedDerivBounds.vfJet_local`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/IteratedDerivBounds.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.GridH.pointwise_derivBound`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevSpace.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.sobNorm_succ_le_inverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridSobolevSpace.lean) | PROVED (Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean, supp_open_local_jets). Encoding: the phase space X^s_h is XS N s = (H^{s+1}_h)^{10} x (H^s_h)^{10} (ten upper components of symmetric records; GridH N r is the grid Sobolev space with norm \|\|.\|\|_{r,h} as a Mathlib normed space), normed by the max of the component norms; this is equivalent to the manuscript norm \|\|X\|\|_{X^s_h} of eq:supp-open-norms with mesh-independent constants (norm_le_Xnorm, Xnorm_le_norm: \|\|X\|\| <= Xnorm <= sqrt 20 \|\|X\|\|). The differential writer is lawField s B X = (v, V_{B,h}(q,v)) with V_{B,h} = lawAccel B (eq:supp-law-family; B = 0 is the open writer eq:main-open-writer with the explicit harmonic source), evaluated on the symmetric records recOf. Statement: for s >= 2 (the manuscript takes s >= 11), every order K >= 1 (the manuscript's 1 <= j <= 9 and 1 <= j <= 8 are K = 9) and every bound b on the mark entries (covers the closed ball of marks), there are delta > 0 and C, independent of N = 1/h and of B, such that on the ball \|\|X\|\| < delta: (1) lawField is C^infinity, \|\|D^j F_{B,h}(X)\|\| <= C h^{-1} (Mathlib operator norm of iteratedFDeriv on X^s_h) for 1 <= j <= K and \|\|F_{B,h}(X)\|\| <= C h^{-1} \|\|X\|\| (eq:supp-open-local-vector-bounds); (2) the vector jets J_1 = F, J_{j+1} = DJ_j F satisfy \|\|(J_j X)_q\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^{1-j} whenever \|\|X\|\| <= eps, and along every exact solution X' = F(X) on an open time set in the ball, at every time t with \|\|X(t)\|\| <= eps, \|\|d_t^j q(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^{1-j} for 1 <= j <= K (eq:supp-open-inverse-time-jets; the q-jet of order j is the v-jet of order j-1, vfJet_proj); (3) locality: J_j(X) on the box of radius rho around any site depends only on X on the box of radius rho + 2j (fixed stencil radius 2 per order, independent of h). Proof as in the manuscript: the writer is decomposed (fieldF_eq) into mesh-uniform smooth blocks (pointwise analytic coefficient maps a^{-1}, c^{ij}, b^i and the compensator with derivative bounds from the discrete Moser estimate, the H^s_h product algebra, D_j^+ : H^{s+1} -> H^s of norm <= 1) composed with operators of norm O(h^{-1}) (D_i^-, D_i^0 on H^s_h, h^2 Lambda_h^2 : H^{s+1} -> H^s, v -> v : H^s -> H^{s+1} by the inverse inequality); the Leibniz rule for iteratedFDeriv then gives one loss h^{-1}. Non-vacuity: the flat history X = 0 is an exact solution in every ball (example at the end of the file). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-open-hermite-residual` | lemma | Complete Taylor--Hermite residual | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.open_hermite_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter_field`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.hermite_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.field`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.qj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.taylorC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.endpoint`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.corr2`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.corr3`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.defectDeriv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.phase`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.exists_exact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.taylor_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.corr_norms`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.exists_solution_mem_closedBall`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.contDiffOn_of_solution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.norm_sub_taylor_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.hermite_residual_full`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean) | PROVED. Concrete statement: Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean, open_hermite_residual: for s >= 2 (manuscript s >= 11), every bound b on the mark entries (B = 0 is the open writer) and 0 < c_-, c_+, there are delta > 0, N_0 and C, independent of h = 1/N and of the mark, such that for N >= N_0 (sufficiently small h), every source X in X^s_h with \|\|X\|\| <= eps <= delta/4 and every step c_- h^2 <= tau <= c_+ h^2, the polynomial Q_X stays in the common chart ((Q_X, Q_X') in the ball) and on the complete interval [0, tau] its actual defect f_X = Q_X'' - V_{B,h}(Q_X, Q_X') satisfies \|\|f_X(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^5, f_X is differentiable with derivative Q_X''' - DV_{B,h}(Q_X,Q_X')[(Q_X',Q_X'')] and \|\|d_t f_X(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^3 (stronger than the manuscript's H^{s-3}_h). Q_X is defined exactly as in the manuscript: Writer.hermiteC X tau = T_X + tau^2 delta_2 H_2(t/tau) + tau^3 delta_3 H_3(t/tau) with T_X the degree-seven Taylor polynomial built from the writer's jets (J_j X)_q (eq:supp-open-local-taylor), delta_2 = V(X_1) - T_X''(tau), delta_3 = J(X_1) - T_X'''(tau); Writer.hermiteC_jets proves its value, velocity, acceleration and jerk are (q, v, V(X), J(X)) at 0 and (X_1, V(X_1), J(X_1)) at tau, and Writer.hermiteC_unique that it is the unique degree <= 7 polynomial with these jets. Proof (Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean, hermite_residual, for any second-order writer F(q,v) = (u v, V(q,v)) with the inverse-mesh bounds Writer.Bounds h A delta, which the grid writer satisfies by lem:supp-open-local-jets, gridWriter_bounds): exact local solution from X by Picard-Lindelof on a time h/(2A) >> tau (exists_exact), its jets are the writer jets (IteratedDerivBounds.iteratedDeriv_clm_vfJet_eq), Taylor remainder \|\|T_X^{(j)} - q^{(j)}\|\| <= C eps h^{-7} tau^{8-j} (eq:supp-open-taylor-remainder), Lipschitz bounds from \|\|DF\|\|, \|\|D^2F\|\| <= A/h, the identities iota delta_2 = -f_T(tau), iota delta_3 = DV(X_1)[(0,-f_T(tau))] - d_t f_T(tau), explicit correction bounds via the scaled Hermite norm equivalence, and c_- h^2 <= tau for the jerk term. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-open-hermite-precision` | lemma | Sobolev-scaled finite precision | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.open_hermite_precision`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.gridWriter_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.hermite_precision`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.norm_ι_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.phase_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.phaseVel_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.defect_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.Bounds.defectDeriv_pert_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.hermite_residual_full`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermite_precision_degree_seven`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.norm_polyCurveDeriv_le_scaled`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean) | PROVED. Concrete statement: Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean, open_hermite_precision: for s >= 2, a mark bound b, 0 < c_-, c_+ and c >= 0 there are delta > 0, N_0, C independent of h = 1/N and of the mark such that for N >= N_0, every source \|\|X\|\| <= eps <= delta/4 (the smaller common chart), every step c_- h^2 <= tau <= c_+ h^2, every degree-seven perturbation dQ (any coefficients d : Fin 8 -> (H^{s+1}_h)^10) of the polynomial Q_X of lem:supp-open-hermite-residual and every b_h >= max_{e in {0,1}, j <= 3} tau^j \|\|dQ^{(j)}(e tau)\|\|_{H^s_h} (eq:supp-open-hermite-precision; equality is allowed), on the whole interval [0,tau]: (1) \|\|dQ^{(j)}(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C_j tau^{-j} b_h with C_j = hermiteConst 3 j (all j, no smallness needed); and if b_h <= c eps tau^2 h^5: (2) Q_X + dQ stays in the chart (local phase tube); (3) the complete residual changes by at most C tau^{-2} b_h and its time derivative by at most C tau^{-3} b_h; (4) hence \|\|f_{Q+dQ}(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^5, f_{Q+dQ} is differentiable and \|\|d_t f_{Q+dQ}(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^3 (the bounds of eq:supp-open-hermite-local-defect are preserved). Abstract form Analysis/TaylorHermitePrecision.lean, hermite_precision, for any second-order writer with the inverse-mesh bounds; proof as in the manuscript: scaled Hermite norm equivalence for clause (1), \|\|delta(Q,Q')\|\| <= C(h^{-1} + tau^{-1}) b_h <= C tau^{-1} b_h since tau/h = O(h), Lipschitz bounds for V and DV from lem:supp-open-local-jets, the velocity bound \|\|(Q_X',Q_X'')\|\| <= C h^{-1} eps of the residual lemma (hermite_residual_full), and b_h <= c eps tau^2 h^5 with tau >= c_- h^2. Non-vacuity: dQ = 0, b_h = 0. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

WriterFiniteWordsEinstein.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterFiniteWords.ofRec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterFiniteWordsEinstein.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterFiniteWords.recOf_ofRec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterFiniteWordsEinstein.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterFiniteWords.exactBank_start`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterFiniteWordsEinstein.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterFiniteWords.exactBank_einstein_hyps`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterFiniteWordsEinstein.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.polyCurve`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.iteratedDeriv_polyCurve`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermiteCoeffs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.jets_hermiteCoeffs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermiteCoeffs_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.eq_of_jets_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.existsUnique_jets_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermiteConst`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.norm_polyCurveDeriv_le_unit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.norm_polyCurveDeriv_le_scaled`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.norm_iteratedDeriv_polyCurve_le_scaled`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermite_precision_degree_seven`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TwoPointHermite.hermiteCorrection_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TwoPointHermiteInterpolation.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.supp_open_local_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLocalJets.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.open_hermite_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLocalJets.open_hermite_precision`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TaylorHermiteResidual.Writer.hermiteC_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TaylorHermiteResidual.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_row_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.sobNorm_le_of_pointwise`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridPointwisePrecision.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicGridSobolev.precConst`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridPointwisePrecision.lean) | PROVED (fD3). Finite construction (Gravity/OpenWriterFiniteWords.lean): a bank sequence beta_j : Fin 4 -> (H^{s+1}_h)^10 (committed value/velocity registers of the j-th source, cached acceleration/jerk) and the chronological bank word bankWord (J = ceil(T h^-2) cells, tau = T/J with min(1/2,T) h^2 <= tau <= h^2, letter j = the degree-7 Hermite polynomial bankPoly of the endpoint banks beta_j, beta_{j+1}, so value/velocity/acceleration/jerk are shared at every node: bankWord_isChronological gives LawWord.IsChronological with JetsMatch ... 3). BankPrecise = the manuscript's precision convention (every scaled endpoint jet tau^l beta within eta pointwise of the jets J_l(X~_j), J_l(X_1) of the local construction Q_{X~_j} of lem:supp-open-hermite-residual). finite_words_bounds (s >= 5, any horizon T > 0, any precision constant c, any mark B of the closed ball incl. the open writer B = 0): for eps <= eps_0(T) and N >= N_0, every bank word with \|\|(Q,Q')(0)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= eps obeying the convention with eta = c eps h^{s+10} has all sources \|\|X~_j\|\| <= C eps, sup_t \|\|(Q_h,Q_h')\|\|_{X^s_h} <= C eps, \|\|f_h^num(t)\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^5, f_h^num differentiable on [0,T] with \|\|d_t f_h^num\|\|_{H^s_h} <= C eps h^3 (eq:supp-open-finite-defects; the derivative even in H^s_h, hence in H^{s-3}_h; the weaker O(eps h^4), O(eps h^2) bounds follow), stage costs c_{h,j} <= C tau (h^10 + h^6) and stopped cost C_h <= C T (h^10 + h^6). Proof = the manuscript's: stage_bounds (open_hermite_residual + open_hermite_precision + sobNorm_le_of_pointwise: b_h <= C eps h^10 <= eps tau^2 h^5 on fine grids) and a stage induction with the forced energy (word_energy_bound, law_forced_energy integrated along the glued C^3 word) which keeps every source in the chart (no first-exit argument is needed since each stage is in the chart by the local bounds). Existence: exactBank (no rounding) satisfies the convention with eta = 0 (exactBank_precise); finite registers: lattice_banks_exist gives, for every tolerance eta > 0, a bank sequence latBank on a lattice (M+1)^-1 Z (rounded value/velocity, rounded acceleration/jerk caches reused at the next source) satisfying the convention, with starting record as close as wished to the given one (finitely many lattice registers at fixed h = finite alphabet). Fixed execution radius: stage_local (the stage polynomial and successor caches on the box of radius rho depend only on the source on the box of radius rho + 20, via jets_local). Strict margin: finite_words_strict_margin (stage cost < M tau h^4 for any fixed M > 0 and C_h <= h^2 on a fine tail). Einstein clause (OpenWriterFiniteWordsEinstein.lean, finite_words_einstein, s >= 6): for (gamma,K) in D_s(eps), arbitrary marks B_n of the closed ball and every sequence of bank words obeying the convention whose starting finite record differs from the compatible preparation (samples of Q0, V0) by <= c0 eps h^4 in X^s_h, the interpolated records converge on every slab [0,T1], T1 <= T, to the same harmonic vacuum development (Q,V,A) as the central writer (supp_law_einstein; it satisfies the normal rows and G(g) = 0) with metric rate O(h eps) in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3}; proved by comparing the word, as a forced law history, with the exact history from the samples (law_difference, lawAccel_diff_bound). Non-vacuity: exactBank_einstein_hyps and the example after it (exact banks satisfy all hypotheses). Renderings disclosed: s >= 5 (bounds) / s >= 6 (Einstein) where the manuscript takes s >= 11; the statement holds for every horizon T > 0 with eps_0 depending on T (the manuscript fixes T < T_s and decreases the initial radius, as here). All cited parents are proved in the ledger (lem:supp-open-hermite-residual, lem:supp-open-hermite-precision, lem:supp-open-local-jets, thm:supp-law-stability, thm:supp-law-einstein, lem:supp-law-cost-control). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

| Proved. New file Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean proves the four order bounds eq:supp-law-orders directly in physical space for the paper's own forward-difference Sobolev norms eq:supp-open-norms (diffSobolevNorm: \|\|u\|\|_{r,h}^2 = sum_{\|alpha\|<=r} \|\|D^alpha u\|\|_h^2 with D^alpha = prod (D_i^+)^{alpha_i}, multiDiff over the multi-index set multiIndices r), no Fourier transform or norm equivalence needed: all forward/backward differences commute and Lambda_h commutes with D^alpha (gridLaplacian_multiDiff), D_i^- is a translate of D_i^+ so \|\|Lambda_h w\|\|^2 <= 3 sum_i \|\|D_i D_i w\|\|^2 without losing h (gridNormSq_gridLaplacian_le), and the already-proved domination eq:supp-law-domination supplies the h-losing steps; per array this gives h^4\|\|Lambda^2 w\|\|^2 <= 144 sum_{ij}\|\|D_j D_i w\|\|^2, <= 36 h^2 sum \|\|D_j D_j D_i w\|\|^2, <= 9 h^4 sum \|\|D_j D_j D_i D_i w\|\|^2 (biharmonic_bound_one/two/three), applied to w = D^alpha q. law_multiplier_bounds (periodic grid Fin 3 -> ZMod N, arrays valued in any real normed space) bundles eq:supp-law-domination for every array w with the four bounds h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-1,h} <= C_s \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-2,h} <= C_s h \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-3,h} <= C_s h^2 \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 v\|\|_{s-3,h} <= C_s h \|\|v\|\|_{s,h} with explicit h- and N-independent constants C_s = 36 sqrt(N_{s-1}), 18 sqrt(N_{s-2}), 9 sqrt(N_{s-3}), 18 sqrt(N_{s-3}) (N_r = #{alpha : \|alpha\| <= r}); exists_uniform_order_constants gives the existential 'C_s independent of h' form. Scoped hypotheses disclosed: h > 0, integer index s >= 3 so that s-1, s-2, s-3 are genuine (the paper applies the lemma with s >= 11; for s < 3 the truncated indices would make the h-gaining bounds false). The Fourier-side symbol facts eq:supp-law-laplacian remain in the earlier file (symbol_bounds, fourth_order_bounds) but are no longer needed for the lemma. |

Statements for marks with \|\|B\|\|_op <= 1/48: the chart certifies the coercivity c_* = 1/2 (quad_lower), so 1/48 = c_*/24 and every admissible ball b_0 <= c_*/24 of the chart is covered (IsMark.mono). (c) eq:supp-law-forced: law_forced_energy: with an additional defect f, d/dt E^B <= 2(E^B)^{1/2}(C(E^B)^{1/2}+C E^B+C\|\|f\|\|_{s,h}) and ((E^B)^{1/2})' <= ... where E^B>0. walGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.accJet_diff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.accJet_size`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.gauge_hasDerivWithinAt_acc`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.riem_moser`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterRateLift.uniform_data_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean) | PROVED. Jet comparison (all four slots j = 0..3) and whole-sequence convergence with the metric rate: supp_law_einstein (OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean) and law_jet_comparison (OpenWriterLawJetLipschitz.lean), as before. NEW (fC2a): the final sentence ('the subsidiary field, rate positivity, cut margin and noncollapse estimates remain uniform') is supp_law_einstein_uniform (Gravity/OpenWriterRateLiftUniform.lean): for s >= 6 there are eps_s, T > 0, T_B >= T and ONE constant C such that for every (gamma,K) in D_s(eps) (DsData, dataSize <= eps <= eps_s) with V0 = prepFun, the central writer (unique on [0,T] from the samples) and, for EVERY mark sequence B_n (IsMark b_n, b_n <= 1/48, no convergence), the law-family solutions (unique on [0,T_B] from the same samples, i.e. those of supp_law_einstein) satisfy at every n and t in [0,T] the package UniformO3 s C eps n: (i) subsidiary field and Einstein residual of g_{B,h} = eta + I_h q_B (2-jet accJet with the actual law acceleration): \|\|c\|\|_{H^{s-2}}, \|\|d_t c\|\|_{H^{s-3}}, \|\|G\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps componentwise (eq:supp-open-subsidiary), with d_t c the actual time derivative (gauge_hasDerivWithinAt_acc); proof: Lipschitz continuum Moser (pjet_moser_fin) on the jet difference accJet(q_B) - interpJet(q_0), bounded by accJet_diff/accJet1_diff through the grid comparison \|\|(q_B - q_0, v_B - v_0)\|\|_{X^{s-2}} + \|\|V_B - V_0\|\|_{s-3} <= C_B h b eps of supp_law_einstein, plus supp_open_subsidiary for the central history (identified on [0,T] by writer_eq_of_small); (ii) rate positivity: at every grid point the A3 rate lift of eq:supp-open-rate-lift for B = N^2 gamma^{-1}, beta^i = gamma^{ij} g_{0j} of eta + q_B(x) (RateLiftAt) satisfies the inversion identities eq:supp-open-rate-inversion (rate_inversion_matrix/shift), lies in the interior cone \|c_a - 1/4\| <= 5/56 and 1/16 <= h^2 k_a^pm <= 3/16 (positive, comparable to h^-2); (iii) noncollapse: N^2, det gamma, rho = sqrt det gamma in [1/2,2] and xi^T gamma xi >= \|xi\|^2/2 at every grid point AND at every point of T^3 for the interpolated metric; (iv) cut margin: for odd N = n+1 (the manuscript's odd periodic regulator) the D3 rate graph (masses rho h^3, symmetrised conductances of the directed rates) has spatial cut constant I^sp >= 1/4096 on every half-volume cut and the full RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen package (L6 Sobolev, Poincare, spectral floor, Weyl count, screen tail; thm:main-spatial-screen) with I = 1/4096, D = 6, V_* = 2 (d3_cut_min, d3_screen: perturbation of the flat D3 regulator via the additive bijection phi(n) = (n0+n1, n0+n2, n1+n2) of the odd grid carrying the A3 coordinate directions to the D3 directions, d3Boundary_eq, and the d-uniform coordinate isoperimetry coordinateGridIsoperimetry); (v) also the uniform curvature bound \|\|Riem(g_{B,h})\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C eps and the jet comparison \|\|accJet(q_B) - interpJet(q_0)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C b_n h eps. All constants independent of the mesh and of the marks. Scoped renderings (disclosed): s >= 6 (manuscript s >= 11); common slab T = min(law-family slab of supp_law_einstein, subsidiary slab); cut margin stated for odd N (for even N the bare D3 graph splits into two parity classes, so the manuscript's odd regulator is necessary); 'noncollapse' rendered as the pointwise lapse/volume/ellipticity bounds plus the screen package; the law histories of supp_law_einstein_uniform and of supp_law_einstein coincide by the uniqueness clauses on [0,T_B]. Non-vacuity: uniform_data_nonvacuous (flat data in D_s(0), zero mark), rateLift_chart example (flat metric, k = 1/(8h^2)). |
