l_ge_nine`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitchingModeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawSwitchingMode.tendsto_cornerSymbol`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitchingModeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawSwitchingMode.cornerOmega`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitchingModeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawSwitchingMode.cornerQuarterPeriod_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitchingModeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawSwitchingMode.cornerRatio_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitchingModeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawSwitchingMode.corner_ratio_separated`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitchingModeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LawSwitchingMode.max_ratio_ge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitchingModeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oscillatorSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oscillatorVelocity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oscillatorSolution_hasDerivAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oscillatorVelocity_hasDerivAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.quarterPeriodTransfer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.quarterPeriodTransfer_mulVec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.quarterPeriodTransfer_two_step`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.two_step_hasEigenvalue`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.two_step_hasEigenvalue_reciprocal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.cornerFrequencySq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.two_step_pow_entry_abs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.switching_amplification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean) | Proved (RapidLawSwitchingModeExact.lean). The dispersion relation is DERIVED, not defined: on the periodic grid (ZMod N)^3, h = 1/N, the grid Fourier mode e_k(n) = exp(2 pi i k.n/N) satisfies Lambda_h e_k = l_h(k) e_k with l_h(k) = 4h^-2 sum_i sin^2(pi h k_i) (gridLaplacian_fourierMode, for the gridLaplacian of SupplementLawMultiplierExact), so the flat linearised law of eq:supp-law-family with B = sigma I, v_t = -Lambda_h q - sigma h^2 Lambda_h^2 q (flatLaw: a = I, c^{ij} = delta^{ij}; the K_b v and G_h terms vanish in the linearisation at flat data, as in the paper's proof 'the flat scalar mode is an oscillator'), acts on e_k by -Omega^2 with Omega^2 = l(1 + sigma h^2 l) (flatLaw_fourierMode = eq:supp-law-dispersion). Writer-oscillator link: (c(t) e_k, c'(t) e_k) with the oscillator c solves the law (modeSolution_isLawSolution), law solutions are unique (lawSolution_unique: Lipschitz linear field + ODE_solution_unique), hence every law solution with data (a e_k, b e_k) is (T *v (a,b)) e_k at the quarter period (law_quarter_transfer), and every switched history (sigma_1 then sigma_2, each held a quarter period, state carried over) is (T2 T1)^n *v s0 after n cycles (law_switching_cycles). Corner on odd grids N = 2m+1, k = (m,m,m): h^2 l = 12 sin^2(pi m/(2m+1)), < 12 (never equal, cornerSymbol_lt_twelve), >= 9, -> 12 (tendsto_cornerSymbol); for distinct admissible marks Omega1^2/Omega2^2 is uniformly separated from 1 for all large m (corner_ratio_separated; limit (1+12 s1)/(1+12 s2) != 1). Assembly rapid_switching_not_included: there are c, C > 0 and m0 such that on every odd grid m >= m0 the two quarter periods sum to <= (pi/2) h, and on any slab [0,T] the floor(T/(tau1+tau2)) full cycles fit in [0,T] and amplify a unit corner-mode datum (e_0 or e_1) to a coefficient \|alpha\| >= C exp(cT/h). Disclosed renderings: admissible interval \|sigma_i\| < 1/24 (the open writer ball \|\|B\|\| < b0 < c_*/24 for the flat chart c = I, c_* = 1); complex-valued mode (the real field is its real part, the law being real); amplitude measured on the transfer-matrix state (field or velocity coefficient), as in the paper's proof. Checklist (a): Omega^2 = l(1+sigma h^2 l) is proved from the law, the transfer matrix is proved to be the solution map, and the corner is reached only in the limit h^2 l -> 12. |
| `lem:supp-law-cost-control` | lemma | A stopped physical cost controls the whole word | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_cost_control`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.IsChronological`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.path`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.recNorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.guardTime`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.stopTime`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.costDensity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.cost`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.residual_contDiffAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawCost_hasDerivAt_Fnorm_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawCostInner`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.abs_lawCostInner_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.flat_isChronological`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.LawWord.flat_cost`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_difference`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.IsLawForcedPair`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawGenEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawGenEnergy_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.hasDerivAt_lawDiffEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_diff_static_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.forcingTerm_bForceAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawForce_sub`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.dAcc_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.lawAccel_diff_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawDifference.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TimeSobolev.sup_le_of_abs_deriv_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TimeSobolevSupBound.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TimeSobolev.sqrt_sup_le_of_abs_deriv_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TimeSobolevSupBound.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TimeSobolev.integral_le_of_integral_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/TimeSobolevSupBound.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SplineGluing.splinePath`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SplineGluing.JetsMatch`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SplineGluing.hasDerivAt_splinePath`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SplineGluing.continuous_splinePath`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_forced_energy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_lifespan`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawFamily.lean) | Proved (law_cost_control). For s >= 5 (manuscript s >= 11) there are eps0, T0 > 0 and a guard radius rho > 0 (the common-energy margin), independent of the mesh h = 1/N, the mark and the word, such that for every T in (0, T0] there is C (depending on T through the one-dimensional Sobolev constant sqrt(1/T + 1) + 1, as in the paper's C_T) with: for every mark B = B^T, \|\|B\|\|_op <= 1/48 (closed ball b0 = c_*/24 with the certified c_* = 1/2, as in thm:supp-law-stability), every chronological word W on [0, T] (LawWord.IsChronological: J >= 1 cells partitioning [0, T], c- h^2 <= tau_j <= c+ h^2, polynomial letters of symmetric records with shared endpoint value, velocity, acceleration and jerk records (JetsMatch .. 3), optional failure index), initial record \|\|(Q,Q')(0)\|\|_{X^s_h} <= eps <= eps0 and 0 <= d <= 1/2 with stopped cost C_h <= d^2 (LawWord.cost = eps^-2 int_0^{t_stop}(\|\|f\|\|_{s,h}^2 + \|\|d_t f\|\|_{s-3,h}^2) + 1_failure, f = Q'' - V_{B,h}(Q,Q'), t_stop = first guard hit \|\|(Q,Q')\|\|_{X^s_h} >= rho or failure node): (i) no failure and no guard exit; (ii) \|\|f\|\|_{L2_t H^s} + \|\|d_t f\|\|_{L2_t H^{s-3}} <= 2 eps d (eq:supp-law-cost-force); (iii) the exact unforced B-history (q_B, v_B) with the same initial record exists and is unique on [0, T0] and for t in [0, T] \|\|(Q - q_B, Q' - q_B')\|\|_{X^{s-1}_h} + \|\|Q'' - V_{B,h}(q_B, q_B')\|\|_{s-3,h} <= C eps d (eq:supp-law-cost-shadow; X^{s-1}_h controls \|\|Q - q_B\|\|_{s,h} and \|\|Q' - q_B'\|\|_{s-1,h}, and q_B'' = V_{B,h}(q_B, q_B') by the ODE). Infrastructure built: the B-family difference estimate at order r <= s-1 with the stencil -h^2 B Lambda^2 dq cancelled through the difference energy (law_difference), the Lipschitz bound of V_{B,h} one order down (lawAccel_diff_bound, dAcc_bound), the one-dimensional Sobolev bound in time (TimeSobolevSupBound), the C^3 gluing of polynomial letters with shared jets (PiecewisePolynomialJetGluing), C^1 regularity of the defect on the analytic chart (residual_contDiffAt). The cost is encoded as the total integral over [0, t_stop] (= sum of the cell costs c_{h,j} by additivity). Non-vacuity: the flat one-letter word with B = 0 (LawWord.flat_isChronological, LawWord.flat_cost, example). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `thm:supp-law-row-neighbourhood` | theorem | A chronological law neighborhood with the same Einstein limit | [`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_row_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_row_einstein_ae`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.expect_totalCost_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.prob_cost_gt_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.prob_lt_le_expect_div`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.odd_cutoff_eventually_not_exceptional`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.isOpen_rowConstraintSet_restrict`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.convex_rowConstraintSet_inter`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.rowConstraintSet_nonempty`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.rowConstraintSet_exists_pos_row`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLifespan.law_cost_control`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawCostControl.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OpenWriterLimitRegularity.supp_law_einstein`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean) | PROVED (fA2a). Probabilistic and convex-geometric parts as before (SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean): for arbitrary history-dependent finite rows satisfying the strict moment condition eq:supp-law-row-moment, E C_h <= M T h^4 and P{C_h > h^2} <= M T h^2 (expect_totalCost_le, prob_cost_gt_le, stopped prefixes carry zero future cost), the strict conditions define nonempty relatively open convex subsets of the product of row simplexes containing strictly positive rows (isOpen_rowConstraintSet_restrict, convex_rowConstraintSet_inter, rowConstraintSet_nonempty, rowConstraintSet_exists_pos_row), and along h = (2m+1)^{-1} the exceptional events eventually do not occur almost surely under any coupling (odd_cutoff_eventually_not_exceptional). NEW: the Einstein clause. supp_law_row_einstein (Gravity/OpenWriterLawEinsteinLimit.lean): for s >= 6, every (gamma, K) in D_s(eps), any marks B_n of the closed ball, any sequence of cutoffs phi(m) -> infinity and chronological words W_m on the grid N = phi(m)+1 (cells c_- h^2 <= tau <= c_+ h^2, shared jets through order three, symmetric letters; LawWord.IsChronological) starting at the samples of the harmonic preparation, with initial record of size <= eps_w <= eps_w0 (the cost normalization): if the stopped physical cost satisfies C_h <= h^2 for m >= m0, then on those cutoffs the word obeys lem:supp-law-cost-control with d_h = h (law_cost_control: no failure, no guard exit, shadowing of the exact B-history at C eps_w h) and its interpolated records I_h Q, I_h Q', I_h Q'' converge uniformly on T^3, for t in [0,T], to the same vacuum development (Q, V, A) as thm:supp-law-einstein (supp_law_einstein; Einstein tensor zero), with rate ((C e^{Kt}(1+t)C_eps + C_B) eps + C_W eps_w) h in H^{s-1}, H^{s-2}, H^{s-3}. supp_law_row_einstein_ae: along the odd cutoffs N = 2m+1, if P{C_h > h^2} <= K h^2 under an arbitrary common coupling (the conclusion eq:supp-law-row-probability), then almost surely the interpolated word records converge to the vacuum development (first Borel-Cantelli). The Lean proofs use only proved statements (supp_law_einstein's convergence clause, law_cost_control); the uniformity sentence of thm:supp-law-einstein (proved separately as supp_law_einstein_uniform) is not needed. Scoped renderings: s >= 6; the identification of the word with the exact B-history uses the uniqueness clauses of law_lifespan/law_cost_control; the abstract WordProcess model of the probabilistic part is linked to the physical cost through the hypothesis on P{C_h > h^2} (which is exactly eq:supp-law-row-probability): the physical stage cost c_{h,j}(pi,e) is an instance of the abstract prefix/letter cost of WordProcess and a common coupling P of the cutoffs has the word path laws as marginals, so prob_cost_gt_le supplies that hypothesis; this marginal identification is bookkeeping and is not itself formalised. |
| `prop:supp-law-transport` | proposition | Physical profile transport certifies a row neighborhood | [`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.W2`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.IsCoupling`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.weighted_sqrt_sum_add_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.sqrt_row_cost_le_sqrt_add_W2`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.row_moment_of_W2_lt`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.W2_le_sqrt_cost_add_totalVariation`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.isOpen_W2_ball_restrict`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean) | W2 d K P is defined as the infimum over couplings (nonnegative pi with marginals K, P) of sqrt(sum pi d^2), profiles Phi e in an arbitrary normed group (the paper's L^2 profile Hilbert space is an instance), c_e = \|\|Phi e\|\|^2, d = \|\|Phi e - Phi e'\|\|. sqrt_row_cost_le_sqrt_add_W2 is eq:supp-law-transport-bound for all rows K, P in the simplex (Minkowski in weighted l^2 under each coupling, then infimum; no minimiser needed). row_moment_of_W2_lt: sum P c <= M0 tau h^4 and W2 < gamma sqrt(tau) h^2 give sum K c < M tau h^4 for every M > (sqrt M0 + gamma)^2 (tau, h > 0). Relative openness of the strict balls {K in simplex : W2(K,P) < rho}: proved (isOpen_W2_ball_restrict) via an explicit coupling perturbation giving the upper-semicontinuity bound W2(K',P)^2 <= cost(pi0) + D * TV(K',K0) for every coupling pi0 of (K0,P) and D >= max d^2. No compactness argument is needed. |
| `thm:supp-chronological-thomson` | theorem | Chronological source-shorted Thomson correction | [`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thm_supp_chronological_thomson`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_L_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomsonMatrix_posSemidef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_variance_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_solvable_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_min_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomson_leverage`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.sourceShort_isOrthogonalProjection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.inner_srcAdj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.inner_tgtAdj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.thomsonPinv_isPenroseInverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.IsPenroseInverse.unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalThomson.exists_isPenroseInverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ChronologicalThomsonExact.lean) | Finite X, Y; faithful alpha > 0; Q >= 0 with unit row sums; R = alpha Q; admitted support E = {Q > 0} = {R > 0} (the only support on which \|\|.\|\|_{R^-1} is defined and on which Q lives); edge arrays = functions X -> Y -> R vanishing off E (SupportedOn). Built on SameCylinderTransferExact (thomsonOperator, thomsonCorrection, invWeightNormSq). Proved: weighted adjoints A* f = R f(x), B* g = R g(y) (inner_srcAdj, inner_tgtAdj); P_s = I - A* diag(alpha)^-1 A is the R^-1-orthogonal projection onto ker A (sourceShort_isOrthogonalProjection); B P_s B* = diag(rho) - Q^T diag(alpha) Q as operator and matrix (thomson_L_eq) and it is PosSemidef (eq:supp-thomson-L); <u, L u> = sum_x alpha(x) Var_{Q(x,.)}(u) (eq:supp-thomson-variance); A h = 0, B h = d solvable by an edge array iff d in Ran L (Penrose projector I - L L^+ plus orthogonality of feasible d to ker L); L^+ = thomsonPinv, the real Moore-Penrose inverse built from the real spectral theorem, satisfying all four Penrose equations, and IsPenroseInverse.unique shows it is THE Moore-Penrose inverse; with u = L^+ d, h_* = R(u(y) - Qu(x)) is feasible, every feasible h has \|\|h\|\|^2 = \|\|h_*\|\|^2 + \|\|h - h_*\|\|^2, so h_* is the unique minimum-R^-1-norm solution (eq:supp-thomson-hstar), and \|\|h_*\|\|^2 = <d, L^+ d> (eq:supp-thomson-energy); if \|u(y) - Qu(x)\| <= 1/2 on E then R + h_* >= 0 everywhere with source marginal alpha and target marginal beta, d = beta - rho. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `lem:supp-same-cylinder-transfer` | lemma | Variance-sensitive source-preserving transfer | [`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.abs_sum_mul_le_of_source_null`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.thomson_transfer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.variance_le_bound_mul_mean`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.thomsonCorrection_source_null`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.invWeightNormSq_thomsonCorrection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SameCylinderTransfer.occurrence_energy_well_defined`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean) | Proved (SameCylinderTransferExact.lean). Finite X, Y; edge arrays on X x Y supported where R > 0 (the admitted support), \|\|h\|\|^2_{R^-1} = sum h^2/R, V_R(c) = sum R (c - Qc(x))^2 (eq:supp-same-cylinder-variance). (1) abs_sum_mul_le_of_source_null: A h = 0, R >= 0, h supported in {R > 0} => \|sum h c\| <= \|\|h\|\|_{R^-1} V_R(c)^{1/2} (eq:supp-same-cylinder-transfer), for arbitrary R, Q. (2) thomson_transfer: with R = alpha Q (alpha >= 0, stochastic Q), L = diag(rho) - Q^T diag(alpha) Q and h_* = R(u(y) - Qu(x)) for ANY u with L u = d: \|E_{R+h_*} c - E_R c\| <= sqrt(<d,u> V_R(c)) (eq:supp-same-cylinder-transfer-thomson). The paper's choice u = L^+ d (d in Ran L) is covered: ChronologicalThomson.thomsonPinv is the real Moore-Penrose inverse (thomsonPinv_isPenroseInverse, IsPenroseInverse.unique) and pinv_solves gives L (L^+ d) = d, while occurrence_energy_well_defined shows <d,u> is independent of the solution, so <d,u> = <d, L^+ d> = E_occ (eq:main-occurrence-energy). (3) variance_le_bound_mul_mean: 0 <= c <= B => V_R(c) <= B E_R c. Nonnegativity/marginals of pi^T = R + h_* under the leverage bound (needed for pi^T to be a coupling, not for the inequality) are proved in thm:supp-chronological-thomson (thomson_leverage). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `thm:supp-exact-constraint-rank` | theorem | Finite characteristic quotient on a regular one-null branch | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.supp_exact_constraint_rank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.euler_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.hasGradientAt_X_eq_adjoint`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.bracket`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.source`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.bracket_lambda_eq_source`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.multiplierHessian_symm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.adjoint_eq_self_of_symm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.constraintMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.characteristicMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.constraintMap_comp_characteristicMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.constraintMap_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.finrank_ker_constraintMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.adjoint_injective_of_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.characteristicMap_injective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.characteristicDirections`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.characteristicDirections_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.charQuotient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.finrank_charQuotient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.supp_exact_constraint_rank_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.diracMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.ker_diracMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.finrank_ker_diracMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.finrank_range_diracMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.finrank_range_diracMatrix_of_dim`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean) | Proved (ExactConstraintRankEulerQuotient.lean, on top of the block-matrix kernel/rank lemmas of SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean). X in E, lambda in L finite-dimensional real inner-product spaces (dim E = 12V, dim L = 4V), H : E -> L -> R with H(X, c mu) = c H(X, mu) for c > 0 (eq:supp-exact-homogeneity), P = grad_lambda H (HasGradientAt at lambda for every X, and near lambda at the base point X0), D_X P the X-derivative of P(., lambda) at X0 (onto), M = D_lambda P at (X0, lambda), B = D_X P (J grad_X H) with grad_X H Mathlib's gradient (source), A = D_X P J (D_X P)^* (bracket). Derived, not assumed: Euler H = <lambda, P> (euler_identity), grad_X H = (D_X P)^* lambda (hasGradientAt_X_eq_adjoint), hence A lambda = B (bracket_lambda_eq_source); M self-adjoint as the lambda-Hessian (multiplierHessian_symm via second_derivative_symmetric). Quotient by rank-nullity: on the extended phase E x L x L (dim 20V) the linearised primary+secondary constraint map (xi, eta, zeta) -> (zeta, D_X P xi + M eta) is onto because D_X P is onto, so its kernel has dim E = 12V (finrank_ker_constraintMap); the Hamiltonian field of <r, mu> + <s, P>, (r, s) -> (J (D_X P)^* s, r, -M^* s), composes with the constraint map to the Dirac matrix (constraintMap_comp_characteristicMap, uses M = M^*) and is injective (J injective, D_X P onto), so it embeds the 2-dimensional ker D as the characteristic directions inside the linearised constraint surface; finrank(charQuotient) + 2 = dim E (finrank_charQuotient). supp_exact_constraint_rank assembles: A lambda = B, ker D = span{(lambda, 0), (lamDot, lambda)} for any solution of B + M lamDot = 0 (the paper's -M^dagger B up to span{lambda}), rank D = 8V - 2, dim charQuotient = 12V - 2. Disclosed: J is any injective operator on E (the canonical J(u, pi) = (pi, -u) is); V enters only through dim E = 12V, dim L = 4V. Non-vacuity: supp_exact_constraint_rank_nonvacuous (E = C, L = R, H = lambda Re X, J = i, D_X P = Re onto, M = 0). Checklist (j): 12V - 2 and 8V - 2 come from finrank computations (rank-nullity on the actual maps); (l): A lambda = B is derived from homogeneity; uses 'D_X P onto'. |
| `thm:supp-exact-transverse` | theorem | Exact all-cutoff transverse rank and natural-order bounds | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.transverseSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.finrank_transverseSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.vecNormSq_curlMode_of_transverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.frobSq_offDiag_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.frobSq_offDiag_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.sourceMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.sourceMode_frobSq_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.source_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.source_bounds_weighted`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.trace_sourceMode`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.finrank_range_source`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.dewittInvK0_of_trace_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.gram_signed_eq_half`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.gram_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean) | Formalised in the Fourier-mode representation, which is how the paper defines delta_i, Omega_h, T_h and A_h (eq:supp-exact-phase-derivative, eq:supp-exact-transverse-space; eq:supp-exact-transverse-source taken as the definition of A_h): K = finite set of modes (the V-1 nonzero modes), kappa k in R^3 real symbols with the hypothesis kappa k != 0 for all k in K (for kappa_i(k) = 2h^-1 sin(pi h k_i) on an odd grid this holds exactly for k != 0; the trigonometric instance is not formalised), sigma k unimodular shift symbols (hypothesis \|sigma_k,i\| = 1), varkappa_h a scalar with 0 <= varkappa_lo <= varkappa_h <= varkappa_hi, shifts = complex mode families b : K -> C^3, transversality kappa(k).b(k) = 0 (zero mode absent). Proved: dim_C T_h = 2\|K\| (complex dimension of complex mode families; equals the real dimension 2(V-1) of real transverse fields by Hermitian symmetry); Lagrange identity \|curl_delta beta\| = \|Omega_h beta\| on transverse modes; off-diagonal map Gram form 2(\|u\|^2 + \|u1+u2+u3\|^2), 2\|w\|^2 <= \|\|L_h w\|\|_F^2 <= 8\|w\|^2; boxed bound c h^2 \|\|Omega_h beta\|\|^2 <= \|\|A_h beta\|\|^2 <= C h^2 \|\|Omega_h beta\|\|^2 with c = varkappa_lo^2/8, C = varkappa_hi^2/2, also with any nonnegative scalar Fourier weight (source_bounds_weighted); P_tr A_h = 0 (trace_sourceMode); rank A_h = 2\|K\| (finrank_range_source, by injectivity); <A beta, K0^-1 A beta> = 1/2 \|\|A beta\|\|^2 (gram_signed_eq_half; the operator identity A^*K0^-1A = 1/2 A^*A follows by polarisation) and \|\|A beta\|\|^2 > 0 for nonzero transverse beta (gram_pos). Not formalised: the DFT/Parseval identification of grid l^2 norms and grid operators with these mode sums and symbols. |
| `cor:supp-exact-transverse-elimination` | corollary | Lossless elimination of the transverse source columns | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.transverse_elimination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.finrank_range_coprod`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.gram_blockUnit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.range_coprod_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.coprod_comp_blockUnit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.elimCoeff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.blockUnit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.transverseSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.signedGramMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.signedGramMap_isSymmetric`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.signedGramMap_transverseSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.finrank_range_transverseSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.inner_toEuc`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.finrank_range_source`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactTransverse.gram_signed_eq_half`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LosslessColumnElimination.rank_fromCols_eq_add_rank_residualCols`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/LosslessColumnElimination.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LosslessColumnElimination.blockUnit_congruence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/LosslessColumnElimination.lean) | Proved, derived from the parent thm:supp-exact-transverse in its mode representation (Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseExact.lean). New file Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean: (1) abstract elimination for linear maps A : E -> H, G : Y -> H into a finite-dimensional complex inner product space: Pi_A = orthogonal projection onto range A (Mathlib starProjection), Gbar = (I - Pi_A) G (residual), C = A^{-1} Pi_A G for injective A (elimCoeff), U(e,y) = (e - C y, y) a linear automorphism (blockUnit); F U = [A Gbar] (coprod_comp_blockUnit), range [A Gbar] = range F (range_coprod_residual: the column transformation leaves the actual target unchanged), rank F = rank A + rank Gbar (finrank_range_coprod, eq:supp-exact-rank-split) and for symmetric K with K A = A/2 the congruence <F U x, K F U x'> = 1/2 <A e, A e'> + <Gbar y, K Gbar y'> (gram_blockUnit, eq:supp-exact-Gram-split). (2) Instantiation: toEuc identifies mode families K -> Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C with EuclideanSpace C (K x Fin 3 x Fin 3), whose inner product is the summed Frobenius pairing (inner_toEuc); transverseSource = A_h restricted to the transverse space; signedGramMap = K0^{-1} = 1/2 I - 3/4 P_tr mode-wise, proved symmetric (signedGramMap_isSymmetric) and acting by 1/2 on the trace-free range of A_h (signedGramMap_transverseSource); rank A_h = 2\|K\| transported from the parent (finrank_range_transverseSource). transverse_elimination states, for every remaining source block G_h : Y -> H: rank F_h = 2\|K\| + rank Gbar_h, F_h U = [A_h Gbar_h] with equal range, and the signed-Gram congruence. Scoping inherited from the parent only: grid operators represented by their Fourier modes, \|K\| = V - 1 nonzero modes, so 2\|K\| = 2(V-1); the remaining source G_h is an arbitrary linear map (the paper does not specify it beyond 'the complete leading graded source'). |
| `thm:supp-exact-scalar-reconstruction` | theorem | Exact scalar--trace response reconstruction | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.dewittK`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.dewittKinv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.dewittK_mul_dewittKinv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.sourceProj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.sourceResponse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.traceCompression`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.traceOp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.traceSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.traceResponseVec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.signedGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.reconstructed`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.IsResponse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.signedGram_isUnit_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.response_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.reconstructed_isResponse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.dotProduct_reconstructed`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.response_norm_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.response_tendsto_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean) | Proved as finite matrix algebra. Setting: physical space n -> R, full-rank source F : Matrix n m R (Gram F^T F invertible, hypothesis hG) with target b, trace isometry E : Matrix n k R with E^T E = 1 (the paper's E tau = tau I/sqrt3), P_tr = E E^T, DeWitt K0 = 2(I - 3 P_tr) with K0^{-1} = (2I - 3P_tr)/4 (dewittK_mul_dewittKinv). Objects Pi = F (F^T F)^{-1} F^T, w = F(F^T F)^{-1} b, C = E^T Pi E, H = 2I - 3C, zeta = E^T w, chi = H^{-1} zeta (Matrix.inv), signed Gram F^T K0^{-1} F. signedGram_isUnit_iff: signed Gram invertible iff H invertible (via Sylvester det(1 - AB) = det(1 - BA); the explicit determinant identity signedGram_det). response_eq (eq:supp-exact-scalar-v): every v with F^T v = b and K0 v in Ran F (IsResponse) satisfies E^T v = -chi and v = w - 3(I - Pi) E chi; reconstructed_isResponse gives existence. dotProduct_reconstructed (eq:supp-exact-scalar-norm): \|v\|^2 = \|w\|^2 + 9 <chi,(I - C) chi>. response_tendsto_zero_iff (eq:supp-exact-response-equivalence): along any filter-indexed family with index-dependent dimension types, \|v_h\|^2 -> 0 iff \|w_h\|^2 -> 0 and \|H_h^{-1} E^* w_h\|^2 -> 0 (from the pointwise bounds response_norm_bounds). Scoped hypotheses: H invertible (regular signed Gram) is the hypothesis hH; the response is characterized by F^T v = b and K0 v in Ran F as in the paper's proof. |
| `prop:supp-exact-clock-test` | proposition | Necessary lapse--shift clock condition for harmonic shadowing | [`RenewalGeometry.exact_clock_test_grid`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.lapseShiftJet_gridNorm_tendsto_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.site_jet_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gridNorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gridNorm_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gridNorm_lapseShiftJet_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.rate_norm_grid`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.sumSq_add_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.weakPart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.activePart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.activePart_add_weakPart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.norm_sq_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.scale`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.activePart_scale`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.weakPart_scale`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.normalizedSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AmplitudeGrading.rate_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.lapseShiftJet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.lapseShiftJet_norm_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.admMetric00`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.admMetric0i`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.admMetric00_hasDerivAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.admMetric0i_hasDerivAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.lapse_shift_clock_condition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.sumSq_le_of_coercive`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftFirstJetShadowing.lean) | Proved on growing site sets (ExactClockTestGridNorm.lean). Along any filter l on a regulator index i, with index-dependent finite site types V i, cell weights w i > 0 (the paper's h^3) and grid norm \|\|v\|\|_h = sqrt(w) \|\|v\|\| on EuclideanSpace R (V i x Fin 4) (so \|\|lambda_t\|\|_h^2 = w sum_x ((d_t N)^2 + \|d_t beta\|^2), gridNorm_lapseShiftJet_sq): if at the initial cut N = N' = 1, beta = beta' = 0, the spatial metric is shared and uniformly coercive (gamma >= c with c independent of i and x), the shadowing holds in the grid norm (w sum_x (Delta d_t g00)^2 -> 0 and w sum_x sum_j (Delta d_t g0j)^2 -> 0) and the harmonic lapse-shift first jet is small in the grid norm, then \|\|lambda_t\|\|_h -> 0 (lapseShiftJet_gridNorm_tendsto_zero, from the per-site estimate site_jet_sq_le: (d_t N)^2 + \|d_t beta\|^2 <= 1/2 (Delta d_t g00)^2 + 2c^-2 sum_j (Delta d_t g0j)^2 + 2((d_t N')^2 + \|d_t beta'\|^2), summed with the weight), and for every family of active/weak gradings G i, a^-1 \|\|(u_a)_A\|\|_h -> 0 and a^-2 \|\|(u_a)_W\|\|_h -> 0 (exact_clock_test_grid, via rate_norm_grid = eq:supp-exact-rate-norm in the grid norm). No electric coefficient enters. Disclosed modelling (inherited from the earlier files): 'small harmonic metric' = small lapse-shift first jet in the grid norm with shared coercive spatial metric; the shadowing topology is the initial first time derivative of g00, g0i in the grid norm. Non-vacuity example in the file with V i = Fin (i+1), w i = 1/(i+1), N = 1 + t/(i+1). Checklist (i): general index-dependent site family, conclusion in the aggregate grid norm. |
| `lem:supp-exact-normal-coordinates` | lemma | Exact nonlinear constraint coordinates | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalCoordinates.supp_exact_normal_coordinates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalCoordinatesChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalCoordinates.exact_normal_coordinates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalCoordinatesChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalCoordinates.exact_normal_chart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalCoordinatesChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalCoordinates.normalChart_bijective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalCoordinatesChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalCoordinates.normalChart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalCoordinatesChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalCoordinates.canonicalComponent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalCoordinatesChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalCoordinates.normal_solve_norm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalCoordinatesChart.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AnalyticImplicit.analytic_implicit_function`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/AnalyticImplicitFunction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AnalyticImplicit.implicit_fderiv_comp_eq_smul`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/AnalyticImplicitFunction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.AnalyticImplicit.analyticAt_implicitFunctionOfProdDomain`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/AnalyticImplicitFunction.lean) | ass:supp-graded-exact-chart (C1)-(C2) enter as hypotheses (ledger convention for that assumption), on arbitrary real Banach spaces (paper: R^108 normal/rows, R^216 q, R^78 x R^29 p, R^324 canonical). (C2): the divided map P((a,w),n) (argument order only changed) analytic at 0, P((a,0),0)=0 near a=0, P((0,w),n) = C n with C = C_0 J_c a linear isomorphism; 'D_qP = O(a), D_pP = O(a^2) with these factors preserved by the fixed-order derivatives' is rendered as the analytic divisibilities D_qP = a Pq, D_pP = a^2 Pp near 0 with Pq, Pp analytic (the paper: 'These are analytic divisibilities'); the analytic prepared record a -> X^0_a analytic; the relation to the original constraints: for a != 0, P_h(Theta(a,n,w)) = 0 <-> P((a,w),n) = 0 (row division by a^2, a^3). (C1): C_0 E = 0, E onto ker C_0 (the dimension count 216 = 324-108), Lambda E = I, Lambda J_c = 0, C_0 J_c = C. supp_exact_normal_coordinates proves: an analytic n(a,w) with n(0)=0, P_h(Theta_a(w)) = 0 for a != 0 near 0, n(a,0)=0, \|\|n\|\| <= K(\|a\|\|q\| + a^2\|p\|) (stronger than the paper's O(a\|q\|+a\|q\|^2+a^2\|p\|)), \|\|D_q n\|\| <= K\|a\|, \|\|D_p n\|\| <= K a^2, and for the canonical component X = X^0_a + a^2(Eq + J_c n): \|\|D_qX\|\| <= K a^2, \|\|D_pX\|\| <= K a^4, \|\|D_q^2 X\|\| <= K\|a\|^3, \|\|D_qD_pX\|\|, \|\|D_p^2 X\|\| <= K a^4, uniformly on a neighbourhood of (a,w)=0 (partial derivatives = fderiv composed with the q-/p-inclusions). Chart clause: Theta(a,.,.) is an affine bijection for a != 0 with the explicit inverse (free projection q = Lambda(X-X^0)/a^2, affine multiplier formulas), w -> Theta_a(w) is injective, and every original-constraint solution Theta(a,n,w) with (a,n,w) near 0 equals Theta_a(w): a coordinate chart on the constraint leaf near the prepared record; the dimension 323 is that of the free space W and enters only through the concrete sizes. Proof: analytic implicit function theorem (new, from Mathlib's analytic inverse of an open partial homeomorphism), divisibility transfer D_q n = a G, D_p n = a^2 H with G, H analytic (implicit_fderiv_comp_eq_smul), mean value inequality. Non-vacuity example: the linear packet P((a,w),n) = n satisfies all (C2) hypotheses. |

ometry.LyapunovPerron.uc_compact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.third_err_late`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.third_err_early`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.traj2_littleODefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.traj2_zero_contDiff_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.graphMap_contDiff_three`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.graphC3Hyp_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.lip_of_hasFDerivAt_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovPerron.invariantGraph_contDiff_three_of_exp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean) | PROVED (fD2). Setting and earlier layers as before: GraphHyp (j = 1, Lyapunov-Perron fixed point, graph h = graphMap, Lipschitz, forward invariance yStar_mem_graph, ODE/flow invariance flow_mem_graph, localization flow_mem_graph_of_local; the manuscript's propagators e^{tA_u}, e^{tA_n} are an instance by graphHyp_of_exp), C^1 layer (LyapunovPerronGraphDifferentiable.lean, j = 2 inequality) and C^2 layer (LyapunovPerronGraphSecondOrder.lean, j = 3 inequality, 3nu < beta, D^2N Lipschitz = bounded D^3N). NEW C^3 layer under exactly the manuscript's regularity hypothesis (C^3 field with bounded derivatives through order three: D^3N continuous and bounded, NOT Lipschitz; no 4nu weight): (1) Analysis/LyapunovPerronTrajectoryDerivatives.lean: the quadratic-defect machinery applied to the value maps n0 -> y*(n0)(t) at every time t >= 0 (trajAt, clamped to max t 0) gives first and second derivatives Y1(t) = traj1, Y2(t) = traj2 with explicit Taylor/Lipschitz/norm bounds in the weights e^{nu t}, e^{2nu t}, e^{3nu t} (trajAt_quadDefect, second_level_traj_le, traj1_quadDefect), continuity in t (traj1_apply_continuous, traj2_apply_continuous, via continuous_of_rate_approx), and the first and second variational equations Y1(t)d = (0, P_n(t)d) + S[DN(y*) Y1 d](t) (traj1_eq) and Y2(t)ed = S[DN(y*) Y2 e d + D^2N(y*)(Y1 e)(Y1 d)](t) (traj2_eq), S = srcOp the Lyapunov-Perron integral operator; obtained without limits inside integrals (explicit rates transported by srcOp_norm_le). (2) Analysis/LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean: general criterion contDiff_one_of_littleODefect (a Lipschitz map with locally uniform little-o three-point defects is C^1); for Sum a_i v_i = 0, Z = Sum a_i (Y2(n+v_i) - Y2(n)) e d solves the linearised LP equation with the third-level source thirdSrc whose part linear in v (linThird) cancels (linThird_comb); third_level_le is the a priori bound in any admissible weight kappa >= 3nu; exists_weight_gt picks kappa in (3nu, beta) with q_kappa < 1 (strict j = 3 inequality); third_err_le gives the crude bound O(\|v\| e^{3nu r}) and the fine bound O(\|v\|^2 e^{4nu r}) + omega(D^3N) O(\|v\| e^{3nu r}) (thirdErr_crude_le/thirdErr_fine_le, taylor_modulus_le); traj2_littleODefect splits time into [0,T] (Heine-Cantor uniform continuity of D^3N at the compact set y*(n0)([0,T]), uc_compact, third_err_early) and [T, inf) (factor e^{-(kappa-3nu)T}, third_err_late) - the assessor's plan; hence D^2(y*(.)(0)) is C^1 and graphMap_contDiff_three: the graph is C^3. invariantGraph_contDiff_three_of_exp states the lemma in the manuscript's formulation: propagators e^{tA_u}, e^{tA_n} with \|\|e^{-sA_u}\|\| <= M e^{-beta s}, \|\|e^{sA_n}\|\| <= M e^{alpha s}, 0 < nu, alpha < nu, 3nu < beta, N with derivatives DN, D^2N, D^3N (D^3N continuous) bounded by L, L2, L3, and the three inequalities j = 1,2,3; conclusion: the forward-weighted-trajectory graph is C^3 and forward invariant (localization clause: flow_mem_graph_of_local). Rendering: Banach spaces U, Nn (the paper's finite-dimensional spaces are a special case; no compactness of the state space is used, only of trajectory segments); the nonlinearity's derivatives are given as explicit maps DN, D^2N, D^3N with HasFDerivAt (equivalent to C^3 with these derivatives). Non-vacuity: graphC3Hyp_nonvacuous (plus graphHyp_nonvacuous, graphC1Hyp_nonvacuous, graphC2Hyp_nonvacuous). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. (LyapunovPerronGraphThirdOrder.lean sets maxSynthPendingDepth 3 so that trilinear maps E ->L E ->L E ->L G get their norm instance.) |
| `prop:supp-exact-normal-growth` | proposition | Rapid Lyapunov growth and slow scalar growth | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_normal_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_normal_rapid_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_normal_slow_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.scalar_growth_of_rate_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixSpectralDecay.matrix_lyapunov_exponential_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MatrixSpectralDecay.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixSpectralDecay.exists_norm_exp_neg_smul_le_half`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MatrixSpectralDecay.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixSpectralDecay.tendsto_norm_exp_neg_pow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MatrixSpectralDecay.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixSpectralDecay.mem_spectrum_exp_neg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/MatrixSpectralDecay.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.lyapunov_exponential_growth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.two_mul_lyapunovGramian_inner_apply`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.lyapunovGramian_inner_self_ge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.le_exp_mul_of_hasDerivWithinAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LyapunovGrowth.exp_mul_le_of_hasDerivWithinAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LyapunovExponentialGrowth.lean) | ass:supp-exact-normal (N1)-(N2) enter as hypotheses (ledger convention for that assumption). (N1): rapid-time normal v : R -> EuclideanSpace R k with v' = N(s) v on [0,S] (N(s) = N_a(u(s),n(s)) along the history; derivatives within [0,S], i.e. until chart exit), J_u a real k x k matrix whose complex spectrum (spectrum C of J.map ofReal) lies in Re > 0, and \|\|N(s) - J_u\|\| <= eps (L2 operator norm) with eps the smallness produced by the theorem ('sufficiently small'). exact_normal_rapid_growth: exists eps,c,C,mu>0 depending only on J_u such that for every a>0 and physical t in [0,a^3 S]: c e^{mu t/a^3}\|v(0)\| <= \|v(t/a^3)\| <= C e^{C t/a^3}\|v(0)\|. Proof: Gelfand formula gives \|\|e^{-TJ}\|\| <= 1/2 for some T>0 (spectral inclusion spec(e^{-J}) subset e^{-spec J} via a common eigenvector), then the finite-horizon Lyapunov Gramian W = int_0^T e^{-rJ*}e^{-rJ} dr satisfies 2<Wv,Jv> = \|v\|^2 - \|e^{-TJ}v\|^2 >= 3/4\|v\|^2 (the paper's infinite-horizon W with J*W+WJ=I is replaced by this finite-horizon form; same argument), and q = <Wv,v> grows exponentially; upper bound by Gronwall on \|v\|^2. (N2): slow-time scalar omega_tau = (lambda_+(a) + r(tau)) omega with \|lambda_+(a)-gamma\| <= K a for 0<a<=a_0 (the O(a)) and \|r\| <= gamma/4; exact_normal_slow_growth: after reducing a_0, e^{(gamma/2) t/a}\|omega(0)\| <= \|omega(t/a)\| <= e^{2 gamma t/a}\|omega(0)\| (mu_s = gamma/2). exact_normal_growth bundles both. Scalar omega taken real-valued, normals in a Euclidean norm (any norm changes only constants). |
| `thm:main-exact-residence` | theorem | Two necessary normal precision scales for local residence | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.main_exact_residence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_rapid_residence_width`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.exact_slow_residence_width`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.residence_width_eventually_lt_pow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactNormalResidence.tendsto_exp_neg_div_div_pow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactNormalResidenceScales.lean) | Under the certificate ass:supp-exact-normal encoded as in prop:supp-exact-normal-growth, plus the paper's two comparison inputs: initial physical normal displacement delta(0) <= K a \|v(0)\| (resp. K a \|omega(0)\|; upper half of 'uniformly comparable') and residence through physical time T keeping the terminal normal in the certified chart radius (\|v(T/a^3)\| <= R_r, \|omega(T/a)\| <= R_s). main_exact_residence proves: (1) delta_r(0) <= C_r a exp(-mu_r T/a^3) (eq:main-exact-rapid-width); (2) for a <= a_1, delta_s(0) <= C_s a exp(-(gamma/2) T/a) (eq:main-exact-slow-width, mu_s = gamma/2); (3) for T>0, every power m and C_0>0 both widths are eventually (a -> 0+) strictly below C_0 a^m, i.e. no resident tube has algebraic thickness. The nesting D_a subset S_a subset P_a with dimensions 311/312/323 is the content of the certificate ass:supp-exact-normal (its graphs) and of the 323-dimensional constraint leaf; it is carried as part of the assumption and not re-derived (the paper's proof likewise only subtracts 11 and 1 normal coordinates). Depends only on prop:supp-exact-normal-growth, proved in the same batch. Non-vacuity examples: the spectral hypothesis holds for J = 1 and the slow factor for omega = e^{gamma tau}. |

other records using that assumption. Encoding: slow space R^294 -> Xs, weak space R^29 -> W (arbitrary real Banach spaces), R_s, R_f : R x (Xs x W) -> Xs, W analytic at (a,v) = 0 with the value bounds \|R_s\| <= C(a^4 + a^2\|v\| + a\|v\|^2), \|R_f\| <= C(a^4 + a\|v\| + \|v\|^2) for 0 < a < delta, \|v\| < delta (the derivative bounds of eq:supp-exact-graded-remainders are not needed). Mixed partials at (0,0) are read off the iterated Frechet derivative along e = (1,0) and (0,xi): D_a^2D_vR_s[xi] = D^3R_s(0)[e,e,(0,xi)] etc. (slot order irrelevant by symmetry of iterated derivatives of analytic maps, Mathlib ContDiffAt.iteratedFDeriv_comp_perm). sigma0, phi1 are exactly the paper's formulas eq:supp-exact-slow-coefficients; Sigma_1(a,xi) = a^-4(R_s(a,a xi) - a^3 Sigma_0(xi)), Phi_2(a,xi) = a^-3(R_f(a,a xi) - a^2 Phi_1(xi)); Phi_0 = ExactSlowBranch.leadingConstraint (C_red x + B z). slow_expansion proves: (1) R_s(a,a xi) = a^3 Sigma_0 + a^4 Sigma_1, R_f(a,a xi) = a^2 Phi_1 + a^3 Phi_2; (2) Sigma_1, Phi_2 uniformly bounded on every ball \|xi\| <= rho for 0 < a < a_0(rho) (the real content: it forces Sigma_0, Phi_1 to be the paper's coefficients, via sigma0_eq_coeff / phi1_eq_coeff: the graded bounds kill the Taylor coefficients of orders < 3 (resp. < 2) along every ray (1,xi), kill D_a^3R_s(0) (from R_s(a,0) = O(a^4)), D_v^3R_s(0) (R_s(0,v) = 0 by continuity) and D_a^2R_f(0), and the symmetric trilinear/bilinear expansion leaves exactly 1/2 D_a^2D_v + 1/2 D_aD_v^2, resp. D_aD_v + 1/2 D_v^2); (3) the substitution v = a xi, s = tau/a^2 maps the unbalanced field eq:supp-exact-unbalanced-field exactly onto the slow system eq:main-exact-slow-system, as vector fields (slowField_eq: slowField a xi = a^-3 unbalancedField a (a xi)) and along solutions on [0, T/a^2] -> [0, T] (slow_system_of_unbalanced, HasDerivWithinAt on closed intervals); (4) Sigma_0(0) = Phi_1(0) = 0 and both are linear + quadratic (explicit LinearMap and bilinear map), i.e. degree <= 2; (5) Sigma_0(xi), Phi_1(xi) are the leading Taylor coefficients p_3((1,xi)^3), p_2((1,xi)^2). New general machinery (Analysis/AnalyticRayExpansion.lean): uniform graded Taylor bound along rays for HasFPowerSeriesOnBall maps, vanishing of coefficients from O(a^k) bounds, graded remainder with uniform bound, symmetric bi/trilinear expansions of iterated derivatives. Non-vacuity: an example with R_s(a,v) = a^2 v_s, R_f(a,v) = a v_f satisfies all hypotheses. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

slow form xi_j(tau) = a_j^-1 v_j(tau/a_j^2) (slowForm), which solves the slow system by lem:supp-exact-slow-expansion (proved in this batch, ExactSlowExpansion.slow_expansion). Hypotheses: a_j > 0, a_j -> 0 (monotonicity not needed), T > 0, finite-dimensional Xs, W; the bounded-rate hypothesis eq:supp-exact-bounded-slow-rate on the actual derivative: \|xi_j(tau)\| + \|derivWithin xi_j [0,T] tau\| <= M; the weak decomposition eq:supp-exact-weak-decomposition (ExactSlowBranch.WeakDecomposition) and the coupling test p_H C_red = 0 (p_g C_red onto is not needed). five_compatibility proves: a uniformly convergent subsequence exists (Arzela-Ascoli, exists_tendstoUniformlyOn_subseq, vector-valued on [0,T]); for EVERY StrictMono subsequence converging uniformly on [0,T] to xi_0: xi_0 is M-Lipschitz, Phi_0(xi_0) = 0 on [0,T] (principal balance eq:supp-exact-slow-principal-balance, RHS O(a^2)), C_sl(xi_0) = (p_g C_red[b_s + Sigma_0(xi_0)], p_H Phi_1(xi_0)) = 0 on all of [0,T], and (x_0)_tau = b_s + Sigma_0(xi_0) at every interior time (stronger than the paper's a.e.; via hasDerivAt_of_tendstoUniformlyOn, the a Sigma_1 term vanishing uniformly by the remainder bound of the lemma). The g-identity is obtained without Rademacher: p_g C_red x_0 = -p_g B z_0 = 0 is constant, so its derivative p_g C_red(b_s + Sigma_0(xi_0)) vanishes on (0,T), and continuity extends it to [0,T]. These readout statements are pointwise in time with explicit constants; they are not assembled into one sequence statement. No open record is used. Non-vacuity: an example instantiates five_compatibility (zero data, W = R^2 with W = 0 + R + R, a_j = 1/(j+1)); slow_expansion's example shows nonzero admissible remainders. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Audit (5 Oct): the readout lemmas are generic and are not instantiated on the sequence of five_compatibility (the slow space Xs is not identified with Qs x LA); the jet-closeness hypothesis of readout_order combines the prepared-record clause of ass:supp-graded-exact-chart, eq:supp-exact-slow-physical-tube and the conclusions of physical_velocity_orders / physical_acceleration_order, and C4 smoothness supplies the Lipschitz readout. Accepted as the paper's own proof structure (chain of order estimates 'subject to the readout conditions'). Instantiability (4 Oct revision): C_red, B, b_s, b_w, R_s, R_f (and Df(0), Dg(0)) are the chart data of ass:supp-graded-exact-chart (C3), which the paper does not write as explicit matrices; by thm:supp-exact-action-provenance they are derivatives/basis restrictions of the now-explicit finite action eq:supp-exact-phase-action / Hamiltonian eq:supp-exact-H, so the abstract theorem is instantiable in principle; the proved abstract statement already covers the paper's claim. |

| `prop:supp-exact-initial-counterexample` | proposition | Analytic preparation does not imply the two-row cancellation | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.prop_supp_exact_initial_counterexample`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.counterexample_euler_lagrange_partials`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.counterexample_euler_lagrange`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.counterexample_first_derivative_bounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactInitialCounterexample.counterexample_jet_family_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactInitialCounterexampleExact.lean) | The polynomial Lagrangian L = 1/2 alpha^2 z'^2 - 1/2 (z - alpha)^2 + lambda alpha' (eq:supp-exact-counterexample-L) is encoded with all six partial derivatives proved (lagrangian_hasDerivAt_*). For every a > 0 the explicit solution alpha = a, z = a(1 - cos(tau/a)), lambda = int_0^tau (a z'^2 + z - a) (eq:supp-exact-counterexample-solution, multiplier solved by integration) satisfies all three Euler-Lagrange equations d/dtau dL/dq' = dL/dq at every time. Initial records alpha(0) = a, z(0) = 0, lambda(0) = 0 and velocities alpha'(0) = 0, z'(0) = 0, lambda'(0) = -a are polynomial (analytic, bounded) in a. First derivatives are uniformly bounded: \|z'\| <= 1, \|lambda'\| <= 2a, alpha' = 0. But a z''(0) = 1 for every a, so the scaled acceleration defect tends to 1 != 0 as a -> 0+. Rendering disclosed: 'the analogue of the upper homological row is nonzero' is encoded, as in the paper's proof, as the one-row reduction a z''(0) = 1 (right-hand side of eq:supp-exact-initial-acceleration-defect with no source term). Bonus: the d-family after prop:supp-finite-acc-invariance (z'(0) = a^2 d, a z''(0) = 1 for every d) is counterexample_jet_family_defect. |

ometry.ExactSlowGate.initial_gate_three_row`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactSlowGateExact.lean) | Abstract block form over real vector spaces: L_0 : K -> Z_g x H any linear map, K = J_g Z_g + E_x X_h (spanning; directness not needed), blocks A_g,B_x,C_g,D_x defined as the components of L_0(J_g z + E_x x) (eq:supp-exact-complete-LS-blocks, no vanishing of the upper block on the complement assumed), H_x = D_x - C_g A_g^{-1} B_x with A_g^{-1} passed as a two-sided inverse AgInv (the paper's invertibility of A_g from positivity of Q_g, eq:supp-exact-Qg-pivots, is this hypothesis). Proved: eq:supp-exact-hard-forcing (three_row_solvable_iff, for arbitrary f); rank L_0 = dim Z_g + rank H_x, = 2 + rank H_x when dim Z_g = 2 (three_row_rank, three_row_rank_two; finite-dimensional Z_g, X_h); five-row surjectivity iff rank H_x = 3 when dim Z_g = 2, dim H = 3 (three_row_surjective_iff); eq:supp-exact-defect-feedback for kappa_* = J_g z_* + E_x x_* with L_0 kappa_* + f_0 = (Delta_g, 0) (three_row_defect_feedback); existence of kappa~ with L_0 kappa~ = -f_0 iff C_g A_g^{-1} Delta_g in Ran H_x (three_row_exists_iff_defect). initial_gate_three_row instantiates the criterion and rank split for the actual initial gate map L_0 kappa = (p_g C_red Df(0) kappa, p_H Dg(0) kappa) of eq:supp-L0-definition restricted to K_0 = ker Phi_0, with vertical insertion z -> (0, J_g z) (B J_g = 0); Df(0), Dg(0), C_red, B, p_g, p_H remain abstract parameters. Instantiability (4 Oct revision): C_red, B, b_s, b_w, R_s, R_f (and Df(0), Dg(0)) are the chart data of ass:supp-graded-exact-chart (C3), which the paper does not write as explicit matrices; by thm:supp-exact-action-provenance they are derivatives/basis restrictions of the now-explicit finite action eq:supp-exact-phase-action / Hamiltonian eq:supp-exact-H, so the abstract theorem is instantiable in principle; the proved abstract statement already covers the paper's claim. |
| `prop:supp-exact-slope-contraction` | proposition | Two-dimensional contraction certificate | [`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.compBilin`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.newtonMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.newtonMap_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.norm_newtonMap_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.quadratic_contraction_certificate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.analytic_root_continuation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.scriptR_contDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.sliceMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.sliceMap_contDiff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.QuadraticNewton.slope_contraction_certificate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean) | slope_contraction_certificate is the proposition for the slice map F_s(y) = R(c_* + E y + s n) - y_0 (sliceMap, with R = ExactSlowBranch.scriptR), on any real Banach space P (the paper uses R^2). Hypotheses: an exact quadratic Taylor identity F_{s0}(ybar+q) = F_{s0}(ybar) + J q + B[q,q]; M injective (equivalent to invertible in finite dimension); r > 0; eta = \|\|M F_{s0}(ybar)\|\|, theta = \|\|I - M J\|\|, beta = 2\|\|M B\|\| (operator norm of the bilinear map compBilin M B), q_* = theta + beta r; q_* < 1 and eta + q_* r < r. Conclusion: a zero y_* in the closed r-ball that lies in the open ball and is the unique zero in the closed ball. Proof: the Lipschitz bound \|\|Tq - Tq'\|\| <= q_*\|\|q - q'\|\| on the ball (norm_newtonMap_sub_le; symmetry of B is not needed), T maps the closed ball into the open ball, and Banach's theorem (ContractingWith.exists_fixedPoint'). Continuation clause: if the y-derivative of (s,y) -> F_s(y) at (s0, y_*) is invertible, there is a root curve psi with psi(s0) = y_*, analytic at every s near s0, F_s(psi(s)) = 0 near s0, and near (s0, y_*) the zero set is exactly graph psi. This uses Mathlib's ContDiffAt.implicitFunction in class omega (analytic), applied because the slice map is polynomial (sliceMap_contDiff). It needs invertibility only at the root, which is weaker than the paper's product-neighbourhood condition. quadratic_contraction_certificate and analytic_root_continuation are the general forms. |

y.SkewPfaffian.Pf_zero_size`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.Pf_ne_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.pfaff_expand`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.sum_mul_adjE`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.pfaff_ne_zero_of_injOnSet`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SkewPfaffian.pfaff_insert_insert`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean) | Proved in full. New infrastructure Algebra/SkewPfaffian.lean (Mathlib has no Pfaffian): pfaff A s = Pfaffian of the principal submatrix of A : ι → ι → R on a finset s of a linear order, defined by expansion along max s; Pf K = pfaff K univ for Matrix (Fin n); conventions Pf[[0,k],[-k,0]] = k (Pf_two), Pf(0x0) = 1 (Pf_zero_size); expansion along an arbitrary index (pfaff_expand), index-moving sign (pfaff_move), vanishing for equal rows (pfaff_eq_zero_of_row_eq), the Pfaffian adjugate identity A P(A) = Pf(A) I (sum_mul_adjE; matrix form mul_pfAdj/pfAdj_mul with the paper's P(K)_{ij} = (-1)^{i+j} Pf(K_{îĵ}), i<j, skew completion), order-embedding invariance (pfaff_map, adjE_map), the two-border expansion (pfaff_insert_insert), and over char-0 fields Pf K != 0 <-> K invertible (Pf_ne_zero_iff, via pfaff_ne_zero_of_injOnSet: induction through the adjugate and the complementary-minor argument for the inverse). Pf^2 = det itself is not proved (not needed). The record (Algebra/BorderedPfaffianRangeTest.lean, supp_finite_range_pfaffian): for K real skew with K.rank + 2 = 2m (the paper's rank 2m-2), R in R^{3x2m}, d in R^3, the border B = [[K,R^T,0],[-R,0_3,d],[0,-d^T,0]] (fromBlocks + finSumFinEquiv, paper's block order), S = R P(K) R^T, n_P = (S_23,-S_13,S_12): (1) Pf B = n_P^T d (Pf_border; holds for every square K over any commutative ring); (2) n_P != 0 <-> finrank R(ker K) = 2; (3) on that branch Pf B = 0 <-> d in R(ker K); (4) on that branch at membership rank B = 2m+2; (5) on that branch away from membership B is invertible (IsUnit det). The rank/invertibility clauses are stated on the branch n_P != 0, which is the paper's 'on that branch' (off the branch Pf B = 0 identically, so invertibility fails, e.g. R = 0). Proof route follows the paper: P(K) = c(z1 z2^T - z2 z1^T) with c != 0 for any ordered kernel basis (c != 0 because [[K,Z],[-Z^T,0]] is nonsingular, so its Pfaffian z1^T P(K) z2 is nonzero), n_P = c (Rz1 x Rz2); rank 2m+2 by a nonzero principal (2m+2)-Pfaffian S_ab (lower bound) and B P(B) = 0 with P(B) != 0 skew (upper bound) instead of the congruence splitting. Last sentence (border_test_iff_range): with the incidence C_* = -D_c^{-1} R_c Z_g, d = D_c q_* (D_c invertible, Z_g a kernel basis: K Z = 0, Z injective), d in R_c(ker K) <-> q_* in Ran C_*, so on the branch Pf B = 0 <-> q_* in Ran C_*; for 2m = 26 the border is 30x30 (example). K_g itself is a certified interval matrix not written in the manuscript; the last sentence is formalized for any K satisfying the proposition's own hypothesis (skew, rank 2m-2), which is how the proposition is stated. Non-vacuity: nonvacuity_branch (m = 1, K = 0, R = [[1,0],[0,1],[0,0]]). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

endpoint-vanishing w with the explicit expression firstVariation = -A0 sum_j (2 R_j b_j - R_j^2 n_j/s_j); abs_firstVariation_le is eq:supp-homogeneous-euler-bound \|delta S_d\| <= 2 sqrt(A0 E)
