[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.entropy_energy_estimate_absCont`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.finiteKL_acceptedPathLaw_absCont`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.finiteKL_nonneg_of_absCont`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.sub_le_mul_log_div_of_absCont`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.acceptedRow_pos_of_acceptedPathLaw_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.log_acceptedPathLaw_of_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.neg_log_stageZ_le_of_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.markovLaw_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.acceptedRow_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.stageZ_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyAbsCont.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.prevState`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.markovLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.markovLaw_sum_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.stageZ`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.acceptedRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.acceptedPathLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.pathCost`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean) | entropy_energy_estimate_absCont proves eq:supp-entropy-energy in the paper's generality: stage proposal rows p_j(x,.) >= 0 summing to one (zeros allowed, rows may vanish off the proposal set), arbitrary real costs E_j (nonnegativity not needed), accepted rows Q_j = p_j e^{-E_j/eps}/Z_j with Markov product law Q (acceptedPathLaw), any complete path law P on Fin M -> X (memory allowed, same initial value x0 by construction) with P << Q as a genuine hypothesis (P w > 0 -> Q w > 0). The candidate hypothesis is required only for relevant rows (rows x_j visited by a P-charged history): some proposal y with E_j(x_j,y) <= e_j and p_j(x_j,y) >= p_{*,j} > 0 (weaker than 'every proposal weight >= p_{*,j}'). Proof: along Q-charged histories every accepted factor, proposal weight and Z_j is positive (acceptedRow_pos_of_acceptedPathLaw_pos), giving the exact identity eps D(P\|\|Q) = eps D(P\|\|P_ref) + E_P E + eps E_P sum_j log Z_j (finiteKL_acceptedPathLaw_absCont, sums over supp P, 0 log 0 = 0), Gibbs' inequality under absolute continuity D(P\|\|P_ref) >= 0 (finiteKL_nonneg_of_absCont, reference may vanish off supp P), and Z_j >= p_* e^{-e_j/eps} (neg_log_stageZ_le_of_nonneg). Disclosed scoping: stage rows/costs are functions of the current state (the product structure of Q stated in the lemma). Non-vacuity example: a two-state chain with proposal rows vanishing off the diagonal. |
| `lem:supp-two-edge-schur` | lemma | Two-edge Schur identity | [`RenewalGeometry.two_edge_schur`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_completed_square`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_minimizer_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_blocked_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_eq_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_determinant`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.edgeQuadratic`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.schurMinimizer`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean) | Fully proved in the paper's generality. edgeQuadratic p q r x y = p x^2 - 2 q x y + r y^2; for p_i,q_i,r_i > 0 with d_i = p_i r_i - q_i^2 >= 0: (i) the minimizer z_* = (q_1 x + q_2 y)/(r_1+p_2) is positive on positive endpoints (twoEdgeSchur_minimizer_pos); (ii) the exact completed square E_1(x,z)+E_2(z,y) = E'(x,y) + (r_1+p_2)(z - z_*)^2 with the blocked coefficients p',q',r' of eq:supp-schur-coefficients (twoEdgeSchur_completed_square), hence E' is the minimum in z (twoEdgeSchur_blocked_le) attained exactly at z_* (twoEdgeSchur_eq_iff); (iii) the boxed determinant identity p'r' - q'^2 = (r_2 d_1 + p_1 d_2)/(r_1+p_2) (twoEdgeSchur_determinant, eq:supp-schur-determinant). two_edge_schur packages all clauses plus the byproduct p'r'-q'^2 >= 0. The identities only need r_1 + p_2 != 0; the d_i >= 0 hypotheses are carried for faithfulness. |
| `prop:main-optimizer-composition` | proposition | Optimizer composition characterizes action--law matching | [`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.hyperbolicCost`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.optimizerRatio`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.optimizerMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.relativeDefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.hyperbolicCost_sub_optimizer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.optimizerMap_isMin`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.optimizerMap_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.optimizerMap_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.optimizerRatio_inv_add_sub`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.optimizerMap_comp_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.relativeDefect_eq_zero_iff_comp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.relativeDefect_eq_zero_iff_mul_eq_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HyperbolicCostOptimizer.optimizerRatio_of_relativeDefect_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/HyperbolicCostOptimizerComposition.lean) | Proved in the paper's generality. hyperbolicCost is eq:main-hyperbolic-cost (coth = cosh/sinh, csch = 1/sinh). For μ > 0, \|C\| ≤ μ (i.e. \|ρ\| ≤ 1, ρ = C/μ) and θ > 0: hyperbolicCost_sub_optimizer gives E(x,y) - E(x, m_θ x) = (μ coth θ + C)(y - m_θ x)^2 with m_θ x = r_θ x, r_θ = (cosh θ + ρ sinh θ)^{-1}, hence optimizerMap_isMin / optimizerMap_unique (unique minimizer over all real y, in particular over y > 0) and optimizerMap_pos (m_θ x > 0 for x > 0): eq:main-optimizer-map. optimizerRatio_inv_add_sub is eq:main-optimizer-defect for all θ, φ, ρ. optimizerMap_comp_iff: for one positive pair (θ, φ) and one x > 0, m_{θ+φ} x = m_φ (m_θ x) iff Δ_rel = 0; relativeDefect_eq_zero_iff_comp packages both the every-positive-pair and the one-pair-at-one-x equivalences; relativeDefect_eq_zero_iff_mul_eq_sq identifies Δ_rel = 0 with AB = C^2 for μ = sqrt(AB), A, B > 0; optimizerRatio_of_relativeDefect_eq_zero gives ρ = ±1 and r_θ = exp(-ρ θ) on the matched branch. Scoped: the standing positivity \|C\| ≤ μ of the measured cost is a hypothesis; the local-Lipschitz stability remark of the appendix proof is not formalized (not part of the proposition). |

newalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.implicitMidpoint_explicit_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.norm_line_midpoint_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.norm_line_symm_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.norm_line_taylor_one_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CharacteristicSplitting.norm_line_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean) | All clauses proved. Exact clauses (earlier waves, GowdyStaggeredMomentumExact.lean): exact preservation of K_l = D+ lambda - 2 A+ J(U) by source steps, transport and the source-half/transport/source-half composition (staggeredMomentum_symmetric_splitting), the transport identity, nonnegative background increment (sum of squared characteristic amplitudes), nearest-neighbour update. NEW (this wave): the second-order consistency clause. GowdySplittingSecondOrderConsistency.lean: GowdyFrameSolution t0 t1 encodes a C^3 solution of the frame system eq:supp-gowdy-frame-system on [t0,t1] x T^1 (fields a,b,c,d,P,Q,lambda on R x R, 2pi-periodic in theta, the seven evolution equations for t in [t0,t1] with the paper's partial derivatives dT, dTheta); the reference state is Z = (sqrt t (a,b,c,d), P, Q, t) (eq:supp-gowdy-rescaled-frame), the grid sampling S_l X_*(tau) on ZMod N with l = 2pi/N. symmetric_splitting_second_order (t0 > 0): there are C, h0, delta > 0 such that for every N with l = 2pi/N <= h0 and every tau with [tau, tau+l] in [t0,t1], (i) the step (implicit-midpoint source half-step of duration l/2 at every site, transport l, source half-step) from the sampled state exists on the near-identity branch (stage increments <= delta; Banach fixed point), and (ii) every such realisation X3 satisfies, at every site j, \|\|X3(j) - X_*(tau+l, theta_j)\|\| <= C l^3 for the local state (U,P,Q,t) (sup norm) and \|lambda3(j) - lambda_*(tau+l, theta_j)\| <= C l^3, i.e. local truncation error O(h^3) with h = l (second-order consistency). Proof: the general theorem CharacteristicSplitting.StrangSetup.local_error (DiscreteAnalysis/CharacteristicStrangSplittingConsistency.lean) for semilinear systems Z_t = (Pi+ - Pi-)Z_theta + F(Z), lambda_t = g+(Z)+g-(Z) with exact characteristic shift and implicit-midpoint source half-steps (stage residual/stability estimates, midpoint and trapezoid rules along the characteristics (1,-+1),(1,0) with local derivative bounds), instantiated with the Gowdy projections onto w+, w-, (P,Q,t) (transport_site_toVec shows the library transport map is exactly Pi+ X_{j+1} + Pi- X_{j-1} + Pi0 X_j), densities g+- = \|w+-\|^2, the characteristic form of the frame system derived by the chain rule (char_plus/minus/zero/lam), chart constants (explicit Lipschitz gowdyLipschitz, bound and second-difference constant by compactness via a smooth regularisation of 1/t, 1/sqrt t below t0/2), and slab derivative bounds from periodicity + compactness. Renderings disclosed: smoothness is C^3 (the paper says smooth) of functions on R x R satisfying the equations on the slab (a smooth solution on the closed slab extends); T^1 is represented by 2pi-periodicity; the coordinate constraints are not assumed (consistency holds without them, so this is more general); the implicit-midpoint map is the near-identity branch (increments <= delta), whose existence is part of the theorem; norms are sup norms on the product state space. Non-vacuity: an example instantiates GowdyFrameSolution (zero data), and the existence clause (i) shows the step hypotheses are satisfiable. Standard axioms only. |

| PROVED (fC1 + fC1b), both branches of the hypothesis 'either a smallness bootstrap or an enforced offset envelope'. (1) SMALLNESS BOOTSTRAP (new, fC1b): GowdyFrameSolution.state_error_bootstrap (Gravity/GowdyHigherOrderBootstrap.lean): for a smooth solution (IsSmooth) and a chart radius R >= chartRadius there are a near-identity branch radius rho_*, a smallness threshold eps_*, l0 > 0, an envelope radius R_env and C such that for h = l = 2pi/N <= l0 every numerical history X_{n+1} = Phi_h(X_n) + r_n of Gowdy split steps whose implicit stages are rho_*-near-identity (the near-identity branch defining Phi_h), with initial C^r_l error eps_0 <= eps_* and sqrt F_h <= eps_*, NO envelope hypothesis, stays in the C^r_l envelope GowdyEnvR r on the whole interval and obeys \|\|X_n - S_l X_*(t_n)\|\|_{r,inf,l} <= C (eps_0 + h^2 + sqrt F_h) for all n <= n0. Proof: discrete continuation argument SmoothSetup.global_error_bootstrap (DiscreteAnalysis/GridFaaDiBrunoBootstrap.lean): stage control SmoothSetup.stageCtl (by induction through the difference-quotient prolongation: C^k_l closeness eta of the state and rho-near-identity stages give the C^k_l envelope; level-one bound fwdDiff_srcStep_le \|\|D+A\|\| <= 3\|\|D+X\|\|, \|\|D+(A-X)\|\| <= 2 sigma L \|\|D+X\|\|; base membership env0_of_near; converse envK_of_prol; thresholds Params.exists_smallK), and by induction on n the proved envelope bound (global_error_crNorm for the setup with enlarged radius, withR) keeps the error below the stage-control threshold at every step, so the envelope is never left. (2) ENFORCED ENVELOPE (fC1): GowdyFrameSolution.state_error_crNorm_flux / state_error_crNorm, via the reusable machinery GridFaaDiBrunoStability / Prolongation / HigherOrder (difference-quotient prolongation, uniform discrete Faa di Bruno bound derivBound_diffQuot, oneStepK, global_error_crNorm; Gowdy instance exists_gowdySmoothSetup). Constraint clauses (earlier): exact increment eq:supp-gowdy-constraint-increment (staggeredMomentum_residual_increment) and for r >= 1 \|K_l(X_n)\| <= \|K_l(X_0)\| + C sqrt F_h (staggeredMomentum_accumulated_of_le). Non-vacuity: zeroFrameSolution_isSmooth, constGrid_history_envelope (envelope branch) and constGrid_bootstrap_data + zeroFrameSolution_sample (bootstrap branch: the constant split steps of the trivial solution have stage increments l/2, zero initial error and zero residuals). Renderings: Phi_h is the near-identity implicit-midpoint split map (stages within rho_* of their inputs, any such choice); C, rho_*, eps_* depend on r, the reference and R. |

| NEW (es10b3): the whole assembly is formalised for the concrete Gowdy scheme in Gravity/GowdyHermiteReadoutCurvature.lean, on top of two new general files. (i) Analysis/TensorHermiteReadout.lean: the physical readout readout x0 y0 h f u v w (x,y) = interp h f u v w ((x-x0)/h) ((y-y0)/h); the chain-rule step is proved (iterate_dP_dQ_readout: d_x^i d_y^j readout(x0+hp, y0+hq) = cellDeriv i j (p,q)/h^{i+j}, i.e. tensor/h^{i+j} IS the physical derivative); readout_jet_error / _le_one / _two: records within JetBound h (c h^2) of the exact records of a smooth phi give \|D^k(readout - phi)\| <= (72c + hermRemConst M) h^2 (k<=1) and h (k=2) on the cell; readout_glue_x/_y: shared nodal jets give equal values, first derivatives and tangential derivatives across interfaces (C^1 gluing, so the weak second derivative is cellwise). (ii) Analysis/SecondOrderChainRuleLipschitz.lean: second-order chain rule (hasFDerivAt_fderiv_comp) and jet errors of compositions with a C^3 map on a compact convex chart (comp_jet_sub_le, comp_jet_norm_le). (iii) Gowdy assembly: GowdySmoothReference = C^infinity solution of the frame system plus the coordinate constraints (the smooth reference; GowdyFrameSolution is only C^3; non-vacuity zeroReference); records timeRec/spaceRec of eq:supp-gowdy-hermite-jets are the exact t- and theta-derivatives on the slab (dT_fld, dΘ_fld); PathwiseHistory packages the hypotheses (split steps in the enforced envelope, offset cap charDist(X_{n+1},B_n) <= c0 h^4 = eq:supp-gowdy-offset-cap, pathwise C^1_h error crNorm 1 <= C1 h^2 = eq:supp-gowdy-pathwise-c1); PathwiseHistory.cell_jetBound: every corner value/time/space/mixed record error is O(h^2) and the nodal time/space differences O(h^3) (time: GowdyFrameSolution.nodal_time_difference; mixed w = D0 u: discrete chain rule norm_sub_sub_sub_le + centered-difference consistency abs_centered_partial_le); readout_second_jet = eq:supp-gowdy-second-jet on every cell of every history: \|D^k(f_h - f_*)\| <= C h^2 for k <= 1, <= C h for k = 2, f = P, Q, lambda; cellReadout_glue_t/_θ = C^1 gluing of the concrete readout. readout_full_curvature = eq:supp-gowdy-full-curvature: with gowdyMetric (eq:supp-gowdy-metric as a smooth map of (t,P,Q,lambda) on t>0) and gowdyInvMetric (proved to be the Matrix inverse, gowdyInvMetric_eq_inv), on every cell \|\|g_h-g_*\|\| + \|\|Dg_h-Dg_*\|\| <= C h^2, \|\|D^2 g_h - D^2 g_*\|\| <= C h, \|\|Riem(g_h)-Riem(g_*)\|\| <= C h (Riem = riemJet of the coordinate 2-jet), \|\|Riem(g_h)\|\| <= C uniformly in h (the L^2 certificate on the finite-volume subslab follows: rendering as a uniform sup bound), and \|\|Ric(g_h)\|\| <= C h at every point where Ric(g_*) = 0. Constants depend only on the reference, R, c0, C1. Renderings: the readout is stated cellwise (cell polynomials + C^1 gluing lemmas) rather than as one global W^{2,inf} function; the curvature is the coordinate Riemann tensor of the metric 2-jet in (t, theta, x, y) (fields independent of x, y). NEW (es10b5) vacuum clause: Gravity/GowdyVacuumRicci.lean proves the algebraic identity ricci_gowdy_algebraic (each of the 16 components of CoordinateCurvatureJet.ricciJet of the Gowdy metric 2-jet vanishes once P_tt = P_θθ - P_t/t + e^{2P}(Q_t^2 - Q_θ^2), Q_tt = Q_θθ - Q_t/t - 2P_tQ_t + 2P_θQ_θ, λ_t = t(P_t^2+P_θ^2+e^{2P}(Q_t^2+Q_θ^2)), λ_θ = 2t(P_tP_θ+e^{2P}Q_tQ_θ) and their first derivatives hold; no residual, the manuscript's claim is correct); Gravity/GowdyReferenceVacuum.lean proves, with a second-order jet calculus (HasJ2), that the actual metric 2-jet of g_* is that jet (gowdyJet_ref_eq; inverse at Matrix type via gowdyInvMetric_eq_inv), derives those relations from the frame system eq_a/eq_c/eq_lam and the coordinate constraints, hence Ric(g_*) = 0 at interior slab points (ricci_flat_interior) and, by continuity, on the closed slab for t0 < t1 (ricci_flat). GowdySmoothReference.readout_full_curvature_vacuum states every clause of the theorem with the unconditional \|\|Ric(g_h)\|\| <= C h. Remaining renderings (disclosed): cellwise statement + C^1 gluing lemmas; L^2 certificate as a uniform sup bound on the finite-volume region. |

ean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.action_test_pathwise`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.ActionTest.action_test`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyActionTest.lean) | `lem:supp-executed-proposal-floor` | lemma | A finite execution certificate for the proposal floor | [`RenewalGeometry.executed_proposal_floor`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.proposal_floor_arith`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.survivalProduct_telescope`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.survivalProduct`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean) | Proved on an abstract probability space (MeasureTheory.Measure with IsProbabilityMeasure), bins 0..J-1 (the paper's 1..J shifted). The launch race is modelled by the decreasing no-earlier-launch events N l (N 0 = univ) and the predictor-marked first-launch events P l, a subset of N l \ N (l+1) (so they are automatically pairwise disjoint); the conditional probabilities 'given no earlier launch' are rendered in product form mu(N l \ N(l+1)) = a_l mu(N l), mu(P l) = f_l mu(N l), and the conditional execution floor as mu(G cap P l) >= q_* mu(P l). Under f_l >= rho_* a_l, -log S_J >= A_* > 0, 0 < rho_* <= 1, q_* > 0, executed_proposal_floor gives mu(G) >= q_* rho_* (1 - e^{-A_*}) (eq:supp-executed-proposal-floor). The proof derives mu(N l) = S_l by induction, sums the disjoint events G cap P l (measure_biUnion_finset), and applies the arithmetic certificate proposal_floor_arith (telescoping survivalProduct_telescope: sum S_l a_l = 1 - S_J, then S_J <= e^{-A_*} via exp_log). Disclosed: the hypotheses A_* > 0 and rho_* <= 1 are carried but not needed (the bound holds without them); the events are assumed measurable. |

| Proved (re-assembled in Gravity/VacuumRegulatorGenuineAssembly.lean, vacuum_regulators_genuine, for Hubble rate H >= 0, Lambda = 3H^2, H = 0 the flat branch). No clause is a definitional unfolding; the old assembly (VacuumRegulatorAssembly / adm_variables_exact / slabSpatialCommonLower / constantCurvatureTensor / scalar Taylor remainders) is no longer cited. (i) desitterProfile_isStationaryRenewalSolution (a = e^{Ht}, N = 1 solves E_u = E_v = 0 of thm:main-homogeneous-friedmann, now also equivalent to the complete Einstein equation) and finite_recurrence_to_profile (scale_factor_log_error_le, explicit O(h^2) log error, H > 0). (ii) A3CutoffRegulator / desitterCutoff H d t: at every cutoff h = 1/d and time t the twelve root rates e^{-2Ht}/(8h^2), vertex masses e^{3Ht}h^3 on V_h = Lambda/dLambda and the directly normalized race law k_alpha/Sum k on the event alphabet of con:supp-flat-regulator (= 1/12, sums to 1); rootMoment = h^2 Sum k_alpha alpha alpha^T and massDensity = m_h/h^3 are computed from these data; ADM inversion (admMetric, admLapse) and the drift give g_h = e^{2Ht} I, N_h = 1, beta_h = 0 (desitterCutoff_adm; no primitive metric, speedDensity/spatialMetric defs not used); the rates/masses are kappa/(8h^2), rho h^3 of the stationary profile (desitterCutoff_stationary_rates) and at H = 0 the law is flatPeriodicRegulator (desitterCutoff_flat). (iii) cutoffLorentz = admLorentz(N_h, beta_h, g_h) reconstructed from the cutoff data equals g_H at every cutoff, hence converges along h = 1/(n+1) (desitterCutoff_lorentz_tendsto), and g_H solves G + 3H^2 g = 0 with the Einstein tensor computed from the metric field (FriedmannLapseEinstein.desitter_lapseMetric_vacuum; H = 0: Minkowski, G = 0, Lambda = 0). Generator: desitterStageGen (the stage generator with the law's own rates) = e^{-2Ht} L_h^flat (desitterStageGen_eq), and desitter_semigroup_strong: e^{s L_h(t)} -> e^{s e^{-2Ht} Delta/2} (heat semigroup of (1/2)Delta_{g(t)}, g(t) = e^{2Ht} I) strongly along the piecewise-constant embeddings, from flat_semigroup_strong; at H = 0 also flat_resolvent_strong. (iv) desitterCutoff_screens: on \|t\| <= T the time-t graph of the cutoff law (masses/rates = scaled periodic data at a = e^{Ht}) has cut constant 1/(256 e^{HT}), Poincare floor poincareConstant e^{-HT} e^{HT} (1/32), Weyl constant weylConstant e^{-HT} e^{HT} (1/32) and the compact-screen tail bound, all independent of h and t (desitter_adm_packet_full / desitter_slab_screens_unconditional). (v) derived Cartan curvature Omega = H^2 theta^A ^ theta_B (DeSitterCartan.curvature_eq; = 0 at H = 0), every frame component \|Omega^A_{BDE}\| <= H^2 (desitter_frame_curvature_bound, uniform L-infinity); transport coefficients: Christoffel symbols of g_H computed from the metric field, \|Gamma\| <= \|H\| e^{2\|H\|T} on \|t\| <= T (desitter_christoffel_slab_bound); observer coefficients N = 1, beta = 0; propagation budgets: for the retained frame-curvature record (curvatureRecord, constant in t) the writer y' = (K+L)y + f of thm:main-curvature-propagation holds with K = L = 0, f = 0, a = 0, so the budgets pass with Z* = z(0), A* = F* = 0 and curvature_energy_uniform_bound applies (desitter_curvature_budgets). (vi) exact links metric-unitary (edgeLink_metric_unitary; flat: identity links), along h_n = 2^{-n^2}, T_n = n, for all large n the normalized curvature/torsion defects of every shape-regular face and the Palatini midpoint-quadrature first-variation defect (any c_P, c_V, finite-volume domain, mesh kappa h_n cell decomposition, unit test) are <= C_{T_n} h_n with Sum C_{T_n} h_n < infinity (desitter_exact_link_defects from finiteDefects_diagonal, finiteDefects_slab, finiteDefects_full_diagonal); flat: all vanish (flat_exact_link_defects). Final sentence: explicit_operational_family (H > 0, omega = 3H/2, expanding branch). Disclosed renderings: A3CutoffRegulator is a standalone concrete record (as for con:supp-flat-regulator), not an instance of a general operational-carrier structure of mt:grand-tensor/mt:adm; the metric 'convergence' in (iii) is exact equality at every cutoff (as in the manuscript's thm:supp-desitter-adm), the nontrivial convergence being the generator limit; the curvature-propagation writer is the trivial one because the de Sitter frame-curvature record is constant. Non-vacuity: the statement is instantiated at H = 1 and H = 0 (example). |

is/A3FlatGeneratorLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatGenerator.contSem`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3FlatGeneratorLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatGenerator.pcSystem`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3FlatGeneratorLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatGenerator.strongOperatorConverges_of_modes`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3FlatGeneratorLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatGenerator.flat_resolvent_strong`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3FlatGeneratorLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatGenerator.flat_semigroup_strong`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3FlatGeneratorLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusPiecewiseConstant.cell`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusPiecewiseConstantEmbedding.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusPiecewiseConstant.volume_cell`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusPiecewiseConstantEmbedding.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusPiecewiseConstant.cell_disjoint`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusPiecewiseConstantEmbedding.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusPiecewiseConstant.mem_cell_index`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusPiecewiseConstantEmbedding.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusPiecewiseConstant.pcEmbedding`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusPiecewiseConstantEmbedding.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusPiecewiseConstant.pcEmbedding_mode_dist_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusPiecewiseConstantEmbedding.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HomogeneousADMPacket.flatSymbol`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HomogeneousADMPacketDerivedExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HomogeneousADMPacket.flat_symbol_expansion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/HomogeneousADMPacketDerivedExact.lean) | Proved. Encoding: V_h = Λ_{A3}/dΛ_{A3} (h = 1/d) in root-basis coordinates (Z/d)^3 (the repo's A3PeriodicGraphSampling.Vertex), roots by rootCoordinates (tied to the Euclidean A3 roots by A3PeriodicSmoothEnergy.root_eq_integerCombination); a dual-lattice vector k ∈ Λ* by its pairings κ_i = <k,b_i> ∈ Z, kvec κ the Euclidean vector (rootPairing_kvec). (1) gen = L_h at the flat rates 1/(8h^2) (eq:supp-root-generator with eq:supp-flat-rates; gen_apply); gen_modeFn: for every dual-lattice mode e_k(x) = e^{2πih<k,x>}, -L_h e_k = λ_h(k) e_k with λ_h(k) = (4h^2)^{-1} Σ_{a=1}^6 [1 - cos(2πh<r_a,k>)] = HomogeneousADMPacket.flatSymbol (pairing of opposite roots, lam_castVec). (2) tendsto_flatSymbol: λ_h(k) → 2π^2\|k\|^2 for every fixed k (h = 1/(n+1); via the explicit O(h^2) remainder of flat_symbol_expansion and the root-frame identity). (3) eq:supp-flat-generator-limit: stage Hilbert spaces ℓ^2(V_h) (= L^2(V_h, h^3) up to the unitary factor N^{3/2}; vertex mass h^3 as in con:supp-flat-regulator), the natural piecewise-constant embeddings pcEmbedding (f ↦ piecewise-constant function on the cells Π_i (x_i/d,(x_i+1)/d] in root-basis coordinates, proved isometric: cells measurable, disjoint, covering, measure d^{-3}), the continuum L^2(T^3_{A3}) identified via the root-basis coordinate chart with L^2 of Mathlib's UnitAddTorus (Fin 3) (normalised Haar measure; constant volume factor), e_k = mFourier κ, and (1/2)Δ the Fourier multiplier -2π^2\|k\|^2 (the paper's own spectral description; its resolvent contRes z and semigroup contSem t are the multipliers (z+2π^2\|k\|^2)^{-1}, e^{-2π^2 t\|k\|^2} built from mFourierBasis). flat_resolvent_strong: for every z > 0 the resolvents (z - L_h)^{-1} (resE, two-sided inverse of z - L_h by resE_spec) converge to (z - Δ/2)^{-1} in the strong varying-Hilbert operator sense of OperatorLimits/VaryingHilbertMosco (StrongOperatorConverges: every strongly convergent input family J_h x_h → x is mapped to J_h R_h x_h → R x); flat_semigroup_strong: for every t ≥ 0, e^{tL_h} (NormedSpace.exp, identified with the multiplier semE by semE_eq_exp) → e^{tΔ/2} in the same sense. Proof as in the manuscript: uniform bounds \|\|(z-L_h)^{-1}\|\| ≤ 1/z, \|\|e^{tL_h}\|\| ≤ 1, grid samples of e_k are eigenvectors with eigenvalues converging pointwise, \|\|J_h(sample of e_k) - e_k\|\|_{L^2} ≤ 2πΣ\|κ_i\|/d (pcEmbedding_mode_dist_le), density of trigonometric polynomials (span_mFourierLp_closure_eq_top) and OperatorLimits/DenseSourceStrongConvergence. Disclosed renderings: real spectral parameter z > 0 (the resolvent set of the nonpositive generators; complex z not stated separately); the continuum Laplacian is given through its Fourier multiplier (resolvent/semigroup) rather than as an unbounded operator object; normalised torus volume. |
| `lem:supp-periodic-screens` | lemma | Uniform spatial screens for the periodic $A_3$ graph | [`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.periodic_screens_unconditional`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.coordinateGridIsoperimetry`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.basisBoundary_ge`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.card_mul_le_box_sum`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.shiftDefect_add`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.shiftDefect_nsmul`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.shiftDefect_box_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.sum_unitVec_defect_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCoordinateIsoperimetry.exists_cube_root`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TorusCoordinateIsoperimetryExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3PeriodicScreens.periodic_screens`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3PeriodicScreensScalingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3PeriodicScreens.CoordinateGridIsoperimetry`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3PeriodicScreensScalingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3PeriodicScreens.scaledGraph`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3PeriodicScreensScalingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3PeriodicScreens.scaledMass_mul_rate`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3PeriodicScreensScalingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3PeriodicScreens.cut_capacity_ge_basisBoundary`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3PeriodicScreensScalingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3PeriodicScreens.cut_min`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3PeriodicScreensScalingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3PeriodicScreens.degree_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3PeriodicScreensScalingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3PeriodicScreens.scaledDegree`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3PeriodicScreensScalingExact.lean) | Now unconditional. The coordinate-grid edge-isoperimetric inequality CoordinateGridIsoperimetry d c_0 is proved for every d with the d-independent constant c_0 = 1/32 (coordinateGridIsoperimetry): for 0 < #A <= d^3/2 on (ZMod d)^3, #dA >= #A^{2/3}/28 (basisBoundary_ge), i.e. h^2 #dA >= (1/32)(h^3 #A)^{2/3}. Proof route DISCLOSED: not the manuscript's Loomis-Whitney slicing but an elementary box-path argument -- with shiftDefect A p = #{u in A : u+p not in A}, subadditivity in p (shiftDefect_add) and shiftDefect A (s p) <= s shiftDefect A p (shiftDefect_nsmul) give shiftDefect A delta <= m basisBoundary(A) for every box vector delta in [0,m)^3; summing over the m^3 box vectors with m <= d (injective translates) gives #A (m^3 - #A) <= m^4 basisBoundary(A) (card_mul_le_box_sum); the least m with m^3 >= 2#A satisfies m < 2.3 #A^{1/3}, hence the bound. periodic_screens_unconditional then states lem:supp-periodic-screens with no hypothesis: for every mesh h = 1/d and every a in [a_-, a_+], (iii) h c_h(dA) >= (1/(256 a_+)) mu_h(A)^{2/3} for 0 < #A <= d^3/2 (the twelve-root cut capacity dominates the basis-direction boundary, cut_capacity_ge_basisBoundary), (i) the spectral floor poincareConstant a_- a_+ (1/32), (ii) N_h(R) <= weylConstant a_- a_+ (1/32) (1 + R^{3/2}), and the compact-screen tail bound \|\|(I-P_R)f\|\|^2 <= R^{-1} E_h(f) in spectral coefficients; all constants depend only on a_-, a_+. Scoping disclosed: (i)-(ii) are derived from (iii) via the finite Cheeger/Sobolev-Weyl route (positiveEigenvalue_floor, eigenvalueCount_bound) rather than the manuscript's Fourier-symbol comparison c_1\|k\|^2 <= lambda_h(k) <= c_2\|k\|^2, so the constants are explicit but not the symbol-comparison ones; the statement holds for every h (not only sufficiently small h). |

| Proved. flat_adm_vacuum: B_h = h^2 sum_alpha (1/(8h^2)) alpha alpha^T = I (tight frame), g_h = I and N_h = 1 by the ADM inversion formula admMetric/admLapse, beta_h = 0 from reversal symmetry, conductance h/8 per root edge; rho_h = 1 appears there as speedDensity 0 t (definitional) and is computed from the regulator's masses (massDensity = m_h/h^3 = 1) in VacuumRegulatorGenuine.desitterCutoff_adm at H = 0, whose cutoff law is the flat regulator (desitterCutoff_flat; flatPeriodicRegulator_spatial identifies the regulator's rates/masses with the H = 0 data). Lorentzian continuum: Minkowski diag(-1,1,1,1) (lorentzMetric_zero_eq_minkowski), Christoffel symbols, Riemann, Ric, R and G vanish (minkowski_riemann_zero); Cartan torsion and curvature of the constant coframe vanish (DeSitterCartan at H = 0). Generator clause: lem:supp-flat-symbol (flat_resolvent_strong for every z > 0, flat_semigroup_strong for every t >= 0, natural piecewise-constant embeddings, (1/2)Delta as the Fourier multiplier -2 pi^2\|k\|^2). Finite residuals: DeSitterExactLink.Palatini.finiteDefects_flat_full (packaged with identity links in VacuumRegulatorGenuine.flat_exact_link_defects) proves that the exact-link curvature and torsion defects of every closed loop and the Palatini midpoint-quadrature first-variation defect (c_V = 0) vanish identically; the one-cell scalar remainders of flat_adm_vacuum are a weaker surrogate. flat_slab_screens_unconditional gives the uniform screen constants. |

| Closed: desitter_adm_packet_full states thm:supp-desitter-adm in full for H >= 0 -- for every h != 0 and t: B_h(t) = h^2 sum_alpha k_alpha(t) alpha alpha^T = e^{-2Ht} I computed from the rates e^{-2Ht}/(8h^2) and the tight frame, g_h(t) = e^{2Ht} I and N_h(t) = 1 from the ADM inversion formula admMetric/admLapse, beta_h(t) = 0 from reversal symmetry, conductance e^{Ht} h/8 (desitter_adm_packet); rho_h(t) = e^{3Ht} is the manuscript's defining value (speedDensity, by definition) and is computed from the vertex masses e^{3Ht}h^3 as massDensity = m_h/h^3 in VacuumRegulatorGenuine.desitterCutoff_adm. On every compact slab \|t\| <= T, for every mesh h = 1/d, the time-t spatial graph (mass/rate identified with the scaled periodic data at a = e^{Ht}, desitter_mass_rate_scaled) satisfies the cut inequality with constant 1/(256 e^{HT}), the Poincare floor poincareConstant e^{-HT} e^{HT} (1/32), the Weyl count with weylConstant e^{-HT} e^{HT} (1/32) and the compact-screen tail bound, uniformly in h and t (desitter_slab_screens_unconditional; its isoperimetric input coordinateGridIsoperimetry, c_0 = 1/32, is a theorem). Scoped hypothesis disclosed: H >= 0 (the paper's H = sqrt(Lambda/3) > 0); the screens are the finite Cheeger/Sobolev-Weyl constants rather than symbol-comparison constants. |

](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.norm_curvatureDefect_spatial_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.norm_curvatureDefect_mixed_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.curvatureDefect_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.developedEdge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.torsionSum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.torsionDefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.norm_torsionDefect_spatial_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.norm_torsionDefect_mixed_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.torsionSum_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.slabConst`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.slabMesh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.curvatureDefect_slab_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.torsionDefect_slab_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.summable_exp_mul_diagonalMesh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.diagonal_exhaustion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.finiteDefects_diagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DeSitterExactLink.finiteDefects_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.timeTransport_metric_unitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.diagonalMesh_summable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean) | PROVED (fA3). Curvature and torsion parts as before (Gravity/DeSitterExactLinkDefects.lean). NEW Palatini part (Gravity/DeSitterPalatiniFirstVariation.lean, on the general cell-quadrature lemma Analysis/CellQuadratureEstimate.lean). Encoding, from the manuscript only (eq:main-phv-densities and the proof of thm:supp-finite-defects): the Palatini-cosmological action S = c_P int L_Palatini + c_V int L_vol, L_Palatini = 1/2 eps_IJKL e^I e^J R^KL, L_vol = (1/4!) eps e e e e; its first variation in Euler-Lagrange (bulk) form, i.e. the 'finite sum of products of coframe, curvature, and test-field coefficients' of the proof: density c_P eps v^I e^J R^KL + (c_V/6) eps v^I e^J e^K e^L - c_P eps T^I e^J w^KL for a coframe variation v and a connection variation w (the connection part after the integration by parts delta_omega 1/2 eps e e R = -eps T e w; boundary terms are not included), four-forms evaluated on (d_t, d_1, d_2, d_3) (levi, wedge4/112/211, firstVariationDensity) and integrated against coordinate Lebesgue measure on the slab [-T,T] x D (D any measurable spatial fundamental domain of finite volume); delta S_h = midpoint quadrature sum_c \|c\| rho(F_h(c), v(x_c)) on any finite measurable cell decomposition of the slab whose points lie within kappa h of the quadrature point (the product cells of K_h with midpoints are a special case). The de Sitter/flat data are those of DeSitterExactLinkDefects in components (dsComp; torsion computed by Cartan's first structure equation and shown to vanish, R^KL = H^2 e^K ^ e^L). Faithfulness checks: levi_ref (eps_0123 = 1); firstVariationDensity_desitter_eq_zero: with c_V = -6 c_P H^2 (Lambda = 3H^2, eq:supp-desitter-einstein) de Sitter is stationary. Proved: \|delta S_h[v] - delta S[v]\| <= C_T^P h for EVERY coefficient pair (c_P, c_V) and every test field with \|v\|,\|w\| <= 1 and 1-Lipschitz components (a superset of the C^1 unit ball, isUnitTest_of_contDiff), C_T^P = densityLip(c_P,c_V,42B_T^3+1) (42B_T^3+1) kappa 2T\|D\|, B_T = (\|H\|+1)e^{\|H\|(T+1)} (palatini_midpoint_slab_le; the general writer version abs_quadratureVariation_sub_firstVariation_le allows cell coefficients within eps of the continuum ones). The '(e_h,U_h)' reading is also covered: the finite holonomy writer holComp (curvature coefficients -\|p\|^{-1} log U_dp of the exact-link holonomies of the coordinate faces centred at the quadrature point, torsion coefficients from their developed-edge closures) is C_{T+2} h accurate (holComp_close) and gives the same O_T(h) bound (palatini_holonomyWriter_slab_le, kappa >= 4, C_{T+2} h <= 1). Record-level: finiteDefects_slab: for T >= 0, kappa >= 1, 0 < h <= h_T, every shape-regular face p (closed spatial polygons, edge x time-step rectangles) at \|t\| <= T, every cell decomposition of mesh kappa h and every unit test, \|\|r_p\|\| + \|\|t_p\|\| + \|delta S_h - delta S\| <= C_T h with C_T = 2 slabConst + C_T^P (eq:supp-finite-defect-bound); finiteDefects_flat_full: flat regulator (H = 0, flat cosmological coefficient c_V = 0): all three parts vanish; finiteDefects_full_diagonal: h_n = 2^{-n^2}, T_n = n gives sum C_{T_n} h_n < infinity and h_n <= h_{T_n} eventually (eq:supp-defect-summability). Disclosures: with the tuned coefficient c_V = -6 c_P H^2 both delta S and its midpoint quadrature vanish identically (palatini_tuned_eq_zero), so the nontrivial content is the bound for arbitrary (c_P,c_V) and for the holonomy writer; the torsion defect is the developed-edge reading (as before); \|p\| is the coordinate area. Non-vacuity: isUnitTest_sin (smooth non-constant unit test), an explicit one-cell decomposition example, and the existing face examples. Audit (a13): faithful. Further renderings stated precisely: the bound is proved for 0 < h <= h_T = 1/(24 kappa B_T) (the manuscript's 'sufficiently small h' for the principal logarithm); edge x time-step faces are traversed from a bottom vertex as mixedLoop h w (both orientations via +-w), spatial faces are arbitrary closed polygons with arbitrary base vertex; \|p\| is any normalizing area >= h^2/kappa (in particular the coordinate area of a shape-regular face); C_T depends on kappa, \|D\|, c_P, c_V but not on h, the face, the test field or the decomposition. The developed-edge torsion reading matches the manuscript's wording 'covariant developed-edge closure' of eq:supp-torsion-defect. |

GravitationalClassDensity is the named class: a member is a spacetime-constant real bilinear pairing of the coframe bivector e^e with the curvature R on the bivector carrier BivectorIndex = Fin 2 x Fin 3 (the bidegree-(2,1) coefficient tensor c_{IJKL} e^I ^ e^J ^ R^{KL}; the spacetime four-form part of a (2,1) term is fixed by the wedge) together with an alternating four-form of the coframe (the curvature-free degree-four part). The exclusions of the definition are the shape of the structure -- no slot for an inverse coframe, torsion polynomial, curvature derivative, background tensor or matter field, and constant rather than spacetime-dependent coefficients. IsLorentzNatural encodes the admitted internal contractions (Lorentz metric, oriented volume tensor, scalars): invariance of the pairing under the induced action inducedBivectorAction of every proper Lorentz matrix; the scalar residual multiplicity commutant is the absence of any further internal index on the carrier. holstDensity / palatiniDensity / volumeDensity are the three densities of eq:main-phv-densities (eta-contraction metricPairing, epsilon-contraction epsilonPairing, det), and classification restates thm:main-gravitational-classification on the named class: every Lorentz-natural member is ofCoefficients alpha beta lambda with unique coefficients (coefficients_unique). Not modelled (disclosed): comparison of densities modulo exact terms whose variations vanish on compactly supported bulk tests and the separate retention of boundary functionals, which concern the variational use of the densities rather than the pointwise algebraic class; the converse naturality of the three named densities under all proper Lorentz matrices is not stated in Lean (the classification file verifies invariance only on the generators it uses). | — |  |

FiniteTypedEventGrammar (TypedEventGrammar.lean) carries (E1) Fintype CutType; (E2) Letter : CutType -> CutType -> Type with Fintype fibres; the prefix-closed reachable typed-history family of (E3) with typed concatenation (TypedWord.append) and empty words (TypedWord.nil); (E4) Fintype Preparation / InterventionOutcome / TerminalRead / Port; (E5) the protected binary orientation Read separated by the Read policy. New (TypedEventGrammarHorizon.lean): the finiteness clause of (E3) in both admitted forms -- FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar extends the grammar by the remaining horizon horizon : CutType -> Nat carried by the cut type and strictly decreasing along every letter, and finite_history proves that the reachable histories H_{X,x} at every cut type then form a finite family (typed words are determined by their letter sequence, TypedWord.toList_injective, and have at most horizon x - horizon y letters, TypedWord.length_add_horizon_le, hence TypedWord.finite_of_horizon); finite_minimalState shows every conditional law on it has a finite-index future congruence. FiniteIndexTypedEventGrammar is the alternative branch: an unbounded grammar with a conditional law whose future congruence (def:supp-future-signature, ConditionalLaw.futureSetoid) is certified to have finite index at every cut type. HistoryFinitenessCertificate is the disjunction of the two certificates and CertifiedFiniteTypedEventGrammar = grammar + certificate is the full definition. Admissible letters Sigma_{X,h} = Admissible, conditional law = ConditionalLaw as before. Rendering (audit 5 Oct 2026): reachable_nil makes every empty word reachable, so History x = Sigma s, reachable typed words s -> x (histories may start at any cut type; no distinguished initial cut); typed concatenation is the word operation TypedWord.append (the reachable family is not required to be closed under it). Non-vacuity: OperationalDatum.QubitExample.grammar, TypedFinitePredictionCountermodel.grammar. | — |  |

walLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.OperationalFrameData.multitimeProb_smul`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.OperationalFrameData.multitimeProb_convex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.OperationalFrameData.multitimeProb_nil`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.OperationalFrameData.frameProb_eq_chain`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.ProbeTuple`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.tupleKron`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.trace_tupleKron_mul`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.QubitExample.qubitPreparationLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.QubitExample.inducedTensor_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.QubitExample.qubitFrame`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.QubitExample.qubitFrame_spans`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.QubitExample.povm_duality`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDatum.QubitExample.povm_posSemidef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawInduced.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.wordProb`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PhysicalChoiTomographyFrame`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SlotIndex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawDatum.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.newestOutputTrace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/OperationalRenewalLawDatum.lean) | Encoded with the frame probabilities INDUCED by the conditional law (supersedes the side-by-side FiniteConditionalOperationalRenewalLaw of OperationalRenewalLawDatum.lean, which is kept). OperationalFrameData G extends the typed ConditionalLaw (p(a\|h) >= 0, zero off admissible letters, row sum 1 after every reachable history = eq:supp-one-step-law; chain law eq:supp-chain-law = ConditionalLaw.wordProb) and carries per exposed port input/output legs, a finite physical CP frame (PhysicalChoiTomographyFrame) on the Choi space whose real span is the full Hermitian space (frame_spans), injective intervention-outcome labels, and -- the repair -- INTERVENTION EVENTS AS GRAMMAR LETTERS: interventionLetter x p alpha : Sigma y, G.Letter x y realizing every frame probe at every cut type (injective), plus terminal Read outcomes as terminal event letters readEvent x r o (injective, disjoint from interventions). Derived, not stored: interventionWord (chronological probe tuple -> typed word, oldest probe first), framePr h ps a := p_X(w_a \| h) (the induced multitime frame table; framePr_cons = chain rule), readProb h r o := p_X(readEvent \| h) (readProb_eq_chain), and the induced tensor inducedTensor h ps = sum_a p_X(w_a \| h) D^{a_1} (x) ... (x) D^{a_n} (eq:supp-comb-tomography) with inducedTensor_pairing (Tr(R (F^{b_1} (x) ... (x) F^{b_n})) = p_X(w_b \| h)), Hermitian, inducedTensor_nil (R^(0) = 1); frameProb_eq_chain: the multitime probability of a tuple of frame probes IS the chain-law probability of its intervention word. The multilinear pairing multitimeProb is additive under coarse graining and affine under randomization (multitimeProb_add/_smul/_convex) and normalized on the empty sequence. FiniteConditionalOperationalRenewalLawInduced G = such data whose INDUCED tensors satisfy, after every reachable history, the finite positivity and causal partial-trace recursion of thm:supp-comb-tomography (comb_posSemidef; comb_causal = normalization on complete instruments / causality under deterministic discard / prefix compatibility). Non-vacuity (non-trivial): QubitExample.qubitPreparationLaw -- a one-port grammar with a qubit output leg, the minimal informationally complete 4-outcome POVM {(I+Z)/6, (I+X)/6, (I+Y)/6, (3I-X-Y-Z)/6} with explicit trace-dual Hermitian basis (povm_duality, povm_posSemidef, qubitFrame_spans), orientation Read outcomes as terminal letters, and the i.i.d. law of measuring \|0><0\| (probabilities 1/3, 1/6, 1/6, 1/3); its induced tensors are rho^{(x)N} (inducedTensor_eq) and satisfy all comb conditions. Renderings disclosed: every exposed port is available at every cut type (the target cut type of an intervention letter may depend on the probe); tuples are newest-slot-first as in CombCarrier. Checklist: (b) the multitime table and tensors are constructed from the law, not stored; (k) non-trivial qubit instance. | — |  |
| `def:supp-future-signature` | definition | Complete predictive future signature | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.wordProb`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureSignature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.FutureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureSetoid`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureEquivalent_iff_futureSignature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteTypedEventGrammar.ConditionalLaw.futureEquivalent_snoc`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TypedEventGrammar.lean) | Typed encoding over FiniteTypedEventGrammar: wordProb is the chain law eq:supp-chain-law; futureSignature h = (p(w\|h))_w indexed by all typed future words starting at the cut type of h (eq:supp-future-signature); FutureEquivalent h h' for histories of the same cut type (eq:supp-future-equivalence) is agreement of all future-word probabilities, and futureEquivalent_iff_futureSignature shows it is equality of signatures; futureSetoid x is ~_X on H_{X,x}. Histories of different cut type live in different fibres and are never identified. Bank: non-admissible words have probability 0 (prob_eq_zero_of_not_admissible), so the bank of all typed words is the admissible bank; on an unbounded-horizon grammar it is all finite admissible continuations, and on a FiniteHorizonTypedEventGrammar (remaining horizon in the cut type, strictly decreasing along letters) every typed word from x is automatically horizon-compatible, so the same bank is the complete horizon-compatible bank. futureEquivalent_snoc additionally proves thm:supp-right-congruence on the positive-probability branch. Reads, when appended to the bank rather than realized as terminal event letters, are handled by TypedPredictiveCategory.TypedOperationalLaw.read_congr. | — |  |

Scoped: T_{X,a} is defined only where p(a\|h) > 0, which is the only branch on which [h] -> [h a] is well defined (thm:supp-right-congruence is stated on the positive-probability branch; on an admissible zero-probability letter, h ~ h' does not constrain the futures of h a and h' a, so the paper's unrestricted T_{X,a} is not well defined in general). The earlier untyped fibrewise encoding (PredictiveSystem.MinimalCarrier, minimalUpdate, minimalBranch) remains valid. | — |  |

Markov contraction: L^q(mu) for every real q >= 1 (heatApply_weightedLq_le), L^1 (heatApply_weightedL1_le) and L^infinity (heatApply_sup_le). The complexification sentence is commentary. | — |  |
| `ass:main-connection-compactness` | assumption | Connection compactness on a common physical cylinder |  | Assumption (no theorem content); encoded as the hypothesis it supplies where it is consumed. The property asserted (a single strictly increasing subsequence along which the interpolated full connections converge in L^2 on every cylinder of a countable compact exhaustion, all in one common trivialization) is the conclusion of HodgeConnection.hodge_connection_exhaustion (thm:main-hodge-connection realizes it from the finite Hodge-temporal budget). Where the assumption is consumed it enters, after extraction, as strong L^2_loc convergence of the connection: DistributionalCurvature.StrongL2LocTendsto (thm:supp-curvature-compactness, thm:supp-interface-curvature) and DistributionalTorsion.L2LocTendsto (field connection_L2 of RenewalPalatiniHandoff.RenewalPalatiniHypotheses, thm:supp-renewal-palatini). The clauses on frame identifications, overlap control and absence of unrecorded gauge transformations are bookkeeping conventions with no separate formal content (one common trivialization is used throughout). | — |  |

(L1) proved (law_lifespan, law_energy_inequality). (L2) now proved in full except the curvature-CONVERGENCE part of the uniformity sentence: whole-sequence convergence for arbitrary B_h and the four-slot jet comparison (supp_law_einstein, law_jet_comparison); NEW (fC2a) the uniformity of the subsidiary, noncollapse, rate-lift/cut-margin and curvature-bound conclusions of thm:main-open-3plus1 on the entire coefficient ball (supp_law_einstein_uniform: one constant C for all marks b <= 1/48; subsidiary <= C h eps, curvature <= C eps, interior-cone rate lift, cut margin I^sp >= 1/4096 for odd N, noncollapse), plus the jet comparison accJet(q_B) - interpJet(q_0) = O(b h eps) in H^{s-3} from which the curvature difference is O(b h eps) by pjet_moser_fin. (L3): probabilistic part, cost control and Einstein conclusion on the complementary event proved (supp_law_row_einstein, supp_law_row_einstein_ae); the sentence 'the construction in thm:supp-open-finite-words supplies the strict residual margin' rests on that open record. (L4) (analytic zero-set chart of the exact four-row constraint map C_h of eq:main-action-initial-constraints, codimension 4N^3, right inverse eq:main-action-prepared-inverse, cofinal branch) is not formalized: the map C_h is built from the original-action canonical Hamiltonian H_h after stationary connection and Legendre elimination, which the manuscript does not write down explicitly (HARD5 item 1: it may not be taken from private material), so it cannot be faithfully encoded from the manuscript alone. (fD3/fD3b): (L2) is now COMPLETE including the curvature-convergence wording: law_family_curvature (OpenWriterFiniteWordsCurvature.lean) gives, for every sequence of marks B_n of the closed ball (no convergence), metric convergence of the law histories to the vacuum development g of supp_open_einstein and \|\|Riem(g_{B_n,h}) - Riem(g)\|\|_{H^{s-3}} <= C h eps with ONE constant C for all marks (Lipschitz Moser estimate for riemOf on the four-slot jet comparison of supp_law_einstein + the Riemann rate of supp_open_einstein); together with supp_law_einstein_uniform this gives all metric, subsidiary, noncollapse and curvature conclusions of thm:main-open-3plus1 uniformly on the ball. (L1) proved. This H_h is never written down at a general cutoff (nor at N = 3: the exact-action appendix only states hypotheses on 'the same original Hamiltonian H_h'); the paper displays only its flat linearization (eq:supp-initial-linear-map, Fierz-Pauli rows) and its quadratic part on the balancing seed (H^[2] in the proof of lem:supp-initial-balance), and asserts that the link remainder starts at cubic order. It is not stated to be the standard ADM Hamiltonian/momentum constraint in a fixed gauge (the proof refers to the stationary connection, Legendre elimination and a link remainder of the reconstructed connection action), so C_h cannot be encoded faithfully from the manuscript. What is proved: the linear part L_h (Lh, delta, kap; InitialConstraintLinearRange.lean) and the abstract Taylor step of the proof, in any real normed spaces with explicit constants: if Phi(0) = 0 and \|\|DPhi(x) - DPhi(0)\|\| <= K\|\|x\|\| on B(0, rho), then N = Phi - DPhi(0) has DN(x) = DPhi(x) - DPhi(0), \|\|DN(x)\|\| <= K\|\|x\|\| and \|\|N(x)\|\| <= K\|\|x\|\|^2 (taylor_remainder_bounds; also norm_le_sq_of_fderiv_bound and the Lipschitz form norm_sub_le_of_fderiv_bound: \|\|N x - N y\|\| <= C s \|\|x - y\|\| for \|\|x\|\|,\|\|y\|\| <= s), i.e. eq:supp-initial-nonlinear-bounds follows from a cutoff-uniform Lipschitz bound on DC_h. Not proved: analyticity of C_h, DC_h(0) = L_h, and the uniform Lipschitz bound of DC_h on H_h^r-type norms, all of which are statements about the unwritten H_h. | hard | Needs the explicit canonical Hamiltonian H_h(gamma, pi, N, beta) of the reconstructed phase-compatible action at a general odd cutoff (stationary connection and Legendre elimination written out), which the manuscript does not give; ask the author for it, or reformulate the lemma as a hypothesis on C_h (its conclusions are exactly the hypotheses hN/hDN/hN0 of LyapunovSchmidt.RangeData, so the downstream abstract theorems apply verbatim). Once H_h is explicit: DC_h(0) = Lh by direct differentiation, and the uniform bound \|\|DC_h(X) - DC_h(0)\|\| <= K\|\|X\|\| from the Moser/product estimates (DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicGridMoserComposition.lean, sobSq_mul_le), then taylor_remainder_bounds. |

(3) Bridge to ExactSlowRankTwo.HarmonicAnnihilation (D c_H(Xi(q))[J_H c] = 0 on the elimination graph): identify c_H with the rescaled constant-shift row p_H Phi_1 of lem:supp-exact-slow-expansion along the graded chart; this identification is informal in the paper and not formalised. | `thm:supp-exact-direct-transverse` | theorem | Actual direct transverse geometric suppression | hard | Requires the pseudoinverse factorization T^dagger, the operator bounds 9/16 <= H_h <= 324 in weighted Sobolev norms H_h^{s+3}, a geometric observation operator whose symbol supplies h^2, and exponential off-diagonal decay of H_h^{-1} in cyclic Fourier distance. These are h-uniform multiplier estimates on the grid with undefined operators; research-level and dependent on the exact action. |
| `thm:supp-exact-scalar-flux` | theorem | Exact positive scalar energy | hard | The identity S_h = (1/2) D_h^* D_h + (h^2/2) sigma_h^* K_Q^{-1} sigma_h and the K_Q bounds 1/324..16/9 are finite congruence/compression statements, but S_h, sigma_h, Q_h, K_{Q,h} are derived from the exact action after transverse elimination and are not defined; the divergence claim on the soft subspace is a compactness argument. Without the exact-action pipeline this cannot be stated faithfully; a scoped abstract version (compression of a positive metric with uniformly invertible coefficient map has bounds inherited from that map) would be medium. |
| `thm:supp-exact-initial-layer` | theorem | Initial-layer obstruction on an exactly prepared branch | hard | Requires the three-site exactly prepared family, the weak Poisson matrix K_1, the tangency functional Delta_nu, and a time-rescaling t = a^3 s limit of an analytic finite vector field with a contradiction argument against a fixed-time linear curvature bound. The rescaling limit is finite-dimensional ODE analysis (Mathlib has ODE existence/Gronwall but not parameter-dependent limits), and every object depends on the unformalized exact action. Research-level. |
| `thm:supp-exact-upstream-deformation` | theorem | The fixed-first-jet tangency obstruction is deformable | hard | With the action encoded over Action/ExactThreeSiteGrid.lean (delta_i = 3(S_i - S_i^{-1}) is exact over Q) and the base data (Y, l), the claim is one exact rational evaluation: solve D L(Y,l)[Z_2, m_2] = 0 for m_2 (exact linear algebra), check the first variation of the Poisson-null condition of nu_diamond, and evaluate the polarised quadratic functional by norm_num/decide on rationals (no native_decide). If Y, l involve sqrt 3 (omega_h = 3 sqrt 3), work in Q(sqrt 3) or scale it out. |
| `thm:supp-certified-rapid-spectrum` | theorem | Certified weak spectral trichotomy | hard | Needs (1) the explicit 29×29 rational matrix B_W and its reduction A_g at the certified event of the three-site regulator (from the exact finite action, not in Lean), (2) a rigorous eigenvalue certification (simple eigenvalues, count per half-plane, gap 0.0015069) without native_decide — Gershgorin/Rouché-type enclosures or a certified block-diagonalization by an explicit rational similarity checked by norm_num, and (3) the semigroup bounds on the invariant splitting (Mathlib has NormedSpace.exp but no exponential-dichotomy estimates from spectral data). Infrastructure-level. |
| `lem:supp-exact-balanced-field` | lemma | Balanced derivative and reference residual estimates | hard | Estimates on the balanced vector field derived from the graded chart ass:supp-graded-exact-chart; the field H_a of eq:supp-exact-reduced-field is defined through Theta_a, F = J grad H_h and the multiplier rate r of the original Hamiltonian, which the manuscript does not write explicitly (HARD5 item 1: not to be taken from private material). An abstract version would encode (C3) as an unbalanced field V(a,v) = (a^3 b_s + R_s, C_red v_s + B v_f + a^3 b_w + R_f) with the graded remainder bounds and the transport identity, define H_a(w) = diag(a^-1 I, I) V(a, diag(aI, I) w), and prove the first-derivative and residual estimates of eq:supp-exact-balanced-estimates directly; the j = 2..4 derivative bounds and the a^2 factor of the canonical row additionally need the analytic divisibility of (C2) (R_s = a * analytic, Cauchy estimates) and the normal-coordinate derivative orders of lem:supp-exact-normal-coordinates, so the statement as written (about the actual H_a) still inherits the chart gap. |
| `thm:supp-exact-boundary` | theorem | Exact constrained boundary history | hard | Requires the sweep map eq:supp-exact-nonlinear-sweeps (a boundary-value iteration for the slow field H_a on an interval of length S ≤ c/a with projections Π_± onto the stable/unstable splitting of thm:supp-certified-rapid-spectrum), a Banach fixed point in C^0 with the a²/a³ bounds, and the reconstruction identity for Θ_a(w_*(t/a³)) solving the original normalized action equations; needs ODE boundary-value theory on invariant splittings and the exact-action chart — infrastructure Mathlib lacks. |
| `thm:main-exact-boundary` | theorem | Exact-action boundary realization on a graded chart | hard | Existence of an exact history of the unchanged N = 3 finite action selected by a two-point boundary contraction on the graded chart; requires the finite action, the graded chart (ass:supp-graded-exact-chart), a hyperbolic-splitting Green operator and a contraction argument on a time interval of length c a^2, then readout estimates for Riemann and link curvature. Nothing of this is defined in Lean; the abstract contraction layer could build on Action/CriticalShadowingExact.lean but the objects are the hard part. Research-level. |
| `prop:supp-exact-sweep-accuracy` | proposition | Accuracy of the constrained nonlinear iterates | hard | Geometric convergence of the sweep iterates with the jet table eq:supp-exact-sweep-jets and the propagated physical/readout errors through the nonlinear reconstruction Θ_a and the curvature readouts; requires the contraction of thm:supp-exact-boundary plus C² dependence of the reconstruction — all on the missing exact-action chart. |
| `thm:supp-exact-initial-gate` | theorem | Initial acceleration-defect identity | hard | Requires the five-row initial equation of the slow chart (rows H and g), the expansion of exact histories in a through second order at the initial cut, and the identification of the g-rows with the scaled limit of the acceleration defect; a limit identity for an undefined analytic family. Encode 𝓛_0, f_0, 𝒦_0, p_g as data and the expansion as hypotheses; even then the derivation of eq:supp-exact-initial-acceleration-defect needs second-order differentiation of the composed source in a — medium after encoding, hard overall. |
| `cor:supp-exact-initial-repair` | corollary | Sufficient acceleration certificate | hard | Corollary of thm:supp-exact-initial-gate (unformalized, hard): if the limit in eq:supp-exact-initial-acceleration-defect is o(a^{-1}) times a, the two-row defect vanishes. The limit algebra is trivial once the gate identity exists as a Lean statement; inherits that record's gap (exact histories, composed source h, slow-time derivatives of the finite action). |
| `thm:supp-exact-reduced-leading-system` | theorem | Reduced three-constraint leading system | hard | An implicit-function reduction of the leading differential-algebraic system eq:supp-exact-leading-dae to coordinates (r,y); needs the leading DAE of the exact action (undefined) and an implicit-function chart (Mathlib's ImplicitFunctionData for C^1 maps could serve). Research-level in the present state of the library. |
| `thm:supp-exact-slope-gate` | theorem | Necessary quadratic slope gate | hard | Asymptotics of Lipschitz solutions of the reduced system eq:supp-exact-reduced-dae near a degenerate initial point (Taylor expansion of r, quadratic constraint on the slope of y); depends on thm:supp-exact-reduced-leading-system and the structure (F_y(0,0) = H_y(0,0) = 0, quadratic form Q) of the reduced maps, none defined in Lean. Research-level. |
